[HTML guidance](https://info.arxiv.org/about/accessible_HTML.html) informed
request coordination, pinned-version identity and conversion/access limits. The
MIT `arxiv.py` client README/license and delay implementation were inspected as
a comparison, not adopted code or a dependency. The original implementation
uses only standard-library modules and never copies paper text into this skill.

The declared claim inventory is an original integrity/coverage aid. Its explicit
partial/contradictory/uncertain judgments and family/status declarations require
actual evidence review. Valid records cannot authenticate their reviewers, prove
truth or guarantee that every consequential claim and contrary source was found.

<a id="resource-references-testing-md"></a>

## Included resource: references/testing.md

# Check this installation

Use the device's actual selected Python 3.10+ interpreter to run the installed
`scripts/self_check.py`. The check creates and removes its own temporary files;
it makes no network requests, model downloads, remote connections or GPU probes.

```sh
python PATH_TO_SKILL/scripts/self_check.py
```

It checks required resources and internal links, runs the packaged bibliography,
project, device-inventory, FASTA and evidence commands, preserves edits/outputs,
accepts a valid zero and rejects the same number with a wrong unit. It also loads
the literature tool without network access and checks a fictional source-support
record, rejecting an invented passage while preserving the semantic-review limit. Its JSON
report distinguishes passed checks from the optional molecular checks skipped
by default. This verifies installation and controls, not writing quality or truth.

To require RDKit and OpenMM controls in an already compatible environment:

```sh
python PATH_TO_SKILL/scripts/self_check.py --molecular
```

This executes ethanol descriptors, a seeded converged 3D conformer and an
analytic OpenMM Reference energy, checking chemistry and artifact hashes. Missing
dependencies or failed controls produce a nonzero exit and `passed: false`;
requesting molecular checks never silently skips them. There is no requirement
for identical conformer bytes across platforms/builds. GPU/backend performance,
heavy engines, remote execution and biological predictions need separate tests.

Run this check after installing/updating, changing Python/dependencies or moving
devices. Read the actual error before continuing. Never treat dependency presence,
a successful smoke or an unexecuted CI matrix as scientific validation.

## Require selected engines

Supply `--obabel PATH` to require independently checked chiral/E/Z conversion.
The current interpreter needs RDKit; Open Babel can live in a separate runtime.
For protein representations, supply both `--protein-python PATH` and
`--protein-model-dir PATH` to require local CPU inference in that separate
interpreter. No model preparation happens in this self-check. Missing runtime or
assets fails rather than silently skipping. Default runs mark these optional
checks skipped. Successful installation controls are not scientific benchmarks.

The default installed check also loads the remote client without connecting or
transferring anything. Remote host/job acceptance is separate and requires an
explicitly selected profile and bounded test; local package success is not proof
that a remote host or GPU is configured.

## Evaluate research and writing separately

The repository's [workflow evaluation](https://github.com/HarryBurd33/research-writer/tree/main/evals/workflow)
provides supplied-source cases for intake, study-family decisions, quantitative
synthesis or refusal, source conflict, theory, tutorials, close reading, proposals
and contribution duplication. A bounded primary-paper case uses original
paraphrased notes; receiving those notes is not personal full-text access.

For a new improvement, freeze fresh cases and review criteria before changing
instructions. Run the prior and candidate copies in separate contexts with
comparable tools and settings; retain actual outputs, calculations and saved-file
readbacks. Have a reviewer separate from construction inspect both usefulness
and claim support, and disclose agent-based or nonblind review. Fabrication,
violated source restrictions and incorrect destinations cannot be averaged away.
Public cases become known regressions after release; a future held-out test needs
new material. Deterministic fixture arithmetic and package checks do not execute
a writing agent, establish semantic truth or replace domain review.


## Deeper discovery and claim-inventory controls

The installed self-check loads the new discovery command without contacting a
provider, executes a fictional declared-claim audit and requires rejection of
a missing review. It keeps record integrity separate from semantic judgment
and drafting clearance. Actual OpenAlex/arXiv access, conversion quality and
content review need separate bounded live acceptance. The repository also has
public offline provider/parser and inventory regressions for use in CI.


The v0.11 Linux client and supervisor load with the standard library and no Docker
probe during the offline self-check. Actual Linux resource enforcement requires
its separately requested disposable-engine integration suite; an offline pass is
not that evidence. See [Linux isolation](#resource-references-linux-isolation-md).


Add `--checkpoints` to the installed self-check to require actual local SQLite
save, idempotent retry, backup, verification and zero-byte restore controls. It
needs the interpreter's SQLite module; ordinary research and the default core
check do not probe a container engine. See [checkpoint guidance](#resource-references-durable-checkpoints-md).
Process-crash tests are separate from physical power-loss or remote-storage
claims. A valid checkpoint is not an execution or scientific-consistency grant.

Optional backup contracts use `evals/backup_checks.py` in the repository and actual disposable transport uses `evals/backup_loopback_checks.py --run-ssh` on POSIX. See [backup usage](#resource-references-off-device-backups-md). A package self-check has no SSH connections; it cannot certify a second device.

```json
      "data_service_terms": "CrossDocked/DUD-E and other training/benchmark datasets or model repositories have separate unverified terms; remote GPU/compute costs need a budget.",
      "import_name": null,
      "distribution": null,
      "cli_name": "gnina",
      "prerequisites": [
        "User-supplied compatible Linux/WSL binary/environment; native macOS execution not established.",
        "Prepared receptor PDB, ligand SDF, binding-site/autobox definition, model identity and explicit bounded search settings.",
        "Current source build requires CUDA>=12 even though runtime --no_gpu exists.",
        "Prebuilt binary alone can be about 2 GB; check full download/build/runtime/output budgets."
      ],
      "inputs": "Receptor PDB, ligand SDF and box coordinates/sizes or reference ligand; optional scoring/minimization/search configuration.",
      "outputs": "Ranked ligand poses, empirical/CNN score fields and logs.",
      "example_argv": null,
      "limitations": [
        "No general one-input command template: receptor, ligand and search-site mapping require an explicit task plan.",
        "--no_gpu is runtime CPU selection, not proof of CUDA-free source build or Apple GPU support.",
        "CNNscore, CNNaffinity and empirical energy are distinct; do not rename them one confidence/affinity metric.",
        "Docking scores/poses are computational hypotheses; protonation, waters/metals, stereochemistry, controls and physical validity matter."
      ],
      "evidence_urls": [
        "https://github.com/gnina/gnina/blob/01cae6aa6e014e0bf11e511f1fe7a61b17975855/README.md",
        "https://github.com/gnina/gnina/blob/01cae6aa6e014e0bf11e511f1fe7a61b17975855/LICENSE.APACHE",
        "https://github.com/gnina/gnina/blob/01cae6aa6e014e0bf11e511f1fe7a61b17975855/LICENSE.GNU",
        "https://github.com/gnina/gnina/blob/01cae6aa6e014e0bf11e511f1fe7a61b17975855/CMakeLists.txt",
        "https://github.com/gnina/gnina/blob/01cae6aa6e014e0bf11e511f1fe7a61b17975855/gninasrc/lib/models/README.md"
      ],
      "status": "source-audited"
    }
  ]
}
```

<a id="resource-references-tool-usage-md"></a>

## Included resource: references/tool-usage.md

# Original tool commands

These helpers are installed with the complete skill folder. Run them with Python 3.10+ from that folder, replacing `PROJECT` with the user-selected project directory. Store reports and profiles outside the installed skill. Inspect `--help` before a new operation. Reports preserve existing files unless `--force` is explicit. No command installs packages, downloads weights, invokes an LLM or launches a remote engine.

## Environment and optional dependencies

The standard-library tools need no API key. Molecular commands require a compatible, separately installed RDKit/OpenMM environment. Use that environment's Python, not merely a profile pointing at it. Keep conflicting heavy engines in separate environments. An optional user-owned `~/.local/share/research-writer/runtime.json` can record this device's interpreter and paths; a project-specific profile overrides that convenience. Never bundle this private file or treat it as permission.

For a fresh, user-authorized CPU environment, create a virtual environment in a chosen user directory, then install `rdkit` and `openmm` from their documented package sources. Record the actual resolved versions. `pip install` is a setup action, not performed by these helpers. Python/package wheels and GPU backends vary by device; consult the catalog and run the same small controls before using a different environment.

```sh
python scripts/research_tools.py profile --output PROJECT/device.json
python scripts/research_tools.py doctor --profile PROJECT/device.json --output PROJECT/inventory.json
python scripts/research_tools.py plan --tool boltz --input PROJECT/complex.yaml --profile PROJECT/device.json --output PROJECT/boltz-plan.json
```

The plan's command is an upstream example with unresolved placeholders, not an executable job. It does not validate the input using the engine. For API-based tools or multi-input operations without a generic CLI, the command is null and the catalog's interfaces/prerequisites govern preparation. Remote profiles are unprobed; authorized remote inspection and execution occur separately. `--probe-gpu` adds a bounded local NVIDIA inventory when available; it does not test all accelerators or models. See [device profiles](#resource-references-device-profiles-md).

## Source records

```sh
python scripts/evidence_tools.py init-project --directory PROJECT
python scripts/evidence_tools.py bibliography --input PROJECT/references-input.json --output PROJECT/references-normalized.json
python scripts/evidence_tools.py search --query "evidence synthesis reporting" --limit 5 --output PROJECT/search.json
python scripts/evidence_tools.py lookup-doi --doi 10.1371/journal.pcbi.1003149 --output PROJECT/doi.json
```

Bibliography input is a list, for example `[{"doi":"https://doi.org/10.1371/journal.pcbi.1003149","title":"Supplied title"}]`. Original records and identifier/version variants remain available after deduplication. Conflicting metadata fails for review rather than silently overwriting it. Search and lookup are the only network commands: public query/DOI goes to Crossref, results are metadata, and transport failure is not scholarly absence. Full-text claim verification still requires reading.

## Local molecular utilities

```sh
python scripts/research_tools.py fasta --input PROJECT/sequences.fasta --output PROJECT/sequence-check.json
python scripts/research_tools.py rdkit-descriptors --smiles CCO --output PROJECT/ethanol.json
python scripts/research_tools.py rdkit-conformer --smiles CCO --seed 481516 --sdf PROJECT/ethanol.sdf --output PROJECT/geometry.json
python scripts/research_tools.py openmm-smoke --output PROJECT/energy-check.json
```

FASTA inspection reports identity, lengths and nonstandard symbols; it is not a chemistry or folding validation. Descriptors include molecular weight, exact mass, logP, heavy atoms, donors, acceptors and formal charge. Generated coordinates use ETKDGv3/MMFF with seed and convergence status; a nonconverged optimization exits unsuccessfully and remains visible. The OpenMM command uses two artificial particles on Reference with analytic expected energy 0.5 kJ/mol. It is an installation/unit test, not a biological MD study.

Descriptor and energy commands also write a context-labelled `.measurements.csv` beside the JSON report, with its SHA-256. Conformer reports link the SDF hash. Neither output demonstrates biological function, measured binding or a unique physical conformation.

## Integrity manifest

`verify-evidence` takes this shape; use an actual artifact hash and exact CSV data, not the placeholder below:

```json
{
  "artifacts": [{"id":"a1","path":"ethanol.measurements.csv","sha256":"ACTUAL_64_HEX_DIGEST"}],
  "measurements": [{"id":"m1","artifact_id":"a1","row":1,"run":"rdkit-descriptors","condition":"CCO","dataset":"explicit_local_input","metric":"MolWt","unit":"g/mol","seed":"not_applicable","statistic":"single_deterministic_calculation","value":"46.069"}],
  "claims": [{"id":"c1","evidence_id":"m1","run":"rdkit-descriptors","condition":"CCO","dataset":"explicit_local_input","metric":"MolWt","unit":"g/mol","seed":"not_applicable","statistic":"single_deterministic_calculation","value":"46.069"}],
  "sources": [],
  "source_claims": []
}
```

```sh
python scripts/evidence_tools.py verify-evidence --input PROJECT/evidence.json --output PROJECT/integrity.json
```

Use the actual CSV row: rows start at 1 after its header, and all context fields must agree. Exact comparison deliberately performs no hidden rounding, unit conversion or tolerance matching; explicitly document derived/rounded quantities separately. A claim citing a different run or unit fails even if its number appears elsewhere. Empty manifests are not checked. Source access declarations cannot turn metadata into full text; recorded access and locators remain assertions needing inspection. A passing integrity report checks traceability and declared consistency, not source entailment, experimental truth or scientific usefulness.

### Verified backup transport

Use `scripts/backup_tools.py` only for an explicitly selected authorized SSH backup destination. Its separate `init-remote`, `prepare`, `push`, `verify` and `fetch` operations preserve uncertain outcomes and immutable identity. See [the full contract](#resource-references-off-device-backups-md). It transfers a consistent whole SQLite store with an explicit 1–64 MiB cap; it does not execute saved application notes.

<a id="resource-templates-research-record-md"></a>

## Included resource: templates/research-record.md

# Research record

Use only fields needed for this task. Store this filled record outside the skill package.

## Brief

- Intended finished work and completion criteria:
- Question or desired function:
- Actual readers, prior knowledge, use of the work, paper genre and length:
- Delivery destination, format and exact existing document or new-document choice:
- Journal type/readership and named venue if chosen, or no-journal/provisional status; requirements actually checked:
- Scope, source/data access and effort constraints:
- Confirmed choices / disclosed assumptions / unresolved decisions:
- Requested source boundaries and confidentiality:

## Premise and method

- Terms needing clarification:
- Desired outcome vs proposed mechanism:
- Established evidence / transfer by analogy / speculative dependencies:
- Inquiry type and whether a prediction is appropriate:
- Selected writing route(s), preferred-method alternative and feasibility gates:
- Search plan, selection rules and stopping rule:
- Changes from the original plan and reasons:

## Question and contribution development

- Territory: conversation, audience, established findings/interpretations and source IDs:
- Candidate niches: unresolved issue, significance, supporting/contrary source IDs:
- Options compared and user's preferences:
- Encompassing main question, definitions/scope and connected subquestions:
- Intended contribution and evidence/method alignment:
- Provisional answer/hypothesis, distinguished from the question and eventual conclusion:
- Question revisions and evidence that prompted them:

## Closest prior work and novelty checks

| Proposed contribution | Nearest precedent and locator | Actual overlap/difference and why it matters | Search/access limits and status |
|---|---|---|---|

Use status such as candidate, supported within inspected literature, already addressed or unresolved. Do not infer worldwide novelty from missing search results.

## Search log

| Date | Source/index | Exact query and filters | Actual coverage or records | Selection/deviation notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|

## Prewriting checkpoint

- Draft/revision stage and audience/genre/venue choices settled or still needed:
- Last actual search date and original scope/coverage/access:
- Adequate / targeted renewal / rebuild decision and reason:
- Actual renewal queries/actions/date and sources inspected, or source-bound/tool limits:
- Changes to question, nearest neighbor, gap, outline or claims after reconciliation:
- Closest published neighbor, inspected comparison and one-sentence prior → proposed increment:
- Duplication test and decision: revise / justified replication / stop / proceed within stated scope:
- Gap hypothesis: significance, venue/readership value, uncertain publishability and feasibility/resources:
- Disconfirming evidence sought, weakest assumption and next check:
- Ready within scope / material blocker; unresolved currency or novelty claims:

## Source and claim ledger

| Source ID and verified citation/URL | Version/status and access level | Faithful source claim and locator | Role, limits and contradictions | Project inference or question implication |
|---|---|---|---|---|

## Declared claim inventory, when needed

| Claim ID and exact claim | Required source IDs/access | Reviewed passages and source hashes/versions | Recorded support/partial/contradiction/uncertainty | Family/status flags, revision or next check |
|---|---|---|---|---|

- Actual source/family/status evidence and scope of the declared inventory:
- Missing reviews, contrary versions and reasons for inventory amendments:
- Record-integrity/coverage report location, distinct from semantic review:
- Separate reviewer role, actual review limits and unresolved drafting decisions:

## Argument and work state

- Thesis or hypothesis:
- Major claims and supporting source IDs:
- Strongest contrary evidence or rival interpretation:
- Proposed methods and tests, distinguished from work performed:
- Extraction, calculation inputs/code/versions/outputs when applicable:
- Draft/output locations:
- Completed checks / unresolved claims / limitations:
- Last completed action and next action:

## Publication preparation, when requested

- Artifact stage, article type, intended readership and venue/requirements actually inspected:
- Title/abstract/keywords alignment with supported contribution:

| Aim/question | Evidence and method | Actual result or argument | Interpretation and limit | Gap and next action |
|---|---|---|---|---|

| Preparation fact | Provided / missing / not applicable (reason) | Evidence or person who must resolve it |
|---|---|---|

Use only needed fields: authors/order/contributions, correspondence and author approval; funding and competing interests; data/material/code availability; applicable ethics/consent and AI-use disclosures; venue-required cover material. Unknown is not none. No journal submission or editor contact is implied.


## Optional computational work

- Device/profile location (outside skill), observed probe scope/date and environment:
- Tool/model/release revisions; code, weights, data/output/service terms separately:
- Representation/protonation/stereochemistry/modified residue/cofactor assumptions:
- Hypothesis, comparators, dataset/splits/leakage checks, seeds and metrics/units:
- Time/trial/storage/download/network/spending budget and stop rule:
- Every attempt, status, protocol amendments and selection rationale (including failures/zeros):
- Actual argv, working directory, versions, input/output artifact paths and hashes:
- Context-keyed measurements, claim links and integrity-report path:
- Capability state: audited / dependency-present / smoke-tested / task-specific validation:
- Computational interpretation limits, controls and independent evidence still needed:

<a id="resource-scripts-backup-tools-py"></a>

## Included resource: scripts/backup_tools.py

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
"""Explicit immutable SQLite backups over authenticated SSH; no execution clearance."""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import base64
from contextlib import contextmanager
import getpass
import hashlib
import importlib.util
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import signal
import socket
import stat
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time
import uuid

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
if 'C' not in globals():
    _spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('rw_backup_checkpoint', ROOT / 'checkpoint_tools.py')
    C = importlib.util.module_from_spec(_spec); _spec.loader.exec_module(C)
if __name__ != 'backup_receiver':
    _spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('rw_backup_remote', ROOT / 'remote_tools.py')
    R = importlib.util.module_from_spec(_spec); _spec.loader.exec_module(R)

MAX_BACKUP = 64 * 1024**2
MAX_WIRE = 96 * 1024**2
MAX_SMALL = 1024**2
BOOTSTRAP = ("import sys,json,types; raw=sys.stdin.buffer.read(100663297); "
             "assert len(raw)<=100663296,'Backup envelope exceeds bound'; "
             "e=json.loads(raw,object_pairs_hook=lambda p: dict(p) if len(dict(p))==len(p) else (_ for _ in ()).throw(ValueError('Duplicate JSON key')),parse_constant=lambda _: (_ for _ in ()).throw(ValueError('Nonfinite JSON'))); "
             "c=types.ModuleType('checkpoint_tools'); c.__file__='<checkpoint_tools.py>'; "
             "exec(compile(e['checkpoint_source'],c.__file__,'exec'),c.__dict__); "
             "g={'__name__':'backup_receiver','__file__':'<backup_tools.py>','C':c}; "
             "exec(compile(e['backup_source'],g['__file__'],'exec'),g); "
             "g['receiver_main'](e['request'])")


def canonical(value):
    return C.canonical(value)


def decode(raw, maximum=MAX_SMALL):
    if len(raw) > maximum:
        raise ValueError('Backup JSON exceeds byte bound')
    return json.loads(raw, object_pairs_hook=C.pairs,
                      parse_constant=lambda _: (_ for _ in ()).throw(ValueError('Nonfinite JSON')))


def cap(value):
    if type(value) is not int or not 1024**2 <= value <= MAX_BACKUP or value % 1024**2:
        raise ValueError('Explicit backup cap must be whole MiB in 1..64MiB')
    return value


def profile(value):
    if not isinstance(value, dict): raise ValueError('Backup profile must be an object')
    extras = {'vault_id', 'max_backup_bytes', 'rpc_timeout_seconds'}
    if set(value) != R.PROFILE_FIELDS | extras:
        raise ValueError('Exact backup profile fields required; no credentials')
    p = R.profile({k: v for k, v in value.items() if k not in extras})
    for name in ('expected_hostname', 'expected_user'):
        R.text(p[name], name)
    C.identifier(value['vault_id']); cap(value['max_backup_bytes'])
    if type(value['rpc_timeout_seconds']) is not int or not 5 <= value['rpc_timeout_seconds'] <= 300:
        raise ValueError('RPC timeout must be explicit 5..300 seconds')
    p.update({k: value[k] for k in extras})
    return p


def private_directory(path):
    path = C.outside_skill(path)
    if not path.is_dir(): raise ValueError('Existing private directory required')
    st = path.stat()
    if os.name == 'posix' and (st.st_uid != os.getuid() or st.st_mode & 0o077):
        raise ValueError('Directory must be current-user private')
    return path


def write_new(path, raw):
    fd = os.open(path, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | getattr(os, 'O_NOFOLLOW', 0), 0o600)
    try:
        with os.fdopen(fd, 'wb', closefd=False) as stream:
            count = stream.write(raw)
            if count != len(raw): raise OSError('Short backup write')
            stream.flush(); os.fsync(fd)
    finally: os.close(fd)


def fsync_file(path):
    fd = os.open(path, os.O_RDONLY | getattr(os, 'O_NOFOLLOW', 0))
    try: os.fsync(fd)
    finally: os.close(fd)


def publish_report(path, raw):
    """A failed flush/publication must not leave a new success report behind."""
    path = C.outside_skill(path); private_directory(path.parent)
    fd, name = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix='.rw-backup-report-', dir=path.parent)
    os.close(fd); stage = Path(name); stage.unlink(); published = False
    try:
        write_new(stage, raw)
        identity = stage.stat()
        C.rename_new(stage, path); published = True
        C.fsync_directory(path.parent)
    except Exception:
        if published:
            try:
                current = path.lstat()
                if (current.st_dev, current.st_ino) == (identity.st_dev, identity.st_ino): path.unlink()
            except OSError: pass
        raise
    finally:
        if stage.exists(): stage.unlink()


def descriptor(value):
    if not isinstance(value, dict) or set(value) != {'schema_version','backup_id','size','sha256','logical_sha256'}:
        raise ValueError('Exact backup descriptor fields required')
    if type(value['schema_version']) is not int or value['schema_version'] != 1:
        raise ValueError('Unknown backup descriptor version')
    C.identifier(value['backup_id']); C.sha(value['sha256']); C.sha(value['logical_sha256'])
    if type(value['size']) is not int or not 1 <= value['size'] <= MAX_BACKUP:
        raise ValueError('Backup size outside absolute bound')
    return value


def drill_store(store):
    """Verify every generation, restore each separately, re-read all payload bytes."""
    result = C.verify_store(store)
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='rw-backup-drill-') as directory:
        work = Path(directory).resolve()
        for item in result['checkpoints']:
            target = work / item['checkpoint_id']
            C.restore(store, item['checkpoint_id'], target, True)
            receipt = C.read_json(target / '.checkpoint.json', C.MAX_RPC)
            if receipt != item:
                raise ValueError('Restore metadata does not match checkpoint')
            for entry in item['files']:
                raw = C.regular(target / entry['path'], C.MAX_FILE)
                if len(raw) != entry['size'] or C.digest(raw) != entry['sha256']:
                    raise ValueError('Restored file mismatch')
    logical = {'policy': result['policy'], 'checkpoints': result['checkpoints']}
    return {'logical_sha256': C.digest(canonical(logical)),
            'store_id': result['policy']['store_id'], 'generations': len(result['checkpoints']),
            'full_restore_drill': True}


def checked_artifact(store, expected, maximum):
    descriptor(expected)
    if expected['size'] > cap(maximum): raise ValueError('Backup exceeds selected cap')
    raw = C.regular(Path(store) / C.DB_NAME, maximum)
    if len(raw) != expected['size'] or C.digest(raw) != expected['sha256']:
        raise ValueError('Backup bytes do not match immutable intent')
    drill = drill_store(store)
    if drill['logical_sha256'] != expected['logical_sha256']:
        raise ValueError('Backup logical inventory mismatch')
    if C.regular(Path(store) / C.DB_NAME, maximum) != raw:
        raise ValueError('Closed backup changed during verification')
    return raw, drill


def new_parent(destination):
    destination = C.outside_skill(destination)
    if destination.exists(): raise ValueError('Destination must be new; no overwrite')
    private_directory(destination.parent)
    return destination


def prepare(p, store, backup_id, state_dir):
    p = profile(p); C.identifier(backup_id); state_dir = new_parent(state_dir)
    source = C.no_symlinks(store)
    if source == state_dir or source in state_dir.parents or state_dir in source.parents:
        raise ValueError('State must be separate from checkpoint source')
    stage = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix='.rw-backup-prepare-', dir=state_dir.parent))
    try:
        C.backup(source, stage / 'snapshot', True, max_backup_bytes=p['max_backup_bytes'])
        raw = C.regular(stage / 'snapshot' / C.DB_NAME, p['max_backup_bytes'])
        if not raw: raise ValueError('Empty database is not a backup')
        drill = drill_store(stage / 'snapshot')
        d = {'schema_version': 1, 'backup_id': backup_id, 'size': len(raw),
             'sha256': C.digest(raw), 'logical_sha256': drill['logical_sha256']}
        descriptor(d); fsync_file(stage / 'snapshot' / C.DB_NAME)
        intent = {'schema_version': 1, 'profile_sha256': C.digest(canonical(p)), 'descriptor': d}
        write_new(stage / 'intent.json', canonical(intent)); C.fsync_directory(stage)
        C.rename_new(stage, state_dir); C.fsync_directory(state_dir.parent)
        return result('prepared', d, drill)
    finally:
        if stage.exists(): shutil.rmtree(stage)


def load_intent(p, state_dir):
    p = profile(p); root = private_directory(state_dir)
    if set(x.name for x in root.iterdir()) - {'intent.json', 'snapshot', 'attempt.json'}:
        raise ValueError('Unexpected state entry')
    value = decode(C.regular(root / 'intent.json', MAX_SMALL))
    if not isinstance(value, dict) or set(value) != {'schema_version','profile_sha256','descriptor'} or type(value['schema_version']) is not int or value['schema_version'] != 1:
        raise ValueError('Invalid immutable backup intent')
    if value['profile_sha256'] != C.digest(canonical(p)):
        raise ValueError('Profile changed since preparation')
    d = descriptor(value['descriptor'])
    if d['size'] > p['max_backup_bytes']: raise ValueError('Intent exceeds selected cap')
    return root, d


def result(state, d=None, drill=None):
    out = {'state': state, 'execution_clearance': 'not_granted',
           'failure_domain': 'unverified_no_topology_attestation',
           'durability': 'storage_flush_dependent_no_physical_failure_attestation'}
    if d is not None: out['descriptor'] = d
    if drill is not None: out['verification'] = drill
    return out


def request(p, operation, d=None):
    return {'schema_version': 1, 'operation': operation,
            'remote_root': p['remote_root'], 'vault_id': p['vault_id'],
            'expected_hostname': p['expected_hostname'], 'expected_user': p['expected_user'],
            'max_backup_bytes': p['max_backup_bytes'], 'descriptor': d}


def rpc(p, req):
    """One SSH call, bounded request/output/deadline; never automatically retries."""
    import shlex
    sources = {'checkpoint_source': C.regular(ROOT / 'checkpoint_tools.py', MAX_SMALL).decode(),
               'backup_source': C.regular(ROOT / 'backup_tools.py', MAX_SMALL).decode(), 'request': req}
    payload = canonical(sources)
    if len(payload) > MAX_WIRE: raise ValueError('Backup request exceeds wire bound')
    argv = R.ssh_argv(p)
    argv[-1] = shlex.join([p['remote_python'], '-c', BOOTSTRAP])
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='rw-backup-rpc-') as directory:
        folder = Path(directory).resolve()
        inp, out, err = [folder / n for n in ('input', 'output', 'error')]
        write_new(inp, payload)
        with inp.open('rb') as source, out.open('wb') as stdout, err.open('wb') as stderr:
            child = subprocess.Popen(argv, stdin=source, stdout=stdout, stderr=stderr,
                                     shell=False, start_new_session=os.name == 'posix')
            deadline = time.monotonic() + p['rpc_timeout_seconds']
            reason = None
            while child.poll() is None:
                if time.monotonic() > deadline: reason = 'SSH deadline; outcome uncertain'; break
                if out.stat().st_size > MAX_WIRE or err.stat().st_size > MAX_SMALL:
                    reason = 'SSH output limit; outcome uncertain'; break
                time.sleep(.025)
            if reason:
                if os.name == 'posix':
                    try: os.killpg(child.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
                    except ProcessLookupError: pass
                else: child.kill()
                child.wait(timeout=5)
                raise R.TransportError(reason)
            child.wait()
        if child.returncode:
            raise R.TransportError('SSH failed; outcome uncertain: ' + C.regular(err, MAX_SMALL).decode('utf-8', 'replace')[-1000:])
        response = decode(C.regular(out, MAX_WIRE), MAX_WIRE)
    if not isinstance(response, dict) or response.get('schema_version') != 1 or response.get('hostname') != p['expected_hostname'] or response.get('user') != p['expected_user'] or response.get('vault_id') != p['vault_id'] or response.get('remote_root') != p['remote_root']:
        raise R.TransportError('Receiver identity/protocol mismatch; outcome uncertain')
    if response.get('ok') is not True:
        raise ValueError('Receiver rejected: ' + str(response.get('error', 'unknown'))[:1000])
    return response


def init_remote(p, allow_transfer=False):
    p = profile(p)
    if not allow_transfer: raise ValueError('Explicit --allow-transfer required for remote initialization')
    reply = rpc(p, request(p, 'init'))
    return {**result('vault_ready'), 'vault_id': reply['vault_id']}


def push(p, state_dir, allow_transfer=False):
    p = profile(p)
    if not allow_transfer: raise ValueError('Explicit --allow-transfer required')
    root, d = load_intent(p, state_dir)
    if (root / 'attempt.json').exists():
        raise ValueError('Upload already attempted; use read-only verify/fetch, never blind replay')
    raw, _ = checked_artifact(root / 'snapshot', d, p['max_backup_bytes'])
    # Durable one-shot intent precedes all network mutation. A failed marker flush
    # sends nothing; a partial/complete marker blocks blind replay conservatively.
    write_new(root / 'attempt.json', canonical({'schema_version': 1, 'descriptor': d}))
    C.fsync_directory(root)
    q = request(p, 'put', d); q['data_base64'] = base64.b64encode(raw).decode('ascii')
    try:
        rpc(p, q)
        return verify(p, root)
    except Exception as exc:
        raise R.TransportError('Backup outcome uncertain; reconcile with verify/fetch: ' + str(exc)[:1000]) from exc


def received(p, state_dir):
    root, d = load_intent(p, state_dir)
    reply = rpc(p, request(p, 'get', d))
    if reply.get('descriptor') != d: raise ValueError('Readback descriptor mismatch')
    encoded = reply.get('data_base64')
    if not isinstance(encoded, str) or len(encoded) != 4 * ((d['size'] + 2) // 3):
        raise ValueError('Readback encoding length mismatch')
    raw = base64.b64decode(encoded, validate=True)
    if len(raw) != d['size'] or C.digest(raw) != d['sha256']:
        raise ValueError('Readback bytes do not match immutable intent')
    return d, raw


def materialize(raw, d, destination, maximum):
    destination = new_parent(destination)
    stage = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix='.rw-backup-fetch-', dir=destination.parent))
    try:
        write_new(stage / C.DB_NAME, raw)
        _, drill = checked_artifact(stage, d, maximum)
        fsync_file(stage / C.DB_NAME); C.fsync_directory(stage)
        C.rename_new(stage, destination); C.fsync_directory(destination.parent)
        return drill
    finally:
        if stage.exists(): shutil.rmtree(stage)


def verify(p, state_dir):
    p = profile(p); d, raw = received(p, state_dir)
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='rw-backup-readback-') as directory:
        drill = materialize(raw, d, Path(directory).resolve() / 'store', p['max_backup_bytes'])
    return result('verified', d, {**drill, 'fresh_ssh_readback': True})


def fetch(p, state_dir, destination, allow_restore=False):
    p = profile(p)
    if not allow_restore: raise ValueError('Explicit --allow-restore required')
    destination = new_parent(destination)
    root, _ = load_intent(p, state_dir)
    if destination == root or root in destination.parents or destination in root.parents:
        raise ValueError('Fetch destination must be separate from intent state')
    d, raw = received(p, root)
    drill = materialize(raw, d, destination, p['max_backup_bytes'])
    return {**result('fetched', d, {**drill, 'fresh_ssh_readback': True}), 'destination': str(destination)}


@contextmanager
def vault_lock(root):
    import fcntl
    root = private_directory(root)
    fd = os.open(root / '.lock', os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT | os.O_NOFOLLOW, 0o600)
    try:
        a, b = os.fstat(fd), (root / '.lock').lstat()
        if not stat.S_ISREG(a.st_mode) or a.st_nlink != 1 or (a.st_dev, a.st_ino) != (b.st_dev, b.st_ino) or a.st_uid != os.getuid() or a.st_mode & 0o077:
            raise ValueError('Unsafe vault lock')
        fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
        yield root
    finally: os.close(fd)


def vault(root, vault_id):
    root = private_directory(root)
    names = {x.name for x in root.iterdir()}
    if names - {'vault.json', '.lock', 'backups'} - {n for n in names if n.startswith('.stage-') and len(n) == 39}:
        raise ValueError('Unexpected vault entries')
    value = decode(C.regular(root / 'vault.json', MAX_SMALL))
    if value != {'schema_version': 1, 'vault_id': vault_id}:
        raise ValueError('Vault identity mismatch')
    private_directory(root / 'backups')
    return root


def init_vault(root, vault_id):
    root = C.outside_skill(root)
    if root.exists():
        with vault_lock(root): vault(root, vault_id)
        return result('vault_ready')
    root = new_parent(root)
    stage = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix='.rw-vault-init-', dir=root.parent))
    try:
        (stage / 'backups').mkdir(mode=0o700)
        write_new(stage / 'vault.json', canonical({'schema_version': 1, 'vault_id': vault_id}))
        write_new(stage / '.lock', b'0')
        C.fsync_directory(stage / 'backups'); C.fsync_directory(stage)
        C.rename_new(stage, root); C.fsync_directory(root.parent)
        return result('vault_ready')
    finally:
        if stage.exists(): shutil.rmtree(stage)


def read_archive(root, d, maximum):
    target = private_directory(root / 'backups' / d['backup_id'])
    if {x.name for x in target.iterdir()} != {'descriptor.json', 'store'}:
        raise ValueError('Unexpected archive entry')
    if decode(C.regular(target / 'descriptor.json', MAX_SMALL)) != d:
        raise ValueError('Immutable backup ID has different descriptor')
    raw, drill = checked_artifact(target / 'store', d, maximum)
    return raw, drill


def put_archive(root, d, raw, maximum):
    destination = root / 'backups' / d['backup_id']
    if destination.exists():
        old, drill = read_archive(root, d, maximum)
        if old != raw: raise ValueError('Immutable backup ID conflict')
        return result('already_present', d, drill)
    # Recognizable, inert UUID stages survive process death without blocking
    # reads of earlier archives. They are never treated as published backups.
    stage = root / ('.stage-' + uuid.uuid4().hex); stage.mkdir(mode=0o700)
    try:
        (stage / 'store').mkdir(mode=0o700)
        write_new(stage / 'store' / C.DB_NAME, raw)
        _, drill = checked_artifact(stage / 'store', d, maximum)
        write_new(stage / 'descriptor.json', canonical(d))
        fsync_file(stage / 'store' / C.DB_NAME)
        C.fsync_directory(stage / 'store'); C.fsync_directory(stage)
        C.rename_new(stage, destination)
        # A failure here means a published but unconfirmed backup; caller must
        # reconcile by immutable ID. Never remove a published destination.
        C.fsync_directory(root / 'backups'); C.fsync_directory(root)
        return result('stored', d, drill)
    finally:
        if stage.exists(): shutil.rmtree(stage)


def receiver(req):
    if os.name != 'posix': raise ValueError('Backup receiver requires POSIX local filesystem')
    fields = {'schema_version','operation','remote_root','vault_id','expected_hostname','expected_user','max_backup_bytes','descriptor'}
    if not isinstance(req, dict) or set(req) not in (fields, fields | {'data_base64'}):
        raise ValueError('Exact receiver request fields required')
    if type(req['schema_version']) is not int or req['schema_version'] != 1 or req['operation'] not in ('init','put','get'):
        raise ValueError('Unknown receiver protocol')
    if req['expected_hostname'] != socket.gethostname() or req['expected_user'] != getpass.getuser():
        raise ValueError('Receiver host/user mismatch')
    C.identifier(req['vault_id']); maximum = cap(req['max_backup_bytes'])
    root = C.outside_skill(req['remote_root'])
    if not isinstance(req['remote_root'],str) or not Path(req['remote_root']).is_absolute() or len(root.parts) < 3 or '..' in root.parts or str(root) != req['remote_root']:
        raise ValueError('Absolute dedicated vault root required')
    # The receiver runs from uploaded source, so its synthetic __file__ cannot
    # identify another installed skill. Conservatively reject skill ancestors.
    if any((p / 'SKILL.md').is_file() for p in (root, *root.parents)):
        raise ValueError('Remote backup vault must be outside any skill folder')
    if req['operation'] == 'init':
        if req['descriptor'] is not None or 'data_base64' in req: raise ValueError('Invalid init payload')
        return init_vault(root, req['vault_id'])
    d = descriptor(req['descriptor'])
    if d['size'] > maximum: raise ValueError('Backup exceeds selected cap before decoding')
    raw = None
    if req['operation'] == 'put':
        encoded = req.get('data_base64')
        if not isinstance(encoded, str) or len(encoded) != 4 * ((d['size'] + 2) // 3):
            raise ValueError('Backup encoding length mismatch')
        raw = base64.b64decode(encoded, validate=True)
        if len(raw) != d['size'] or C.digest(raw) != d['sha256']:
            raise ValueError('Backup transfer checksum mismatch')
    elif 'data_base64' in req: raise ValueError('Read-only request must contain no upload')
    with vault_lock(root):
        vault(root, req['vault_id'])
        if req['operation'] == 'put': return put_archive(root, d, raw, maximum)
        raw, drill = read_archive(root, d, maximum)
        return {**result('verified', d, drill), 'data_base64': base64.b64encode(raw).decode('ascii')}


def receiver_main(req):
    identity = {'schema_version': 1, 'hostname': socket.gethostname(), 'user': getpass.getuser(),
                'vault_id': req.get('vault_id') if isinstance(req, dict) else None,
                'remote_root': req.get('remote_root') if isinstance(req, dict) else None}
    try: reply = {**identity, 'ok': True, **receiver(req)}
    except Exception as exc:
        reply = {**identity, 'ok': False, 'state': 'failed_or_uncertain', 'error': str(exc)[:1500],
                 'execution_clearance': 'not_granted'}
    raw = canonical(reply)
    if len(raw) > MAX_WIRE: raise ValueError('Receiver response exceeds wire bound')
    sys.stdout.buffer.write(raw); sys.stdout.buffer.flush()


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest='operation', required=True)
    for op in ('init-remote','prepare','push','verify','fetch'):
        cmd = sub.add_parser(op); cmd.add_argument('--profile', required=True); cmd.add_argument('--report', required=True)
        if op != 'init-remote': cmd.add_argument('--state-dir', required=True)
        if op == 'prepare': cmd.add_argument('--store', required=True); cmd.add_argument('--backup-id', required=True)
        if op in ('push','init-remote'): cmd.add_argument('--allow-transfer', action='store_true')
        if op == 'fetch': cmd.add_argument('--destination', required=True); cmd.add_argument('--allow-restore', action='store_true')
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    report = None; report_safe = False
    try:
        report = C.outside_skill(args.report)
        if report.exists(): raise ValueError('Report destination must be new')
        private_directory(report.parent)
        for name in ('store', 'state_dir', 'destination'):
            if getattr(args, name, None) is not None:
                protected = C.no_symlinks(getattr(args, name))
                if report == protected or protected in report.parents:
                    raise ValueError('Report must be separate from checkpoint, intent and restore directories')
        report_safe = True
        p = profile(decode(C.regular(args.profile, MAX_SMALL)))
        if args.operation == 'init-remote': reply = init_remote(p, args.allow_transfer)
        elif args.operation == 'prepare': reply = prepare(p, args.store, args.backup_id, args.state_dir)
        elif args.operation == 'push': reply = push(p, args.state_dir, args.allow_transfer)
        elif args.operation == 'verify': reply = verify(p, args.state_dir)
        else: reply = fetch(p, args.state_dir, args.destination, args.allow_restore)
        publish_report(report, canonical({'schema_version': 1, 'ok': True, **reply}) + b'\n')
        return 0
    except Exception as exc:
        reply = {'schema_version': 1, 'ok': False, 'state': 'failed_or_uncertain',
                 'error': str(exc)[:1500], 'execution_clearance': 'not_granted'}
        try:
            if report_safe and report is not None and not report.exists(): write_new(report, canonical(reply) + b'\n')
        except Exception: pass
        print(json.dumps(reply), file=sys.stderr); return 2


if __name__ == '__main__': raise SystemExit(main())
```

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
"""Bounded local SQLite file/application checkpoints. Saved notes never authorize execution."""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import base64
from contextlib import contextmanager
import ctypes
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath, PureWindowsPath
import re
import shutil
import sqlite3
import stat
import sys
import tempfile
import time
import uuid
import unicodedata

MAX_FILE = 8 * 1024**2
MAX_TOTAL = 16 * 1024**2
MAX_RPC = 32 * 1024**2
MAX_META = 64 * 1024
MAX_DB = 512 * 1024**2
APP_ID = 0x52574350
CONSISTENCY = 'application_declared_not_automatically_verified'
DB_NAME = 'checkpoint.sqlite'
SCHEMA = {
    'policy': 'CREATE TABLE policy(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(id=1),body TEXT NOT NULL,fingerprint TEXT NOT NULL)',
    'snapshots': 'CREATE TABLE snapshots(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,metadata TEXT NOT NULL,fingerprint TEXT NOT NULL,created_at REAL NOT NULL)',
    'files': 'CREATE TABLE files(checkpoint_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES snapshots(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,path TEXT NOT NULL,size INTEGER NOT NULL,sha256 TEXT NOT NULL,data BLOB NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(checkpoint_id,path))'
}
SKILL_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent


def outside_skill(path):
    path = no_symlinks(path)
    if (SKILL_ROOT / 'SKILL.md').is_file() and (path == SKILL_ROOT or SKILL_ROOT in path.parents):
        raise ValueError('Checkpoint storage/publication must be outside installed skill')
    return path


def canonical(v):
    return json.dumps(v, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':'), allow_nan=False).encode('utf-8')


def digest(raw):
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()


def pairs(items):
    result = {}
    for key, value in items:
        if key in result:
            raise ValueError('Duplicate JSON key')
        result[key] = value
    return result


def decode(raw):
    if len(raw) > MAX_RPC:
        raise ValueError('JSON exceeds byte bound')
    return json.loads(raw, object_pairs_hook=pairs,
                      parse_constant=lambda _: (_ for _ in ()).throw(ValueError('Nonfinite JSON')))


def no_symlinks(path):
    if '..' in Path(path).parts:
        raise ValueError('Parent traversal component forbidden')
    path = Path(path).absolute()
    for p in (path, *path.parents):
        if p.is_symlink():
            raise ValueError('Symlink component forbidden')
    return path


def regular(path, maximum):
    path = no_symlinks(path)
    before = path.lstat()
    if not stat.S_ISREG(before.st_mode) or before.st_nlink != 1 or before.st_size > maximum:
        raise ValueError('Bounded regular non-hardlinked file required')
    fd = os.open(path, os.O_RDONLY | getattr(os, 'O_NOFOLLOW', 0) | getattr(os, 'O_NONBLOCK', 0))
    try:
        actual = os.fstat(fd)
        if (before.st_dev, before.st_ino) != (actual.st_dev, actual.st_ino) or not stat.S_ISREG(actual.st_mode) or actual.st_nlink != 1:
            raise ValueError('File changed during open')
        with os.fdopen(fd, 'rb', closefd=False) as stream:
            raw = stream.read(maximum + 1)
        after = os.fstat(fd)
        if len(raw) > maximum or (actual.st_size, actual.st_mtime_ns, actual.st_ctime_ns) != (after.st_size, after.st_mtime_ns, after.st_ctime_ns):
            raise ValueError('File changed or exceeds bound')
        return raw
    finally:
        os.close(fd)


def read_json(path, maximum=MAX_META):
    return decode(regular(path, maximum))


def relative(value):
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value or len(value) > 512 or any(c in value for c in ('\x00', '\n', '\r', '\\')):
        raise ValueError('Confined bounded relative path required')
    p, w = PurePosixPath(value), PureWindowsPath(value)
    if p.is_absolute() or w.drive or str(p) != value or any(x in ('', '.', '..') for x in value.split('/')):
        raise ValueError('Normalized confined relative path required')
    if value != unicodedata.normalize('NFC', value):
        raise ValueError('Portable checkpoint names require NFC Unicode')
    reserved = {'CON', 'PRN', 'AUX', 'NUL', *(f'COM{i}' for i in range(1, 10)), *(f'LPT{i}' for i in range(1, 10)), *(f'COM{i}' for i in '¹²³'), *(f'LPT{i}' for i in '¹²³')}
    for part in p.parts:
        if any(ord(c) < 32 or ord(c) == 127 or c in '<>:"|?*' for c in part) or part.endswith(('.', ' ')) or part.split('.')[0].upper() in reserved:
            raise ValueError('Nonportable/reserved filename forbidden')
    if any(x.casefold() in ('.ssh', '.aws', '.git', '.codex', '.checkpoint.json', '.collection.json', '.logs') or x.casefold() == '.env' or x.casefold().startswith('.env.') for x in p.parts):
        raise ValueError('Credential/configuration/reserved path forbidden')
    return value


def identifier(value):
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not re.fullmatch('[0-9a-f]{32}', value):
        raise ValueError('32 lowercase hexadecimal checkpoint ID required')
    return value


def sha(value):
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not re.fullmatch('[0-9a-f]{64}', value):
        raise ValueError('Lowercase SHA-256 required')
    return value


def application(value):
    if not isinstance(value, dict) or set(value) != {'format', 'version', 'resume_notes'}:
        raise ValueError('Exact application fields required')
    for name in ('format', 'version'):
        x = value[name]
        if not isinstance(x, str) or not x.strip() or len(x) > 128 or any(c in x for c in ('\x00', '\n', '\r')):
            raise ValueError('Bounded application format/version required')
    if not isinstance(value['resume_notes'], str) or len(value['resume_notes'].encode('utf-8')) > 8192 or '\x00' in value['resume_notes']:
        raise ValueError('Bounded resume notes required; notes are not commands')
    return value


def provenance(value):
    if not isinstance(value, dict):
        raise ValueError('Provenance object required')
    if value.get('kind') == 'local' and set(value) == {'kind', 'manifest_sha256'}:
        sha(value['manifest_sha256'])
    elif value.get('kind') == 'linux' and set(value) == {'kind', 'job_id', 'manifest_sha256', 'image_id', 'supervisor_sha256', 'container_id', 'engine_id'}:
        identifier(value['job_id']); sha(value['manifest_sha256']); sha(value['supervisor_sha256']); sha(value['container_id'])
        if not isinstance(value['image_id'], str) or not re.fullmatch('sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}', value['image_id']):
            raise ValueError('Immutable image identity required')
        if not isinstance(value['engine_id'], str) or not value['engine_id'] or len(value['engine_id']) > 4096 or any(c in value['engine_id'] for c in ('\x00', '\n', '\r')):
            raise ValueError('Bounded engine identity required')
    else:
        raise ValueError('Unknown provenance fields/kind')
    return value


def inventory(files, payload=True):
    if not isinstance(files, list) or not 1 <= len(files) <= 32:
        raise ValueError('One to32 checkpoint files required')
    names, total, decoded = [], 0, []
    for item in files:
        fields = {'path', 'size', 'sha256'} | ({'data_base64'} if payload else set())
        if not isinstance(item, dict) or set(item) != fields:
            raise ValueError('Exact checkpoint file fields required')
        name = relative(item['path']); names.append(name); sha(item['sha256'])
        if type(item['size']) is not int or not 0 <= item['size'] <= MAX_FILE:
            raise ValueError('Invalid checkpoint size')
        total += item['size']
        if total > MAX_TOTAL:
            raise ValueError('Checkpoint total exceeds16MiB')
        if payload:
            if not isinstance(item['data_base64'], str) or len(item['data_base64']) > 4 * (MAX_FILE // 3 + 2):
                raise ValueError('Bounded base64 required')
            raw = base64.b64decode(item['data_base64'], validate=True)
            if len(raw) != item['size'] or digest(raw) != item['sha256']:
                raise ValueError('Checkpoint data size/hash mismatch')
            decoded.append((name, raw))
    normalized = [unicodedata.normalize('NFC', x.casefold()) for x in names]
    if len(set(normalized)) != len(normalized):
        raise ValueError('Duplicate/aliased checkpoint paths')
    paths = list(map(PurePosixPath, normalized))
    if any(a != b and a in b.parents for a in paths for b in paths):
        raise ValueError('Checkpoint file/directory conflict')
    return decoded


def validate_packet(packet):
    fields = {'schema_version', 'checkpoint_id', 'application', 'provenance', 'files', 'consistency'}
    if not isinstance(packet, dict) or set(packet) != fields or type(packet['schema_version']) is not int or packet['schema_version'] != 1:
        raise ValueError('Exact checkpoint packet schema1 required')
    identifier(packet['checkpoint_id']); application(packet['application']); provenance(packet['provenance'])
    if packet['consistency'] != CONSISTENCY:
        raise ValueError('Application consistency is not automatically verified')
    decoded = inventory(packet['files'])
    meta = {**packet, 'files': [{k: v for k, v in x.items() if k != 'data_base64'} for x in packet['files']]}
    meta['files'].sort(key=lambda x: x['path'])
    raw = canonical(meta)
    if len(raw) > MAX_META:
        raise ValueError('Checkpoint metadata exceeds64KiB')
    return meta, decoded, digest(raw)


def local_packet(manifest_path):
    manifest_path = no_symlinks(manifest_path)
    raw_manifest = regular(manifest_path, MAX_META); m = decode(raw_manifest)
    if not isinstance(m, dict) or set(m) != {'schema_version', 'checkpoint_id', 'application', 'files'} or type(m['schema_version']) is not int or m['schema_version'] != 1:
        raise ValueError('Exact local checkpoint manifest schema1 required')
    identifier(m['checkpoint_id']); application(m['application'])
    if not isinstance(m['files'], list) or not 1 <= len(m['files']) <= 32:
        raise ValueError('Bounded checkpoint files required')
    declared = []
    for x in m['files']:
        if not isinstance(x, dict) or set(x) != {'source', 'path', 'size', 'sha256'}:
            raise ValueError('Exact local source fields required')
        relative(x['source'])
        declared.append({k: x[k] for k in ('path', 'size', 'sha256')})
    inventory(declared, payload=False)  # Enforce aggregate bounds before reading any file.
    files = []
    for x in m['files']:
        if not isinstance(x, dict) or set(x) != {'source', 'path', 'size', 'sha256'}:
            raise ValueError('Exact local source fields required')
        source = manifest_path.parent / relative(x['source'])
        data = regular(source, x['size'])
        if len(data) != x['size'] or digest(data) != x['sha256']:
            raise ValueError('Local checkpoint source size/hash mismatch')
        files.append({'path': x['path'], 'size': x['size'], 'sha256': x['sha256'], 'data_base64': base64.b64encode(data).decode('ascii')})
    packet = {'schema_version': 1, 'checkpoint_id': m['checkpoint_id'], 'application': m['application'],
              'provenance': {'kind': 'local', 'manifest_sha256': digest(raw_manifest)},
              'files': files, 'consistency': CONSISTENCY}
    validate_packet(packet)
    if regular(manifest_path, MAX_META) != raw_manifest:
        raise ValueError('Input manifest changed during capture')
    return packet


def policy(value):
    if not isinstance(value, dict) or set(value) != {'schema_version', 'store_id', 'max_store_bytes', 'max_generations'} or type(value['schema_version']) is not int or value['schema_version'] != 1:
        raise ValueError('Invalid store policy')
    identifier(value['store_id'])
    if type(value['max_store_bytes']) is not int or not 4 * 1024**2 <= value['max_store_bytes'] <= MAX_DB or value['max_store_bytes'] % 1024**2:
        raise ValueError('Store byte cap must be4..512 wholeMiB')
    if type(value['max_generations']) is not int or not 1 <= value['max_generations'] <= 128:
        raise ValueError('Store generation cap must be1..128')
    return value


def safe_store(root):
    root = outside_skill(root)
    if not root.is_dir():
        raise ValueError('Existing dedicated store required')
    st = root.stat()
    if os.name == 'posix' and (st.st_uid != os.getuid() or st.st_mode & 0o077):
        raise ValueError('Store must be current-user private directory')
    allowed = {DB_NAME, '.lock', DB_NAME + '-journal'}
    for path in root.iterdir():
        if path.name not in allowed:
            raise ValueError('Unexpected store entry; nothing deleted')
        info = path.lstat()
        if not stat.S_ISREG(info.st_mode) or info.st_nlink != 1 or info.st_size > 2 * MAX_DB + 1024**2:
            raise ValueError('Unsafe store entry')
        if os.name == 'posix' and (info.st_uid != os.getuid() or info.st_mode & 0o077):
            raise ValueError('Store files must remain private and current-user owned')
    return root


@contextmanager
def store_lock(root):
    root = safe_store(root)
    path = root / '.lock'
    if path.is_symlink():
        raise ValueError('Lock symlink forbidden')
    fd = os.open(path, os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT | getattr(os, 'O_NOFOLLOW', 0), 0o600)
    stream = os.fdopen(fd, 'r+b'); acquired = False
    try:
        actual, named = os.fstat(fd), path.lstat()
        if not stat.S_ISREG(actual.st_mode) or actual.st_nlink != 1 or (actual.st_dev, actual.st_ino) != (named.st_dev, named.st_ino):
            raise ValueError('Lock identity changed')
        if actual.st_size == 0:
            stream.write(b'0'); stream.flush()
        stream.seek(0)
        try:
            if os.name == 'posix':
                import fcntl
                fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
            elif os.name == 'nt':
                import msvcrt
                msvcrt.locking(fd, msvcrt.LK_NBLCK, 1)
            else:
                raise ValueError('Unsupported lock platform')
            acquired = True
        except OSError as exc:
            raise ValueError('Another live process owns store lock; no lock is stolen') from exc
        yield root
    finally:
        if acquired:
            if os.name == 'posix':
                import fcntl
                fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_UN)
            else:
                import msvcrt
                stream.seek(0); msvcrt.locking(fd, msvcrt.LK_UNLCK, 1)
        stream.close()


def configure(conn, p=None):
    for pragma, expected in [('journal_mode=DELETE', 'delete'), ('synchronous=EXTRA', 3), ('foreign_keys=ON', 1), ('temp_store=MEMORY', 2), ('trusted_schema=OFF', 0)]:
        name = pragma.split('=')[0]
        conn.execute('PRAGMA ' + pragma)
        row = conn.execute('PRAGMA ' + name).fetchone()
        if row is None or row[0] != expected:
            raise ValueError('Required SQLite pragma unavailable: ' + name)
    if sys.platform == 'darwin':
        conn.execute('PRAGMA fullfsync=ON')
        if conn.execute('PRAGMA fullfsync').fetchone() != (1,):
            raise ValueError('SQLite fullfsync request unavailable')
    if conn.execute('PRAGMA page_size').fetchone() != (4096,):
        raise ValueError('Expected SQLite4096byte pages')
    if p is not None:
        maximum = p['max_store_bytes'] // 4096
        got = conn.execute('PRAGMA max_page_count=' + str(maximum)).fetchone()
        if got != (maximum,):
            raise ValueError('Database already exceeds cap or max_page_count unavailable')


def raw_connection(db):
    return sqlite3.connect(db.as_uri() + '?mode=rw', uri=True, isolation_level=None, timeout=0.2)


def connect_store(root):
    """Call under store_lock. Reapply the nonpersistent page ceiling on every open."""
    root = safe_store(root); db = root / DB_NAME
    st = db.lstat()
    if not stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode) or st.st_nlink != 1 or st.st_size > MAX_DB:
        raise ValueError('Invalid bounded store database')
    conn = raw_connection(db)
    try:
        if conn.execute('PRAGMA application_id').fetchone() != (APP_ID,) or conn.execute('PRAGMA user_version').fetchone() != (1,):
            raise ValueError('Unknown checkpoint database format')
        if conn.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM sqlite_master WHERE name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%'").fetchone() != (3,):
            raise ValueError('Unexpected database schema object count')
        if conn.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM sqlite_master WHERE name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%' AND (length(CAST(name AS BLOB))>32 OR length(CAST(type AS BLOB))>16)").fetchone() != (0,):
            raise ValueError('Invalid bounded schema object names')
        tables = conn.execute("SELECT name,type FROM sqlite_master WHERE name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%'").fetchall()
        if set(tables) != {('policy', 'table'), ('snapshots', 'table'), ('files', 'table')}:
            raise ValueError('Unexpected database schema objects')
        for name, expected in SCHEMA.items():
            row = conn.execute('SELECT length(CAST(sql AS BLOB)),typeof(sql) FROM sqlite_master WHERE name=?', (name,)).fetchone()
            if row is None or row[0] > 4096 or row[1] != 'text':
                raise ValueError('Invalid bounded table schema')
            actual = conn.execute('SELECT sql FROM sqlite_master WHERE name=?', (name,)).fetchone()[0]
            if ' '.join(actual.split()).casefold() != ' '.join(expected.split()).casefold():
                raise ValueError('Store columns/keys/constraints changed')
        row = conn.execute('SELECT length(CAST(body AS BLOB)),typeof(body),length(CAST(fingerprint AS BLOB)),typeof(fingerprint) FROM policy WHERE id=1').fetchone()
        if row is None or row[0] > MAX_META or row[1] != 'text' or row[2] != 64 or row[3] != 'text' or conn.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM policy').fetchone() != (1,):
            raise ValueError('Invalid bounded store policy record')
        body, fingerprint = conn.execute('SELECT body,fingerprint FROM policy WHERE id=1').fetchone()
        p = policy(decode(body))
        if canonical(p).decode('utf-8') != body or digest(canonical(p)) != fingerprint:
            raise ValueError('Store policy fingerprint changed')
        if st.st_size > p['max_store_bytes']:
            raise ValueError('Store database exceeds configured cap')
        configure(conn, p)
        if conn.execute('PRAGMA page_count').fetchone()[0] * 4096 > p['max_store_bytes']:
            raise ValueError('Database page count exceeds cap')
        return conn, p
    except Exception:
        conn.close(); raise


def fsync_directory(path):
    if os.name == 'posix':
        fd = os.open(path, os.O_RDONLY | getattr(os, 'O_DIRECTORY', 0))
        try: os.fsync(fd)
        finally: os.close(fd)


def init_store(root, max_store_bytes, max_generations):
    p = policy({'schema_version': 1, 'store_id': uuid.uuid4().hex, 'max_store_bytes': max_store_bytes, 'max_generations': max_generations})
    root = outside_skill(root)
    if root.exists():
        raise ValueError('Store destination must be new')
    root.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True); root.mkdir(mode=0o700)
    fd = os.open(root / DB_NAME, os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_WRONLY, 0o600); os.close(fd)
    with store_lock(root):
        conn = raw_connection(root / DB_NAME)
        try:
            configure(conn, p)
            conn.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
            conn.execute('PRAGMA application_id=' + str(APP_ID)); conn.execute('PRAGMA user_version=1')
            for statement in SCHEMA.values(): conn.execute(statement)
            conn.execute('INSERT INTO policy VALUES(1,?,?)', (canonical(p).decode(), digest(canonical(p))))
            conn.execute('COMMIT')
        finally: conn.close()
    fsync_directory(root); fsync_directory(root.parent)
    return {'state': 'initialized', 'policy': p, 'durability': 'process_crash_testable_storage_flush_dependent'}


def snapshot_packet(conn, identity):
    identifier(identity)
    row = conn.execute('SELECT length(CAST(metadata AS BLOB)),typeof(metadata),length(CAST(fingerprint AS BLOB)),typeof(fingerprint) FROM snapshots WHERE id=?', (identity,)).fetchone()
    if row is None:
        raise ValueError('Checkpoint not found')
    if row[0] > MAX_META or row[1] != 'text' or row[2] != 64 or row[3] != 'text':
        raise ValueError('Invalid bounded checkpoint metadata')
    meta_text, fingerprint = conn.execute('SELECT metadata,fingerprint FROM snapshots WHERE id=?', (identity,)).fetchone()
    meta = decode(meta_text)
    if not isinstance(meta, dict) or 'files' not in meta:
        raise ValueError('Invalid checkpoint metadata')
    count = conn.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM files WHERE checkpoint_id=?', (identity,)).fetchone()[0]
    if not 1 <= count <= 32:
        raise ValueError('Invalid checkpoint file count')
    invalid = conn.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM files WHERE checkpoint_id=? AND NOT (typeof(path)='text' AND length(CAST(path AS BLOB))<=2048 AND typeof(size)='integer' AND size BETWEEN 0 AND 8388608 AND typeof(sha256)='text' AND length(CAST(sha256 AS BLOB))=64 AND typeof(data)='blob' AND length(data)=size)", (identity,)).fetchone()[0]
    if invalid:
        raise ValueError('Invalid bounded stored file fields')
    file_rows = conn.execute('SELECT path,size,sha256,length(data),typeof(data) FROM files WHERE checkpoint_id=? ORDER BY path', (identity,)).fetchall()
    if not 1 <= len(file_rows) <= 32 or any(type(r[1]) is not int or not 0 <= r[1] <= MAX_FILE or r[1] != r[3] or r[4] != 'blob' for r in file_rows) or sum(r[1] for r in file_rows) > MAX_TOTAL:
        raise ValueError('Invalid bounded stored file inventory')
    files = []
    for name, size, checksum, _, _ in file_rows:
        relative(name); sha(checksum)
        raw = conn.execute('SELECT data FROM files WHERE checkpoint_id=? AND path=?', (identity, name)).fetchone()[0]
        files.append({'path': name, 'size': size, 'sha256': checksum, 'data_base64': base64.b64encode(raw).decode('ascii')})
    if meta.get('checkpoint_id') != identity or meta['files'] != [{k: v for k, v in f.items() if k != 'data_base64'} for f in files]:
        raise ValueError('Stored inventory differs from checkpoint declaration')
    packet = {**meta, 'files': files}
    actual_meta, _, actual_fingerprint = validate_packet(packet)
    if fingerprint != actual_fingerprint or canonical(actual_meta).decode() != meta_text:
        raise ValueError('Stored checkpoint metadata fingerprint mismatch')
    return packet


def summary(packet):
    return {**packet, 'files': [{k: v for k, v in f.items() if k != 'data_base64'} for f in packet['files']], 'execution_clearance': 'not_granted'}


def save_packet(root, packet, allow_save=False):
    if not allow_save:
        raise ValueError('Explicit --allow-save required')
    meta, decoded, fingerprint = validate_packet(packet)
    with store_lock(root) as root:
        conn, p = connect_store(root)
        try:
            conn.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
            old = conn.execute('SELECT 1 FROM snapshots WHERE id=?', (packet['checkpoint_id'],)).fetchone()
            if old is not None:
                existing = snapshot_packet(conn, packet['checkpoint_id'])
                existing_meta, _, existing_fingerprint = validate_packet(existing)
                if existing_fingerprint != fingerprint:
                    raise ValueError('Checkpoint ID already has different contents/provenance')
                conn.execute('ROLLBACK')
                return {'state': 'already_saved', **summary(existing)}
            if conn.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM snapshots').fetchone()[0] >= p['max_generations']:
                raise ValueError('Generation cap reached; explicit deletion required, no automatic pruning')
            conn.execute('INSERT INTO snapshots VALUES(?,?,?,?)', (packet['checkpoint_id'], canonical(meta).decode(), fingerprint, time.time()))
            expected = {x['path']: x for x in meta['files']}
            for name, raw in decoded:
                x = expected[name]
                conn.execute('INSERT INTO files VALUES(?,?,?,?,?)', (packet['checkpoint_id'], name, x['size'], x['sha256'], sqlite3.Binary(raw)))
            conn.execute('COMMIT')
            return {'state': 'saved', **summary({**meta, 'files': packet['files']})}
        except Exception:
            if conn.in_transaction: conn.execute('ROLLBACK')
            raise
        finally: conn.close()


def verify_store(root, checkpoint_id=None):
    with store_lock(root) as root:
        conn, p = connect_store(root)
        try:
            if conn.execute('PRAGMA integrity_check(1)').fetchone() != ('ok',):
                raise ValueError('SQLite integrity check failed')
            count = conn.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM snapshots').fetchone()[0]
            if count > p['max_generations']:
                raise ValueError('Stored generation count exceeds cap')
            if conn.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM snapshots WHERE typeof(id)!='text' OR length(CAST(id AS BLOB))!=32").fetchone() != (0,):
                raise ValueError('Invalid bounded checkpoint IDs')
            ids = [x[0] for x in conn.execute('SELECT id FROM snapshots ORDER BY id').fetchall()]
            if checkpoint_id is not None:
                identifier(checkpoint_id)
                if checkpoint_id not in ids: raise ValueError('Checkpoint not found')
                ids = [checkpoint_id]
            summaries = [summary(snapshot_packet(conn, identity)) for identity in ids]
            if conn.execute('PRAGMA foreign_key_check').fetchone() is not None:
                raise ValueError('Orphan checkpoint file record')
            return {'state': 'verified', 'policy': p, 'checkpoints': summaries,
                    'sqlite_version': sqlite3.sqlite_version, 'max_page_count': conn.execute('PRAGMA max_page_count').fetchone()[0],
                    'durability': 'process_crash_testable_storage_flush_dependent'}
        finally: conn.close()


def list_store(root):
    return verify_store(root)


def rename_new(source, destination):
    if os.name == 'nt':
        os.rename(source, destination); return
    libc = ctypes.CDLL(None, use_errno=True); a, b = os.fsencode(source), os.fsencode(destination)
    if sys.platform == 'linux' and hasattr(libc, 'renameat2'):
        call = libc.renameat2; call.argtypes = [ctypes.c_int, ctypes.c_char_p, ctypes.c_int, ctypes.c_char_p, ctypes.c_uint]
        result = call(-100, a, -100, b, 1)
    elif sys.platform == 'darwin' and hasattr(libc, 'renamex_np'):
        call = libc.renamex_np; call.argtypes = [ctypes.c_char_p, ctypes.c_char_p, ctypes.c_uint]
        result = call(a, b, 4)
    else: raise ValueError('Atomic no-replace publication unavailable')
    if result:
        number = ctypes.get_errno(); raise OSError(number, os.strerror(number), str(destination))


def write_new(path, raw):
    with Path(path).open('xb') as stream:
        if stream.write(raw) != len(raw): raise OSError('Short checkpoint write')
        stream.flush(); os.fsync(stream.fileno())


def restore(root, checkpoint_id, destination, allow_restore=False):
    if not allow_restore: raise ValueError('Explicit --allow-restore required')
    destination = outside_skill(destination)
    source = no_symlinks(root)
    if destination.exists() or destination == source or source in destination.parents or destination in source.parents:
        raise ValueError('New restore destination separate from store required')
    with store_lock(source):
        conn, _ = connect_store(source)
        try: packet = snapshot_packet(conn, checkpoint_id)
        finally: conn.close()
    _, decoded, _ = validate_packet(packet)
    destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    stage = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix='.checkpoint-restore-', dir=destination.parent))
    try:
        for name, raw in decoded:
            target = stage / name; target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            write_new(target, raw)
        receipt = summary(packet); write_new(stage / '.checkpoint.json', canonical(receipt) + b'\n')
        for folder in sorted([p for p in stage.rglob('*') if p.is_dir()], key=lambda p: len(p.parts), reverse=True): fsync_directory(folder)
        fsync_directory(stage); rename_new(stage, destination); fsync_directory(destination.parent)
        return {'state': 'restored', 'destination': str(destination), **receipt}
    finally:
        if stage.exists(): shutil.rmtree(stage)


def backup(root, destination, allow_copy=False, max_backup_bytes=None):
    if not allow_copy: raise ValueError('Explicit --allow-copy required')
    if max_backup_bytes is not None and (type(max_backup_bytes) is not int or not 1 <= max_backup_bytes <= MAX_DB):
        raise ValueError('Invalid optional backup byte cap')
    destination = outside_skill(destination); source = no_symlinks(root)
    if destination.exists() or destination == source or source in destination.parents or destination in source.parents:
        raise ValueError('New backup destination separate from store required')
    destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    stage = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix='.checkpoint-backup-', dir=destination.parent))
    try:
        fd = os.open(stage / DB_NAME, os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_WRONLY, 0o600); os.close(fd)
        with store_lock(source):
            conn, p = connect_store(source)
            target = raw_connection(stage / DB_NAME)
            try:
                if max_backup_bytes is not None and conn.execute('PRAGMA page_count').fetchone()[0] * 4096 > max_backup_bytes:
                    raise ValueError('Source database exceeds selected backup cap')
                configure(target, p); conn.backup(target); configure(target, p)
            finally: target.close(); conn.close()
        result = verify_store(stage)
        fsync_directory(stage); rename_new(stage, destination); fsync_directory(destination.parent)
        return {'state': 'backed_up', 'destination': str(destination), 'policy': result['policy'], 'checkpoints': len(result['checkpoints'])}
    finally:
        if stage.exists(): shutil.rmtree(stage)


def delete(root, checkpoint_id, allow_delete=False):
    if not allow_delete: raise ValueError('Explicit --allow-delete required')
    identifier(checkpoint_id)
    with store_lock(root) as root:
        conn, _ = connect_store(root)
        try:
            conn.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
            snapshot_packet(conn, checkpoint_id)
            conn.execute('DELETE FROM snapshots WHERE id=?', (checkpoint_id,)); conn.execute('COMMIT')
            return {'state': 'deleted', 'checkpoint_id': checkpoint_id, 'physical_erasure': 'not_guaranteed'}
        except Exception:
            if conn.in_transaction: conn.execute('ROLLBACK')
            raise
        finally: conn.close()


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest='operation', required=True)
    for name in ('init', 'save', 'list', 'verify', 'restore', 'backup', 'delete'):
        cmd = sub.add_parser(name); cmd.add_argument('--store', required=True); cmd.add_argument('--report', required=True)
        if name == 'init':
            cmd.add_argument('--max-store-bytes', type=int, required=True); cmd.add_argument('--max-generations', type=int, required=True)
        if name == 'save': cmd.add_argument('--manifest', required=True); cmd.add_argument('--allow-save', action='store_true')
        if name in ('verify', 'restore', 'delete'): cmd.add_argument('--checkpoint-id', required=name != 'verify')
        if name in ('restore', 'backup'): cmd.add_argument('--destination', required=True)
        if name == 'restore': cmd.add_argument('--allow-restore', action='store_true')
        if name == 'backup': cmd.add_argument('--allow-copy', action='store_true')
        if name == 'delete': cmd.add_argument('--allow-delete', action='store_true')
    args = parser.parse_args(argv); report = None
    try:
        report = no_symlinks(args.report)
        if report.exists(): raise ValueError('Report destination must be new')
        if args.operation == 'init': result = init_store(args.store, args.max_store_bytes, args.max_generations)
        elif args.operation == 'save': result = save_packet(args.store, local_packet(args.manifest), args.allow_save)
        elif args.operation == 'list': result = list_store(args.store)
        elif args.operation == 'verify': result = verify_store(args.store, args.checkpoint_id)
        elif args.operation == 'restore': result = restore(args.store, args.checkpoint_id, args.destination, args.allow_restore)
        elif args.operation == 'backup': result = backup(args.store, args.destination, args.allow_copy)
        else: result = delete(args.store, args.checkpoint_id, args.allow_delete)
        write_new(report, canonical({'schema_version': 1, 'ok': True, **result}) + b'\n'); return 0
    except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError, KeyError, sqlite3.Error) as exc:
        result = {'schema_version': 1, 'ok': False, 'state': 'failed', 'error': str(exc)[:1500], 'execution_clearance': 'not_granted'}
        try:
            if report is not None and not report.exists():
                no_symlinks(report); write_new(report, canonical(result) + b'\n')
        except (OSError, ValueError): pass
        print(json.dumps(result), file=sys.stderr); return 2


if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())
```
