## Included resource: scripts/claim_audit.py

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
"""Offline claim/review coverage audit; record validity grants no drafting clearance."""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from decimal import Decimal
import importlib.util
import json
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path, PureWindowsPath
import stat
import sys

MAX_BYTES = 10 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_SOURCE_BYTES = 20 * 1024 * 1024
LIMITS = {'sources': 32, 'claims': 100, 'reviews': 500}
PUBLICATION_STATUS = ('current', 'corrected', 'retracted', 'withdrawn', 'unknown')
JUDGMENTS = ('supports', 'partial', 'contradicts', 'uncertain')
_LEGACY = None


def _legacy():
    """Load the unchanged sibling, including when imported by absolute file path."""
    global _LEGACY
    if _LEGACY is None:
        spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(
            '_research_writer_support_integrity', Path(__file__).with_name('support_tools.py'))
        if spec is None or spec.loader is None:
            raise ImportError('Cannot load local support integrity checker')
        module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
        spec.loader.exec_module(module)
        _LEGACY = module
    return _LEGACY


def _strict_pairs(pairs):
    result = {}
    for key, value in pairs:
        if key in result:
            raise ValueError('Duplicate JSON key: ' + key)
        result[key] = value
    return result


def _invalid_constant(value):
    raise ValueError('Nonfinite JSON number: ' + value)


def read_json(path):
    raw = _read_source(Path(path))
    if len(raw) > MAX_BYTES:
        raise ValueError('Manifest exceeds 10 MiB')
    return json.loads(raw.decode('utf-8'), object_pairs_hook=_strict_pairs,
                      parse_float=Decimal, parse_constant=_invalid_constant)


def _validate_json(value):
    """Bound direct API inputs too, rejecting non-JSON/nonfinite values."""
    def visit(item):
        if item is None or type(item) in (str, bool, int):
            return
        if isinstance(item, (float, Decimal)):
            if not (item.is_finite() if isinstance(item, Decimal) else math.isfinite(item)):
                raise ValueError('Nonfinite number in manifest')
            return
        if isinstance(item, list):
            for child in item:
                visit(child)
            return
        if isinstance(item, dict):
            for key, child in item.items():
                if not isinstance(key, str):
                    raise ValueError('JSON object keys must be strings')
                visit(child)
            return
        raise ValueError('Manifest contains a non-JSON value')
    visit(value)
    payload = json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False,
                         default=lambda item: str(item) if isinstance(item, Decimal) else item)
    if len(payload.encode('utf-8')) > MAX_BYTES:
        raise ValueError('Manifest exceeds 10 MiB')


def _source_path(root, name):
    if not isinstance(name, str) or not name.strip():
        raise ValueError('Nonempty relative source path required')
    windows = PureWindowsPath(name)
    if (Path(name).is_absolute() or windows.drive or windows.root
            or '..' in Path(name).parts or '..' in windows.parts
            or any(':' in part for part in windows.parts)):
        raise ValueError('Relative source path without traversal or Windows escape required')
    path = (root / name).resolve()
    if not path.is_relative_to(root):
        raise ValueError('Source resolves outside base directory')
    return path


def _read_source(path):
    """Read bounded regular files, rejecting FIFOs/devices before any read.

    The initial stat rejects known special files. On POSIX, O_NONBLOCK also
    prevents a regular-to-FIFO replacement from blocking open; fstat checks the
    opened object before reading. The legacy checker subsequently reopens paths,
    so callers must use a cooperating filesystem without concurrent replacement.
    This is input validation, not an adversarial filesystem security sandbox.
    """
    if not stat.S_ISREG(path.stat().st_mode):
        raise ValueError('Source must be a regular file')
    flags = os.O_RDONLY | getattr(os, 'O_BINARY', 0) | getattr(os, 'O_NONBLOCK', 0)
    descriptor = os.open(path, flags)
    try:
        if not stat.S_ISREG(os.fstat(descriptor).st_mode):
            raise ValueError('Opened source must be a regular file')
        with os.fdopen(descriptor, 'rb') as stream:
            descriptor = None
            return stream.read(MAX_BYTES + 1)
    finally:
        if descriptor is not None:
            os.close(descriptor)


def audit_claims(manifest, base_directory='.'):
    """Validate source/review records and declared claim/source pair coverage.

    Study families and publication status are declarations, not independently
    certified facts. All judgments remain the named reviewer's assertions.
    """
    errors = []
    result = {
        'schema_version': 1, 'status': 'failed', 'passed': False,
        'record_integrity': 'not_checked',
        'semantic_entailment': 'not_automatically_verified',
        'drafting_clearance': 'not_granted',
        'scientific_validity': 'not_checked',
        'study_family_status': 'declared_not_independently_verified',
        'errors': errors, 'claims': [], 'flags': [], 'review_needed': True,
        'declared_pair_coverage': {'required': 0, 'covered': 0, 'missing': []},
    }

    def error(code, record, detail):
        errors.append({'code': code, 'record': record, 'detail': detail})

    def nonempty(value):
        return isinstance(value, str) and bool(value.strip())

    def records(name):
        values = manifest.get(name, [])
        if not isinstance(values, list):
            error('invalid_records', name, 'Expected an array')
            return []
        if len(values) > LIMITS[name]:
            error('record_limit', name, 'Maximum ' + str(LIMITS[name]) + ' records')
            return []
        indexed = {}
        for index, item in enumerate(values):
            label = name + '[' + str(index) + ']'
            if not isinstance(item, dict) or not nonempty(item.get('id')):
                error('invalid_record', label, 'Object with nonempty string id required')
            elif item['id'] in indexed:
                error('duplicate_id', label, item['id'])
            else:
                indexed[item['id']] = item
        return list(indexed.values())

    try:
        _validate_json(manifest)
    except (ValueError, TypeError, UnicodeError, OverflowError, RecursionError) as exc:
        error('invalid_manifest', 'manifest', str(exc))
        return result
    if not isinstance(manifest, dict):
        error('invalid_manifest', 'manifest', 'Expected an object')
        return result
    if type(manifest.get('schema_version')) is not int or manifest['schema_version'] != 1:
        error('invalid_schema', 'manifest', 'schema_version must be integer 1')
    sources, claims, reviews = records('sources'), records('claims'), records('reviews')
    source_index = {item['id']: item for item in sources}
    claim_index = {item['id']: item for item in claims}
    for source in sources:
        label = 'source:' + source['id']
        if not nonempty(source.get('study_family')):
            error('invalid_study_family', label, 'study_family must be a nonempty string')
        if source.get('publication_status') not in PUBLICATION_STATUS:
            error('invalid_publication_status', label, 'Expected a declared publication status')
    required_pairs = []
    for claim in claims:
        label = 'claim:' + claim['id']
        if not nonempty(claim.get('text')):
            error('invalid_claim_text', label, 'text must be a nonempty string')
        if claim.get('required_access') not in ('abstract', 'full_text'):
            error('invalid_required_access', label, 'Expected abstract or full_text')
        ids = claim.get('required_source_ids')
        if (not isinstance(ids, list) or not ids
                or any(not nonempty(sid) for sid in ids)):
            error('invalid_required_sources', label, 'Nonempty array of unique source IDs required')
            continue
        if len(ids) != len(set(ids)):
            error('duplicate_required_source', label, 'Required source IDs must be unique')
        for sid in dict.fromkeys(ids):
            required_pairs.append((claim['id'], sid))
            source = source_index.get(sid)
            if source is None:
                error('unknown_source', label, sid)
            elif (source.get('access') not in ('abstract', 'full_text')
                  or (claim.get('required_access') == 'full_text'
                      and source.get('access') != 'full_text')):
                error('insufficient_access', label, 'Required source ' + sid + ' lacks required access')

    invalid_reviews = set()
    for review in reviews:
        label = 'review:' + review['id']
        before = len(errors)
        cid, sid = review.get('claim_id'), review.get('source_id')
        claim = claim_index.get(cid) if isinstance(cid, str) else None
        source = source_index.get(sid) if isinstance(sid, str) else None
        if claim is None:
            error('unknown_claim', label, 'Review must name a declared claim_id')
        elif review.get('claim') != claim.get('text'):
            error('claim_text_mismatch', label, 'Review claim must exactly equal declared text')
        if source is None:
            error('unknown_source', label, 'Review must name a declared source_id')
        elif claim is not None and (source.get('access') not in ('abstract', 'full_text')
                or (claim.get('required_access') == 'full_text' and source.get('access') != 'full_text')):
            error('insufficient_access', label, 'Reviewed source lacks claim required access')
        if review.get('judgment') not in JUDGMENTS:
            error('invalid_judgment', label, 'Expected supports, partial, contradicts or uncertain')
        if len(errors) != before:
            invalid_reviews.add(review['id'])

    # Enforce confinement and aggregate bytes before handing any path to the
    # unchanged legacy checker. A failed resource/path preflight stops source IO.
    try:
        root = Path(base_directory).resolve()
    except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError, RuntimeError) as exc:
        error('invalid_base_directory', 'manifest', str(exc))
        return result
    total_bytes, preflight_ok = 0, True
    for source in sources:
        try:
            path = _source_path(root, source.get('path'))
            raw = _read_source(path)
            if len(raw) > MAX_BYTES:
                raise ValueError('Source exceeds 10 MiB')
            total_bytes += len(raw)
            if total_bytes > MAX_SOURCE_BYTES:
                raise ValueError('Source aggregate exceeds 20 MiB')
            raw.decode('utf-8')
        except (OSError, ValueError, UnicodeError, RuntimeError) as exc:
            error('source_unreadable_or_unsafe', 'source:' + source['id'], str(exc))
            preflight_ok = False
            break
    valid_reviews = []
    if preflight_ok:
        adapted = [dict(review, judgment='uncertain') if review.get('judgment') == 'partial'
                   else dict(review) for review in reviews]
        legacy = _legacy().verify_support(
            {'schema_version': manifest.get('schema_version'), 'sources': sources, 'reviews': adapted}, root)
        result['integrity_check'] = legacy
        errors.extend(legacy['errors'])
        valid_ids = {review['id'] for review in legacy['recorded_judgments']}
        valid_reviews = [review for review in reviews
                         if review['id'] in valid_ids and review['id'] not in invalid_reviews]
        result['record_integrity'] = 'failed' if errors else 'valid'
    else:
        result['record_integrity'] = 'failed'

    covered = {(review['claim_id'], review['source_id']) for review in valid_reviews}
    missing = [{'claim_id': cid, 'source_id': sid}
               for cid, sid in required_pairs if (cid, sid) not in covered]
    result['declared_pair_coverage'] = {
        'required': len(required_pairs),
        'covered': sum(pair in covered for pair in required_pairs),
        'missing': missing, 'complete': not missing,
    }
    global_flags = set()
    source_families = {}
    result['sources'] = []
    for source in sources:
        result['sources'].append({field: source.get(field) for field in (
            'id', 'version', 'access', 'identity_status', 'path', 'sha256',
            'locator', 'study_family', 'publication_status')})
        status = source.get('publication_status')
        if status in PUBLICATION_STATUS and status != 'current':
            global_flags.add('publication_status_' + status)
        family, version = source.get('study_family'), source.get('version')
        if nonempty(family) and nonempty(version):
            source_families.setdefault(family, set()).add(version)
    if any(len(versions) > 1 for versions in source_families.values()):
        global_flags.add('same_family_multiple_versions')
    for claim in claims:
        claim_reviews = [review for review in valid_reviews if review['claim_id'] == claim['id']]
        required_ids = claim.get('required_source_ids', [])
        if not isinstance(required_ids, list):
            required_ids = []
        declared_ids = {sid for sid in required_ids if isinstance(sid, str) and sid in source_index}
        reviewed_ids = {review['source_id'] for review in claim_reviews}
        families, flags = {}, set()
        for sid in sorted(declared_ids | reviewed_ids):
            source = source_index[sid]
            family = source.get('study_family')
            if nonempty(family):
                group = families.setdefault(family, {'study_family': family, 'source_ids': [], 'versions': []})
                group['source_ids'].append(sid)
                version = source.get('version')
                if nonempty(version) and version not in group['versions']:
                    group['versions'].append(version)
            status = source.get('publication_status')
            if status in PUBLICATION_STATUS and status != 'current':
                flags.add('publication_status_' + status)
        for family in families.values():
            if len(family['versions']) > 1:
                flags.add('same_family_multiple_versions')
        for review in claim_reviews:
            if review['judgment'] != 'supports':
                flags.add({'partial': 'partial_judgment', 'contradicts': 'contradiction',
                           'uncertain': 'uncertain_judgment'}[review['judgment']])
        claim_missing = [pair for pair in missing if pair['claim_id'] == claim['id']]
        if claim_missing:
            flags.add('missing_required_reviews')
        judgments = []
        for review in claim_reviews:
            # Preserve actual partial judgments rather than the legacy adapter.
            source = source_index[review['source_id']]
            record = {field: review.get(field) for field in (
                'id', 'claim_id', 'claim', 'source_id', 'source_version',
                'source_sha256', 'passage', 'start', 'end', 'locator',
                'judgment', 'rationale', 'reviewer', 'reviewed_at')}
            judgments.append(dict(record, access=source.get('access'),
                                  study_family=source.get('study_family'),
                                  publication_status=source.get('publication_status'),
                                  judgment_status='recorded_not_automatically_verified'))
        declared_families = {source_index[sid].get('study_family') for sid in declared_ids
                             if nonempty(source_index[sid].get('study_family'))}
        reviewed_families = {source_index[sid].get('study_family') for sid in reviewed_ids
                             if nonempty(source_index[sid].get('study_family'))}
        result['claims'].append({
            'id': claim['id'], 'text': claim.get('text'),
            'required_access': claim.get('required_access'),
            'required_source_ids': required_ids, 'judgments': judgments,
            'declared_family_count': len(declared_families),
            'reviewed_family_count': len(reviewed_families),
            'family_count': len(families), 'families': list(families.values()),
            'missing_required_pairs': claim_missing, 'flags': sorted(flags),
            'review_needed': bool(flags),
        })
        global_flags.update(flags)
    result['flags'] = sorted(global_flags)
    result['counts'] = {'sources': len(sources), 'claims': len(claims),
                        'reviews': len(reviews), 'valid_review_records': len(valid_reviews),
                        'declared_families': len({source['study_family'] for source in sources
                                                 if nonempty(source.get('study_family'))}),
                        'source_bytes': total_bytes}
    if errors:
        result['status'] = 'failed'
    elif not claims:
        result['status'] = 'not_checked'
        result['reason'] = 'no_claims_to_check'
    elif missing:
        result['status'] = 'incomplete'
    else:
        result['status'], result['passed'] = 'records_valid', True
    result['review_needed'] = bool(global_flags) or result['status'] != 'records_valid'
    return result


def _preflight_output(path):
    target = Path(path)
    if os.path.lexists(target):
        raise FileExistsError('Output exists; choose a new output path')
    if not target.parent.is_dir():
        raise FileNotFoundError('Output parent directory does not exist')
    return target


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    commands = parser.add_subparsers(dest='command', required=True)
    audit = commands.add_parser('audit-claims')
    audit.add_argument('--input', required=True)
    audit.add_argument('--output', required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        target = _preflight_output(args.output)  # Must precede all input IO.
        manifest = read_json(args.input)
        result = audit_claims(manifest, Path(args.input).resolve().parent)
        payload = json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, allow_nan=False,
                             default=str) + '\n'
        with target.open('x', encoding='utf-8') as stream:
            stream.write(payload)
        return 0 if result['status'] == 'records_valid' else 2
    except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError, UnicodeError, RuntimeError, RecursionError) as exc:
        print(json.dumps({'status': 'failed', 'passed': False, 'error': str(exc),
                          'semantic_entailment': 'not_automatically_verified',
                          'drafting_clearance': 'not_granted'}), file=sys.stderr)
        return 2


if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
"""Bounded Open Babel conversion with independent RDKit identity validation.

Only explicit local conversion executes an engine. No downloads or network.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import io
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import platform
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time

MAX_BYTES = 10 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_MOLECULES = 32
FORMATS = ('smi', 'sdf')


def sha(raw):
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()


def bounded_read(path, maximum=MAX_BYTES):
    with Path(path).open('rb') as stream:
        raw = stream.read(maximum + 1)
    if len(raw) > maximum:
        raise ValueError('File exceeds byte limit')
    return raw


def identities(raw, format_name):
    """Independently parse every input/output molecule; no Open Babel API used."""
    from rdkit import Chem
    from rdkit.Chem import rdMolDescriptors
    if format_name not in FORMATS:
        raise ValueError('Only explicit smi or sdf formats are supported')
    if len(raw) > MAX_BYTES or not raw.strip():
        raise ValueError('Empty or oversized molecular input')
    if format_name == 'smi':
        lines = [line.strip() for line in raw.decode('utf-8-sig').splitlines() if line.strip()]
        if not 1 <= len(lines) <= MAX_MOLECULES:
            raise ValueError('Expected 1–32 molecules')
        molecules = [Chem.MolFromSmiles(line.split()[0]) for line in lines]
    else:
        text = raw.decode('utf-8-sig')
        blocks = text.split('$$$$')
        if blocks[-1].strip() or not 1 <= len(blocks) - 1 <= MAX_MOLECULES:
            raise ValueError('SDF requires 1–32 terminated molecule records')
        molecules = list(Chem.ForwardSDMolSupplier(io.BytesIO(raw), removeHs=False, strictParsing=True))
        if len(molecules) != len(blocks) - 1:
            raise ValueError('SDF record count differs from parsed molecules')
    result = []
    for index, mol in enumerate(molecules, 1):
        if mol is None or mol.GetNumAtoms() == 0:
            raise ValueError('Invalid molecule at position ' + str(index))
        Chem.SanitizeMol(mol)
        mol = Chem.RemoveHs(mol)
        Chem.AssignStereochemistry(mol, cleanIt=True, force=True)
        result.append({'position': index,
                       'canonical_isomeric_smiles': Chem.MolToSmiles(mol, canonical=True, isomericSmiles=True),
                       'formula': rdMolDescriptors.CalcMolFormula(mol),
                       'formal_charge': Chem.GetFormalCharge(mol),
                       'heavy_atom_count': mol.GetNumHeavyAtoms(),
                       'bond_types': sorted(str(b.GetBondType()) for b in mol.GetBonds())})
    return result


def bounded_process(argv, directory, timeout, watched=()):
    """No shell; bound elapsed time and staged output/log file sizes."""
    directory = Path(directory)
    stdout, stderr = directory / 'stdout.log', directory / 'stderr.log'
    with stdout.open('wb') as out, stderr.open('wb') as err:
        process = subprocess.Popen(argv, cwd=directory, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=out, stderr=err,
                                   shell=False)
        start = time.monotonic()
        failure = None
        try:
            while process.poll() is None:
                if time.monotonic() - start > timeout:
                    failure = 'Engine timeout'
                elif any(p.exists() and p.stat().st_size > MAX_BYTES for p in [stdout, stderr, *map(Path, watched)]):
                    failure = 'Engine output exceeds byte limit'
                if failure:
                    process.kill()
                    break
                time.sleep(0.02)
            process.wait(timeout=5)
        finally:
            if process.poll() is None:
                process.kill(); process.wait(timeout=5)
    logs = {'stdout': bounded_read(stdout).decode('utf-8', errors='replace'),
            'stderr': bounded_read(stderr).decode('utf-8', errors='replace')}
    if failure:
        return {'returncode': process.returncode, 'elapsed_seconds': time.monotonic() - start,
                'error': failure, **logs}
    return {'returncode': process.returncode, 'elapsed_seconds': time.monotonic() - start, **logs}


def convert(args):
    if isinstance(args.timeout, bool) or not 0 < args.timeout <= 120:
        raise ValueError('Timeout must be greater than zero and at most 120 seconds')
    if args.input_format not in FORMATS or args.output_format not in FORMATS:
        raise ValueError('Only smi and sdf are supported')
    source, destination, report_path = map(lambda p: Path(p).expanduser().resolve(),
                                         (args.input, args.output, args.report))
    if len({source, destination, report_path}) != 3:
        raise ValueError('Input, molecular output and report must be distinct')
    if not args.force and (destination.exists() or report_path.exists()):
        raise FileExistsError('An output exists; preserve it or explicitly use --force')
    if not destination.parent.is_dir() or not report_path.parent.is_dir():
        raise ValueError('Output/report parent directories must exist')
    raw = bounded_read(source)
    before = identities(raw, args.input_format)
    supplied = os.fspath(args.executable)
    resolved = shutil.which(supplied) or (str(Path(supplied).expanduser().resolve()) if Path(supplied).expanduser().is_file() else None)
    if not resolved or not os.access(resolved, os.X_OK):
        raise ValueError('Provide a compatible executable; none will be installed automatically')
    binary = Path(resolved).resolve()
    # A native binary can exceed the molecular-file limit; hash streaming bytes.
    hasher = hashlib.sha256()
    with binary.open('rb') as stream:
        for part in iter(lambda: stream.read(1024 * 1024), b''):
            hasher.update(part)
    result = {'status': 'failed', 'passed': False, 'engine': 'Open Babel',
              'backend': 'local_cpu_conversion', 'input': {'path': str(source), 'sha256': sha(raw),
                                                         'format': args.input_format, 'identities': before},
              'executable': {'path': str(binary), 'sha256': hasher.hexdigest()},
              'runtime': {'python': platform.python_version(), 'platform': platform.platform()},
              'scientific_validity': 'not_checked', 'network_contacted': False,
              'limits': ['Identity conversion only; no 3D pose, coordinate or property preservation claim.',
                         'No affinity, biological function or scientific accuracy benchmark.',
                         'Executable hash does not hash its complete environment or linked libraries.']}
    from rdkit import rdBase
    result['runtime']['rdkit'] = rdBase.rdkitVersion
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='research-writer-convert-') as temporary:
        work = Path(temporary)
        version_dir = work / 'version'; version_dir.mkdir()
        version = bounded_process([str(binary), '-V'], version_dir, min(args.timeout, 10))
        result['version_probe'] = version
        if version.get('error') or version['returncode'] != 0 or 'Open Babel' not in version['stdout'] + version['stderr']:
            result['error'] = 'Executable version probe failed'
            return result, None
        staged_input, staged_output = work / ('input.' + args.input_format), work / ('output.' + args.output_format)
        staged_input.write_bytes(raw)
        argv = [str(binary), '-i' + args.input_format, str(staged_input), '-o' + args.output_format, '-O', str(staged_output)]
        if args.input_format == 'smi' and args.output_format == 'sdf':
            # Open Babel's 0D stereo extension is not independently understood
            # by RDKit. Generate a 2D depiction so standard bond stereo survives.
            argv.append('--gen2D')
            result['coordinate_operation'] = 'generated_2D_depiction_not_3D'
        else:
            result['coordinate_operation'] = 'no_generation_requested'
        result['argv'] = argv
        run_dir = work / 'run'; run_dir.mkdir()
        execution = bounded_process(argv, run_dir, args.timeout, [staged_output])
        result['execution'] = execution
        if execution.get('error') or execution['returncode'] != 0:
            result['error'] = execution.get('error', 'Nonzero engine exit')
            return result, None
        try:
            converted = bounded_read(staged_output)
            after = identities(converted, args.output_format)
        except (OSError, UnicodeError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as exc:
            result['error'] = 'Independent output parsing failed: ' + str(exc)
            return result, None
        result['output'] = {'path': str(destination), 'sha256': sha(converted),
                            'format': args.output_format, 'identities': after}
        if after != before:
            result['error'] = 'Independent molecular count/order/identity/charge/bond/stereochemistry mismatch'
            return result, None
        result.update(status='converted_identity_checked', passed=True, identity_validation='independent_rdkit_pass')
        return result, converted


def write_report(path, result, force=False):
    with Path(path).open('w' if force else 'x', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n') as stream:
        json.dump(result, stream, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False)
        stream.write('\n')


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    subs = parser.add_subparsers(dest='command', required=True)
    sub = subs.add_parser('convert')
    sub.add_argument('--executable', required=True)
    sub.add_argument('--input', required=True)
    sub.add_argument('--input-format', required=True, choices=FORMATS)
    sub.add_argument('--output', required=True)
    sub.add_argument('--output-format', required=True, choices=FORMATS)
    sub.add_argument('--report', required=True)
    sub.add_argument('--timeout', type=float, default=30)
    sub.add_argument('--force', action='store_true')
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        result, output = convert(args)
        if result['passed']:
            with Path(args.output).open('wb' if args.force else 'xb') as stream:
                stream.write(output)
        write_report(args.report, result, args.force)
        print(json.dumps({'status': result['status'], 'report': args.report}))
        return 0 if result['passed'] else 2
    except (OSError, UnicodeError, ValueError, RuntimeError, ImportError) as exc:
        failure = {'status': 'unavailable' if isinstance(exc, ImportError) else 'failed',
                   'passed': False, 'error': str(exc), 'scientific_validity': 'not_checked'}
        # Never replace an existing report while handling an exception.
        if not Path(args.report).exists():
            try:
                write_report(args.report, failure)
            except OSError:
                pass
        print(json.dumps(failure), file=sys.stderr)
        return 2


if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
"""Bounded no-key OpenAlex discovery and version-pinned arXiv HTML retrieval.

Python 3.10+, standard library only. Requests are serialized in this process and
arXiv request starts are spaced by at least three seconds. Operators must also
coordinate other processes and all machines they control; this is no global
rate-limit service. No retries, pagination, PDF fallback, scripts or asset fetches.
Retrieval and provider flags never establish reading, scientific truth or rights.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import hashlib
from html.parser import HTMLParser
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import sys
import threading
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.parse
import urllib.request

MAX_BYTES = 10 * 1024 * 1024
TIMEOUT = 10
ARXIV_SPACING = 3.0
OPENALEX = 'https://api.openalex.org/works'
ARXIV = 'https://arxiv.org/html/'
_NETWORK_LOCK = threading.Lock()
_last_arxiv_start = None
_ARXIV_PATTERN = r'(?:\d{4}\.\d{4,5}|[a-z][a-z0-9.-]*/\d{7})v[1-9]\d*'
_ARXIV_RE = re.compile(_ARXIV_PATTERN)


def now():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


def _sha(raw):
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()


def arxiv_id(value):
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not re.fullmatch(_ARXIV_PATTERN, value):
        raise ValueError('arXiv ID must be a bare modern or legacy ID with an explicit positive vN')
    return value


def work_id(value):
    if not isinstance(value, str):
        raise ValueError('Invalid OpenAlex work ID')
    value = value.removeprefix('https://openalex.org/')
    if not re.fullmatch(r'W[1-9]\d*', value):
        raise ValueError('OpenAlex work ID must be W followed by a positive integer')
    return value


def normalize_doi(value):
    if not isinstance(value, str):
        raise ValueError('DOI must be a string')
    value = re.sub(r'(?i)^doi:\s*', '', value.strip())
    if re.match(r'(?i)^https?://(?:dx\.)?doi\.org/', value):
        parts = urllib.parse.urlsplit(value)
        if parts.query or parts.fragment:
            raise ValueError('DOI URL must not include a query or fragment')
        value = urllib.parse.unquote(parts.path.lstrip('/'))
    if len(value) > 1000 or not re.fullmatch(r'10\.\d{4,9}/[^\s<>"{}\\]+', value):
        raise ValueError('Invalid DOI')
    return value.lower()


def allowed_url(url):
    """Validate even direct request() callers; query parameters cannot enable keys/pages."""
    try:
        p = urllib.parse.urlsplit(url)
        if p.scheme != 'https' or p.username or p.password or p.port not in (None, 443) or p.fragment:
            return False
        if p.hostname == 'arxiv.org' and not p.query and p.path.startswith('/html/'):
            arxiv_id(p.path[len('/html/'):])
            return True
        if p.hostname != 'api.openalex.org':
            return False
        if p.path == '/works':
            q = urllib.parse.parse_qs(p.query, keep_blank_values=True, strict_parsing=True)
            return (set(q) == {'search', 'per_page'} and all(len(v) == 1 for v in q.values())
                    and bool(q['search'][0].strip()) and len(q['search'][0]) <= 1000
                    and re.fullmatch(r'(?:[1-9]|1\d|20)', q['per_page'][0]) is not None)
        if p.query or not p.path.startswith('/works/'):
            return False
        suffix = urllib.parse.unquote(p.path[len('/works/'):])
        if suffix.startswith('https://doi.org/'):
            normalize_doi(suffix)
        else:
            work_id(suffix)
        return True
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return False


class RestrictedRedirect(urllib.request.HTTPRedirectHandler):
    def redirect_request(self, req, fp, code, msg, headers, newurl):
        # Even same-provider redirects may silently change a requested version.
        return None


def request(url):
    """Return status/body/receipt; never follow redirects or retry failures."""
    global _last_arxiv_start
    if not allowed_url(url):
        raise ValueError('Only fixed no-key OpenAlex and pinned arXiv HTML endpoints are permitted')
    receipt = {'url': url, 'timeout_seconds': TIMEOUT, 'max_bytes': MAX_BYTES,
               'http_status': None, 'sha256': None, 'redirects_followed': False}
    req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={
        'User-Agent': 'research-writer-discovery-tools/0.10.0',
        'Accept': 'text/html' if url.startswith(ARXIV) else 'application/json',
        'Accept-Encoding': 'identity'})
    with _NETWORK_LOCK:
        if url.startswith(ARXIV):
            if _last_arxiv_start is not None:
                time.sleep(max(0.0, ARXIV_SPACING - (time.monotonic() - _last_arxiv_start)))
            _last_arxiv_start = time.monotonic()
        receipt['retrieved_at'] = now()
        try:
            opener = urllib.request.build_opener(RestrictedRedirect())
            with opener.open(req, timeout=TIMEOUT) as response:
                receipt['http_status'] = response.status
                receipt['content_type'] = response.headers.get('Content-Type')
                receipt['content_encoding'] = response.headers.get('Content-Encoding')
                raw = response.read(MAX_BYTES + 1)
            receipt['bytes'] = len(raw)
            receipt['sha256'] = _sha(raw)
            if len(raw) > MAX_BYTES:
                receipt['hash_scope'] = 'bounded prefix; response rejected'
                return {'status': 'failed', 'error': 'response_too_large', 'receipt': receipt}
            receipt['hash_scope'] = 'complete response bytes'
            if receipt['content_encoding'] not in (None, '', 'identity'):
                return {'status': 'failed', 'error': 'unsupported_content_encoding', 'receipt': receipt}
            return {'status': 'success', 'body': raw, 'receipt': receipt}
        except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
            receipt.update(http_status=exc.code,
                           retry_after=exc.headers.get('Retry-After') if exc.headers else None)
            exc.close()
            return {'status': 'unavailable' if exc.code in (404, 429) or exc.code >= 500 else 'failed',
                    'error': 'http_error', 'receipt': receipt}
        except (urllib.error.URLError, TimeoutError, OSError) as exc:
            return {'status': 'unavailable', 'error': type(exc).__name__, 'receipt': receipt}


def _record(raw):
    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
        raise ValueError('OpenAlex record must be an object')
    wid = work_id(raw.get('id'))
    doi = normalize_doi(raw['doi']) if raw.get('doi') is not None else None
    refs = raw.get('referenced_works', [])
    if not isinstance(refs, list) or any(not isinstance(x, str) for x in refs):
        raise ValueError('Invalid referenced_works')
    for ref in refs:
        work_id(ref)
    retracted = raw.get('is_retracted')
    if retracted is not None and not isinstance(retracted, bool):
        raise ValueError('Invalid is_retracted flag')
    location = raw.get('primary_location')
    if location is not None and not isinstance(location, dict):
        raise ValueError('Invalid primary_location')
    return {'identity': 'openalex:' + wid, 'id': raw['id'], 'work_id': wid, 'doi': doi,
            'title': raw.get('title'), 'version': raw.get('version'), 'access': 'metadata',
            'full_text_retrieved': False, 'read_or_reviewed': False,
            'referenced_works': refs, 'reference_access': 'citation leads; not retrieved or read',
            'is_retracted': retracted, 'retraction_certification': 'not_certified',
            'license': location.get('license') if location else None,
            'reuse_rights': 'article-specific; unknown unless independently established',
            'open_access': raw.get('open_access'), 'primary_location': location, 'raw': raw}


def search(query, limit=10):
    if not isinstance(query, str) or not query.strip() or len(query) > 1000:
        raise ValueError('Query must contain 1–1000 characters')
    if isinstance(limit, bool) or not isinstance(limit, int) or not 1 <= limit <= 20:
        raise ValueError('Limit must be an integer from 1 to 20')
    result = {'status': 'failed', 'provider': 'openalex', 'query': query, 'limit': limit,
              'started_at': now(), 'records': [], 'errors': [], 'page_limit': 1,
              'coverage': 'bounded no-key first page; not exhaustive', 'exhausted': False,
              'full_text_retrieved': False, 'read_or_reviewed': False,
              'semantic_verification': 'not_performed', 'study_independence': 'not_assessed'}
    fetched = request(OPENALEX + '?' + urllib.parse.urlencode({'search': query, 'per_page': limit}))
    result['receipt'] = fetched['receipt']
    if fetched['status'] != 'success':
        return {**result, 'status': fetched['status'], 'error': fetched.get('error')}
    try:
        payload = json.loads(fetched['body'])
        items = payload['results']
        if not isinstance(items, list) or len(items) > limit:
            raise ValueError('Invalid or oversized OpenAlex results')
        meta = payload.get('meta')
        if 'meta' in payload and not isinstance(meta, dict):
            raise ValueError('OpenAlex meta must be an object when supplied')
        count = meta.get('count') if meta is not None else None
        if meta is not None and 'count' in meta:
            if isinstance(count, bool) or not isinstance(count, int) or count < 0:
                raise ValueError('OpenAlex meta.count must be a nonnegative integer')
            if count < len(items) or count > 0 and not items:
                raise ValueError('OpenAlex meta.count conflicts with first-page results')
        result['provider_meta'] = meta
        result['provider_hit_count'] = count
        for index, raw in enumerate(items):
            try:
                result['records'].append(_record(raw))
            except (ValueError, TypeError, KeyError) as exc:
                result['errors'].append({'index': index, 'error': str(exc)})
        result['status'] = ('partial' if result['records'] else 'failed') if result['errors'] else (
            'success' if result['records'] else 'empty')
    except (ValueError, TypeError, KeyError) as exc:
        result['error'] = 'invalid_provider_response: ' + str(exc)
    result['finished_at'] = now()
    return result


def work(work_id=None, doi=None):
    if (work_id is None) == (doi is None):
        raise ValueError('Supply exactly one work ID or DOI')
    wid = globals()['work_id'](work_id) if work_id is not None else None
    normalized = normalize_doi(doi) if doi is not None else None
    suffix = wid or urllib.parse.quote('https://doi.org/' + normalized, safe='')
    fetched = request(OPENALEX + '/' + suffix)
    result = {'status': fetched['status'], 'provider': 'openalex', 'requested_work_id': wid,
              'requested_doi': normalized, 'receipt': fetched['receipt'],
              'identity_status': 'unresolved', 'full_text_retrieved': False,
              'read_or_reviewed': False, 'semantic_verification': 'not_performed',
              'coverage': 'one exact metadata lookup; references remain citation leads'}
    if fetched['status'] != 'success':
        return {**result, 'error': fetched.get('error')}
    try:
        record = _record(json.loads(fetched['body']))
        if wid is not None and record['work_id'] != wid or normalized is not None and record['doi'] != normalized:
            raise ValueError('Returned work identity does not match request')
        return {**result, 'record': record, 'identity_status': 'identity_verified'}
    except (ValueError, TypeError, KeyError) as exc:
        return {**result, 'status': 'failed', 'error': str(exc)}


class _Node:
    def __init__(self, tag, attrs, parent, order):
        self.tag, self.attrs, self.parent, self.order = tag, dict(attrs), parent, order
        self.children, self.closed = [], False

    def classes(self):
        return set(self.attrs.get('class', '').split())


_VOID = {'area', 'base', 'br', 'col', 'embed', 'hr', 'img', 'input', 'link', 'meta', 'param', 'source', 'track', 'wbr'}
_SKIP = {'script', 'style', 'nav', 'header', 'footer', 'noscript', 'iframe', 'object', 'embed', 'svg', 'canvas', 'form', 'button', 'template'}
_BLOCK = {'article', 'section', 'div', 'p', 'h1', 'h2', 'h3', 'h4', 'h5', 'h6', 'li', 'table', 'tr', 'blockquote', 'br'}


class _HTML(HTMLParser):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(convert_charrefs=True)
        self.root = _Node('root', [], None, 0)
        self.stack, self.nodes = [self.root], []

    def handle_starttag(self, tag, attrs):
        if len(self.nodes) >= 200000 or len(self.stack) > 512:
            raise ValueError('HTML structure limit exceeded')
        normalized = {}
        for key, value in attrs:
            if key in normalized:
                # Some official HTML duplicates presentation-only tooltips.
                # First title wins; identity, visibility and URL duplicates fail.
                if key == 'title':
                    continue
                raise ValueError('Duplicate HTML attributes')
            normalized[key] = value or ''
        node = _Node(tag, normalized.items(), self.stack[-1], len(self.nodes) + 1)
        self.nodes.append(node)
        self.stack[-1].children.append(node)
        if tag not in _VOID:
            self.stack.append(node)
        else:
            node.closed = True

    def handle_startendtag(self, tag, attrs):
        self.handle_starttag(tag, attrs)
        if tag not in _VOID:
            self.handle_endtag(tag)

    def handle_endtag(self, tag):
        for index in range(len(self.stack) - 1, 0, -1):
            if self.stack[index].tag == tag:
                self.stack[index].closed = True
                del self.stack[index:]
                break

    def handle_data(self, data):
        self.stack[-1].children.append(data)


def _ancestors(node):
    while node.parent is not None:
        node = node.parent
        yield node


def _skip(node):
    return node.tag in _SKIP or 'ltx_page_logo' in node.classes() or node.attrs.get('aria-hidden') == 'true' or 'hidden' in node.attrs


def _plain(node):
    if _skip(node):
        return ''
    return ' '.join(c if isinstance(c, str) else _plain(c) for c in node.children)


def _extract(article):
    pieces, spans, size = [], [], 0

    def append(value):
        nonlocal size
        pieces.append(value)
        size += len(value)

    def newline():
        if pieces and not pieces[-1].endswith('\n'):
            append('\n')

    def visit(node):
        if _skip(node):
            return
        if node.tag in _BLOCK:
            newline()
        start = size
        for child in node.children:
            if isinstance(child, str):
                value = re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', child).strip()
                if value:
                    if pieces and not pieces[-1].endswith(('\n', ' ')):
                        append(' ')
                    append(value)
            else:
                visit(child)
        end = size
        if node.tag == 'section':
            spans.append((node, start, end))
        if node.tag in _BLOCK:
            newline()

    visit(article)
    text = ''.join(pieces).rstrip() + '\n'
    sections = []
    for number, (node, start, end) in enumerate(sorted(spans, key=lambda x: x[1]), 1):
        while start < end and text[start].isspace():
            start += 1
        while end > start and text[end - 1].isspace():
            end -= 1
        if start == end:
            continue
        title_node = next((n for n in node.children if isinstance(n, _Node) and n.tag in {'h1', 'h2', 'h3', 'h4', 'h5', 'h6'}), None)
        sections.append({'locator': 'article/section[' + str(number) + ']', 'id': node.attrs.get('id'),
                         'title': re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', _plain(title_node)).strip() if title_node else None,
                         'start': start, 'end': end, 'text': text[start:end]})
    return text, sections


def parse_arxiv_html(raw_bytes, identifier):
    identifier = arxiv_id(identifier)
    result = {'status': 'failed', 'provider': 'arxiv', 'arxiv_id': identifier,
              'version': identifier.rsplit('v', 1)[1], 'version_identifier': 'arxiv:' + identifier,
              'access': 'unavailable', 'full_text_retrieved': False, 'read_or_reviewed': False,
              'identity_status': 'unresolved', 'license': None, 'reuse_rights': 'unknown; article-specific',
              'semantic_verification': 'not_performed',
              'conversion_limits': 'HTML conversion may omit or alter equations, tables and other content'}
    try:
        if not isinstance(raw_bytes, bytes) or len(raw_bytes) > MAX_BYTES:
            raise ValueError('HTML must be bytes within the 10 MiB limit')
        result['raw_sha256'] = _sha(raw_bytes)
        parser = _HTML()
        parser.feed(raw_bytes.decode('utf-8-sig'))
        parser.close()
        articles = [n for n in parser.nodes if n.tag == 'article' and 'ltx_document' in n.classes()]
        if len(articles) != 1 or not articles[0].closed:
            raise ValueError('One complete article.ltx_document body is required')
        article = articles[0]
        signals = []
        for node in parser.nodes:
            ancestors = list(_ancestors(node))
            if node.order >= article.order or article in ancestors or any(_skip(n) and n.tag != 'header' for n in ancestors):
                continue
            if node.tag == 'div' and node.attrs.get('id') == 'watermark-tr':
                marker = _plain(node)
                found = re.findall(r'arXiv:\s*(' + _ARXIV_PATTERN + r')(?![\w./])', marker, flags=re.I)
                signals.extend({'kind': 'watermark-tr', 'id': x} for x in found)
            if node.tag == 'a' and 'header-button' in node.classes():
                href = urllib.parse.urlsplit(node.attrs.get('href', ''))
                if href.scheme not in ('', 'https') or href.netloc not in ('', 'arxiv.org') or href.query or href.fragment:
                    continue
                match = re.fullmatch(r'/(?:abs|pdf)/(' + _ARXIV_PATTERN + r')(?:\.pdf)?', href.path)
                if match:
                    signals.append({'kind': 'header-button', 'id': match.group(1)})
        result['identity_signals'] = signals
        if not signals or {s['id'] for s in signals} != {identifier}:
            raise ValueError('Missing, mismatched or conflicting structural article identity/version')
        substantive = []
        for node in parser.nodes:
            if node.tag != 'p' or article not in list(_ancestors(node)):
                continue
            chain = [node, *list(_ancestors(node))]
            if any(_skip(n) or {'ltx_abstract', 'ltx_bibliography'} & n.classes() or n.attrs.get('id') == 'abstract' for n in chain):
                continue
            substantive.append(_plain(node))
        prose = re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', ' '.join(substantive)).strip()
        if len(prose) < 80 or len(prose.split()) < 12:
            raise ValueError('Substantive article prose unavailable; metadata/abstract is not full text')
        text, sections = _extract(article)
        return {**result, 'status': 'success', 'identity_status': 'identity_verified',
                'access': 'full_text', 'full_text_retrieved': True, 'text': text,
                'text_sha256': _sha(text.encode('utf-8')), 'sections': sections,
                'offset_unit': 'Unicode code points in LF-normalized extracted text'}
    except (ValueError, TypeError, UnicodeError, RecursionError) as exc:
        return {**result, 'error': str(exc)}


def arxiv_fulltext(identifier):
    identifier = arxiv_id(identifier)
    fetched = request(ARXIV + identifier)
    if fetched['status'] != 'success':
        return {'status': fetched['status'], 'provider': 'arxiv', 'arxiv_id': identifier,
                'access': 'unavailable', 'identity_status': 'unresolved', 'full_text_retrieved': False,
                'read_or_reviewed': False, 'error': fetched.get('error'), 'receipt': fetched['receipt'],
                'license': None, 'reuse_rights': 'unknown; article-specific'}
    return {**parse_arxiv_html(fetched['body'], identifier), 'receipt': fetched['receipt']}


def _preflight(paths):
    resolved = [os.path.normcase(str(p.resolve())) for p in paths]
    if len(set(resolved)) != len(paths):
        raise ValueError('Text and report outputs must differ')
    for path in paths:
        if os.path.lexists(path):
            raise FileExistsError('Existing output preserved; choose a new path')
        if not path.parent.is_dir():
            raise ValueError('Output parent directory must already exist')
        if not os.access(path.parent, os.W_OK):
            raise PermissionError('Output parent is not writable')


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    subs = parser.add_subparsers(dest='command', required=True)
    for name in ('search', 'work', 'arxiv-fulltext'):
        command = subs.add_parser(name)
        command.add_argument('--output', required=True)
        if name == 'search':
            command.add_argument('--query', required=True)
            command.add_argument('--limit', type=int, default=10)
        elif name == 'work':
            identity = command.add_mutually_exclusive_group(required=True)
            identity.add_argument('--work-id')
            identity.add_argument('--doi')
        else:
            command.add_argument('--arxiv-id', required=True)
            command.add_argument('--text-output', required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    created = []
    try:
        output = Path(args.output)
        paths = [output] + ([Path(args.text_output)] if args.command == 'arxiv-fulltext' else [])
        _preflight(paths)
        if args.command == 'search':
            result = search(args.query, args.limit)
        elif args.command == 'work':
            result = work(args.work_id, args.doi)
        else:
            result = arxiv_fulltext(args.arxiv_id)
            if result['status'] == 'success':
                text_path = paths[1]
                with text_path.open('x', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n') as stream:
                    created.append(text_path)
                    stream.write(result.pop('text'))
                result['text_path'] = str(text_path.resolve())
        with output.open('x', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n') as stream:
            created.append(output)
            json.dump(result, stream, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, allow_nan=False)
            stream.write('\n')
        print(json.dumps({'status': result['status'], 'output': str(output)}))
        return 0 if result['status'] in ('success', 'empty') else 2
    except (ValueError, TypeError, OSError) as exc:
        for path in reversed(created):
            try:
                path.unlink()
            except OSError:
                pass
        print(json.dumps({'status': 'failed', 'error': str(exc)}), file=sys.stderr)
        return 2


if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
"""Original MIT-licensed research record tools; Python standard library only.

Integrity checks establish recorded identity and consistency, never semantic
entailment or scientific correctness. Network access occurs only in explicit
search and lookup-doi commands. Input files and HTTP bodies are capped at 10 MiB.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import hashlib
import io
import json
import os
import re
import sys
import tempfile
import urllib.error
import urllib.parse
import urllib.request
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from decimal import Decimal, InvalidOperation
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

MAX_BYTES = 10 * 1024 * 1024
CONTEXT = ("run", "condition", "dataset", "metric", "unit", "seed", "statistic", "value")
ACCESS = {"metadata": 0, "abstract": 1, "full_text": 2}


class ToolError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


class ToolArgumentParser(argparse.ArgumentParser):
    def error(self, message):
        print(json.dumps({"status": "failed", "error": {"code": "invalid_arguments",
                                                       "message": message}}, sort_keys=True), file=sys.stderr)
        raise SystemExit(2)


def _error(code: str, record: str, detail: str) -> dict:
    return {"code": code, "record": record, "detail": detail}


def _unique_object(pairs):
    result = {}
    for key, value in pairs:
        if key in result:
            raise ToolError("duplicate_json_key", f"Duplicate JSON key: {key}")
        result[key] = value
    return result


def _read_bytes(path: Path) -> bytes:
    with path.open("rb") as stream:
        data = stream.read(MAX_BYTES + 1)
    if len(data) > MAX_BYTES:
        raise ToolError("input_too_large", "Input exceeds 10 MiB")
    return data


def read_json(path: str | Path) -> Any:
    try:
        return json.loads(_read_bytes(Path(path)).decode("utf-8"),
                          object_pairs_hook=_unique_object, parse_float=Decimal,
                          parse_constant=lambda value: (_ for _ in ()).throw(
                              ToolError("nonfinite_json", f"Invalid JSON number: {value}")))
    except (ValueError, UnicodeError) as exc:
        raise ToolError("invalid_json", str(exc)) from exc


def write_json(path: str | Path, value: Any, force: bool = False) -> None:
    target = Path(path)
    payload = json.dumps(value, indent=2, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False,
                         allow_nan=False, default=str) + "\n"
    if not force:
        # Exclusive creation also protects against a competing writer.
        with target.open("x", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
            stream.write(payload)
        return
    descriptor, temporary = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=".research-record-", dir=target.parent)
    try:
        with os.fdopen(descriptor, "w", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
            stream.write(payload)
        os.replace(temporary, target)
    finally:
        if os.path.exists(temporary):
            os.unlink(temporary)


def init_project(directory: str | Path) -> dict:
    root = Path(directory)
    root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    manifest = root / "research-project.json"
    if manifest.exists():
        return {"status": "preserved", "path": str(manifest)}
    try:
        write_json(manifest, {"schema_version": 1, "target": None, "destination": None,
                              "scope": None, "decisions": [], "budgets": {}, "records": {}})
    except FileExistsError:
        return {"status": "preserved", "path": str(manifest)}
    return {"status": "created", "path": str(manifest)}


def normalize_identifier(value: Any, kind: str | None = None) -> dict:
    """Normalize DOI/arXiv/PMID identity, retaining arXiv version separately."""
    original = value
    result = {"original": original, "kind": kind, "normalized": None, "valid": False}
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
        return {**result, "error": "Identifier must be a nonempty string"}
    text = value.strip()
    if kind:
        kind = kind.lower()
    if not kind:
        if re.match(r"(?i)^(?:doi:|https?://(?:dx\.)?doi\.org/|10\.)", text):
            kind = "doi"
        elif re.match(r"(?i)^(?:arxiv:|https?://(?:www\.)?arxiv\.org/|\d{4}\.\d{4,5}|[a-z-]+(?:\.[a-z-]+)?/\d{7})", text):
            kind = "arxiv"
        elif re.match(r"(?i)^(?:pmid:|https?://pubmed\.ncbi\.nlm\.nih\.gov/|\d+$)", text):
            kind = "pmid"
    result["kind"] = kind
    if kind == "doi":
        text = re.sub(r"(?i)^doi:\s*", "", text)
        if re.match(r"(?i)^https?://(?:dx\.)?doi\.org/", text):
            text = urllib.parse.unquote(urllib.parse.urlsplit(text).path.lstrip("/"))
        text = text.lower()
        if re.fullmatch(r'10\.\d{4,9}/[^\s<>"{}]+', text):
            return {**result, "normalized": text, "valid": True}
    elif kind == "arxiv":
        text = re.sub(r"(?i)^arxiv:\s*", "", text)
        if re.match(r"(?i)^https?://(?:www\.)?arxiv\.org/", text):
            text = urllib.parse.urlsplit(text).path
            text = re.sub(r"^/(?:abs|pdf|html)/", "", text)
        text = re.sub(r"(?i)\.pdf$", "", text).lower()
        match = re.fullmatch(r"(\d{4}\.\d{4,5}|[a-z-]+(?:\.[a-z-]+)?/\d{7})(v[1-9]\d*)?", text)
        if match:
            core = match.group(1)
            # Modern IDs contain a valid calendar month.
            if "." not in core[:5] or 1 <= int(core[2:4]) <= 12:
                return {**result, "normalized": core, "version": match.group(2), "valid": True}
    elif kind == "pmid":
        text = re.sub(r"(?i)^pmid:\s*", "", text)
        if re.match(r"(?i)^https?://pubmed\.ncbi\.nlm\.nih\.gov/", text):
            text = urllib.parse.urlsplit(text).path.strip("/")
        if re.fullmatch(r"\d{1,12}", text) and int(text) > 0:
            return {**result, "normalized": str(int(text)), "valid": True}
    return {**result, "error": "Invalid or unsupported identifier"}


def normalize_records(records: Any) -> dict:
    """Deduplicate by shared canonical identity, retaining every original record."""
    if not isinstance(records, list) or not all(isinstance(r, dict) for r in records):
        raise ToolError("invalid_bibliography", "Bibliography input must be a list of objects")
    groups, errors = [], []
    for index, record in enumerate(records):
        identifiers = []
        for kind in ("doi", "arxiv", "pmid"):
            if kind not in record:
                continue
            values = record[kind] if isinstance(record[kind], list) else [record[kind]]
            for value in values:
                normalized = normalize_identifier(value, kind)
                identifiers.append(normalized)
                if not normalized["valid"]:
                    errors.append(_error("invalid_identifier", str(index), f"Invalid {kind} identifier"))
        if not identifiers:
            errors.append(_error("missing_identifier", str(index), "No DOI, arXiv ID or PMID supplied"))
        keys = {(i["kind"], i["normalized"]) for i in identifiers if i["valid"]}
        matches = [g for g in groups if g["keys"] & keys]
        if matches:
            group = matches[0]
            for other in matches[1:]:
                group["keys"] |= other["keys"]
                group["identifiers"] += other["identifiers"]
                group["provenance"] += other["provenance"]
                groups.remove(other)
        else:
            group = {"keys": set(), "identifiers": [], "provenance": []}
            groups.append(group)
        group["keys"] |= keys
        group["identifiers"] += identifiers
        group["provenance"].append({"input_index": index, "record": record})
    output, conflicts = [], []
    for index, group in enumerate(groups):
        canonical = {kind: sorted(value for k, value in group["keys"] if k == kind)
                     for kind in ("doi", "arxiv", "pmid")}
        for kind, values in canonical.items():
            if len(values) > 1:
                conflicts.append(_error("identity_conflict", str(index), f"Conflicting {kind} identities: {values}"))
        for field in ("title", "year", "authors"):
            variants = {json.dumps(p["record"][field], sort_keys=True, default=str)
                        for p in group["provenance"] if field in p["record"]}
            if len(variants) > 1:
                conflicts.append(_error("metadata_conflict", str(index), f"Different {field} values retained"))
        output.append({"canonical": canonical, "identifier_variants": group["identifiers"],
                       "provenance": sorted(group["provenance"], key=lambda p: p["input_index"])})
    return {"status": "failed" if errors or conflicts else "success", "records": output,
            "errors": errors, "conflicts": conflicts, "semantic_verification": "not_performed"}


def _public_crossref(url: str) -> dict:
    retrieved = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
    base = {"request_url": url, "retrieved_at": retrieved, "access": "metadata_only",
            "identity_only": True, "semantic_verification": "not_performed", "results": []}
    request = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": "research-writer-evidence-tools/1.0",
                                                   "Accept": "application/json"})
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=10) as response:
            data = response.read(MAX_BYTES + 1)
        if len(data) > MAX_BYTES:
            return {**base, "status": "failed", "error": "response_too_large"}
        payload = json.loads(data)
        if not isinstance(payload, dict) or not isinstance(payload.get("message"), dict):
            return {**base, "status": "failed", "error": "invalid_provider_response"}
        message = payload["message"]
        items = message.get("items") if "items" in message else [message]
        if not isinstance(items, list) or not all(isinstance(i, dict) for i in items):
            return {**base, "status": "failed", "error": "invalid_provider_results"}
        results = []
        for item in items:
            normalized = normalize_identifier(item.get("DOI"), "doi")
            if not normalized["valid"]:
                return {**base, "status": "failed", "error": "invalid_provider_record"}
            results.append({"doi": normalized["normalized"], "title": item.get("title", []),
                            "authors": item.get("author", []), "published": item.get("published"),
                            "publisher": item.get("publisher"), "type": item.get("type"),
                            "url": item.get("URL"), "metadata_url": "https://api.crossref.org/works/" +
                            urllib.parse.quote(normalized["normalized"] or "", safe=""),
                            "access": "metadata_only", "retrieved_at": retrieved})
        return {**base, "status": "success" if results else "empty", "results": results}
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
        status = "empty" if exc.code == 404 else "unavailable" if exc.code == 429 or exc.code >= 500 else "failed"
        return {**base, "status": status, "error": "http_error", "http_status": exc.code}
    except (urllib.error.URLError, TimeoutError, OSError):
        return {**base, "status": "unavailable", "error": "network_unavailable"}
    except (ValueError, UnicodeError, TypeError):
        return {**base, "status": "failed", "error": "invalid_provider_response"}


def search_crossref(query: str, limit: int = 5) -> dict:
    if not isinstance(query, str) or not query.strip() or len(query) > 1000:
        raise ToolError("invalid_query", "Query must contain 1–1000 characters")
    if isinstance(limit, bool) or not isinstance(limit, int) or not 1 <= limit <= 20:
        raise ToolError("invalid_limit", "Limit must be between 1 and 20")
    parameters = urllib.parse.urlencode({"query": query.strip(), "rows": limit})
    return _public_crossref("https://api.crossref.org/works?" + parameters)


def lookup_doi(doi: str) -> dict:
    normalized = normalize_identifier(doi, "doi")
    if not normalized["valid"]:
        raise ToolError("invalid_doi", "DOI is invalid")
    result = _public_crossref("https://api.crossref.org/works/" + urllib.parse.quote(normalized["normalized"], safe=""))
    result["requested_doi"] = normalized["normalized"]
    if result["status"] == "success":
        matches = [r for r in result["results"] if r["doi"] == normalized["normalized"]]
        result["identity_status"] = "identity_verified" if matches else "mismatch"
        if not matches:
            result["status"] = "failed"
            result["error"] = "doi_identity_mismatch"
    else:
        result["identity_status"] = "unresolved"
    return result


def _decimal(value: Any) -> Decimal:
    if isinstance(value, bool) or value is None or isinstance(value, (dict, list)):
        raise ValueError("Value must be a finite decimal number")
    try:
        number = Decimal(str(value))
    except (InvalidOperation, ValueError) as exc:
        raise ValueError("Value must be a finite decimal number") from exc
    if not number.is_finite():
        raise ValueError("Value must be finite")
    return number


def verify_evidence(manifest: Any, base_directory: str | Path = ".") -> dict:
    """Verify hashes and exact context-linked CSV values; no network or writes."""
    errors = []
    report = {"status": "fail", "passed": False, "errors": errors,
              "semantic_entailment": "not_checked", "scientific_validity": "not_checked",
              "scope": "artifact hashes, CSV row/context identity, source access and locators"}
    if not isinstance(manifest, dict):
        errors.append(_error("invalid_manifest", "manifest", "Manifest must be an object"))
        return report
    root = Path(base_directory).resolve()
    indexes = {}
    for category in ("artifacts", "measurements", "claims", "sources", "source_claims"):
        records = manifest.get(category, [])
        if not isinstance(records, list):
            errors.append(_error("invalid_records", category, "Expected list"))
            records = []
        index = {}
        for position, record in enumerate(records):
            label = f"{category}[{position}]"
            if not isinstance(record, dict) or not isinstance(record.get("id"), str) or not record["id"].strip():
                errors.append(_error("missing_id", label, "Nonempty string ID required"))
                continue
            if record["id"] in index:
                errors.append(_error("duplicate_id", label, record["id"]))
                continue
            index[record["id"]] = record
        indexes[category] = index
    if not indexes["claims"] and not indexes["source_claims"]:
        report["status"] = "not_checked"
        report["reason"] = "no_claims_to_check"
        report["counts"] = {key: len(value) for key, value in indexes.items()}
        return report
    tables = {}
    for identity, artifact in indexes["artifacts"].items():
        label = f"artifact:{identity}"
        try:
            relative = artifact.get("path")
            if not isinstance(relative, str) or not relative or Path(relative).is_absolute() or ".." in Path(relative).parts:
                raise ToolError("unsafe_artifact_path", "Relative artifact path without traversal required")
            path = (root / relative).resolve()
            if not path.is_relative_to(root):
                raise ToolError("unsafe_artifact_path", "Artifact resolves outside manifest directory")
            expected = artifact.get("sha256")
            if not isinstance(expected, str) or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-fA-F]{64}", expected):
                raise ToolError("invalid_hash", "SHA-256 hex digest required")
            raw = _read_bytes(path)
            if hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest() != expected.lower():
                raise ToolError("hash_mismatch", "Artifact content differs from recorded SHA-256")
            reader = csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(raw.decode("utf-8-sig"), newline=""))
            header = reader.fieldnames or []
            if len(header) != len(set(header)) or any(k not in header for k in CONTEXT):
                raise ToolError("invalid_csv_header", "CSV needs unique headers including all context fields")
            rows = list(reader)
            if any(None in row or any(row[k] is None for k in CONTEXT) for row in rows):
                raise ToolError("invalid_csv_row", "CSV has missing or excess cells")
            tables[identity] = rows
        except (OSError, UnicodeError, csv.Error, ToolError) as exc:
            errors.append(_error(getattr(exc, "code", "artifact_unreadable"), label, str(exc)))
    valid_measurements = set()
    def valid_context(record, label):
        good = True
        for key in CONTEXT:
            if key == "value":
                try:
                    _decimal(record.get(key))
                except ValueError as exc:
                    errors.append(_error("invalid_value", label, str(exc)))
                    good = False
            elif not isinstance(record.get(key), str) or not record[key].strip():
                errors.append(_error("missing_context", label, f"Nonempty string required: {key}"))
                good = False
        return good
    def compare(left, right, label):
        good = True
        for key in CONTEXT:
            try:
                same = _decimal(left.get(key)) == _decimal(right.get(key)) if key == "value" else left.get(key) == right.get(key)
            except ValueError:
                same = False
            if not same:
                errors.append(_error("context_mismatch", label, f"Mismatch in {key}"))
                good = False
        return good
    for identity, measurement in indexes["measurements"].items():
        label = f"measurement:{identity}"
        good = valid_context(measurement, label)
        aid = measurement.get("artifact_id")
        if not isinstance(aid, str) or aid not in indexes["artifacts"]:
            errors.append(_error("missing_artifact", label, "Artifact ID does not resolve"))
            continue
        if aid not in tables:
            errors.append(_error("unverified_artifact", label, "Artifact failed hash or CSV checks"))
            continue
        row = measurement.get("row")
        if isinstance(row, bool) or not isinstance(row, int) or not 1 <= row <= len(tables[aid]):
            errors.append(_error("invalid_row", label, "Row must be a 1-based CSV data-row index"))
            continue
        if compare(measurement, tables[aid][row - 1], label) and good:
            valid_measurements.add(identity)
    for identity, claim in indexes["claims"].items():
        label = f"claim:{identity}"
        valid_context(claim, label)
        eid = claim.get("evidence_id")
        if not isinstance(eid, str) or eid not in indexes["measurements"]:
            errors.append(_error("missing_evidence", label, "Evidence ID does not resolve"))
        else:
            compare(claim, indexes["measurements"][eid], label)
            if eid not in valid_measurements:
                errors.append(_error("unverified_evidence", label, "Linked measurement failed verification"))
    for identity, source in indexes["sources"].items():
        if not isinstance(source.get("access"), str) or source["access"] not in ACCESS:
            errors.append(_error("invalid_access", f"source:{identity}", "Access must be metadata, abstract or full_text"))
        if not isinstance(source.get("identity_status"), str) or not source["identity_status"].strip():
            errors.append(_error("missing_identity_status", f"source:{identity}", "Identity status required"))
        if not isinstance(source.get("locator"), str) or not source["locator"].strip():
            errors.append(_error("missing_locator", f"source:{identity}", "Source locator required"))
    for identity, claim in indexes["source_claims"].items():
        label = f"source_claim:{identity}"
        sid = claim.get("source_id")
        source = indexes["sources"].get(sid) if isinstance(sid, str) else None
        if source is None:
            errors.append(_error("missing_source", label, "Source ID does not resolve"))
            continue
        required = claim.get("required_access")
        if not isinstance(required, str) or required not in ACCESS:
            errors.append(_error("invalid_required_access", label, "Required access must be metadata, abstract or full_text"))
        elif not isinstance(source.get("access"), str) or ACCESS.get(source["access"], -1) < ACCESS[required]:
            errors.append(_error("insufficient_source_access", label, "Recorded source access is below claim requirement"))
        if source.get("identity_status") not in ("verified", "identity_verified"):
            errors.append(_error("unverified_source_identity", label, "Source identity has not been verified"))
        for record, name in ((source, "source"), (claim, "claim")):
            if not isinstance(record.get("locator"), str) or not record["locator"].strip():
                errors.append(_error("missing_locator", label, f"{name} requires a source locator"))
    report["counts"] = {key: len(value) for key, value in indexes.items()}
    report["passed"] = not errors
    report["status"] = "pass" if not errors else "fail"
    return report


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    parser = ToolArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    commands = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    initialize = commands.add_parser("init-project")
    initialize.add_argument("--directory", required=True)
    for command in ("bibliography", "verify-evidence", "search", "lookup-doi"):
        sub = commands.add_parser(command)
        sub.add_argument("--output", required=True)
        sub.add_argument("--force", action="store_true")
        if command in ("bibliography", "verify-evidence"):
            sub.add_argument("--input", required=True)
        if command == "search":
            sub.add_argument("--query", required=True)
            sub.add_argument("--limit", type=int, default=5)
        if command == "lookup-doi":
            sub.add_argument("--doi", required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        if args.command == "init-project":
            print(json.dumps(init_project(args.directory), sort_keys=True))
            return 0
        if Path(args.output).exists() and not args.force:
            raise ToolError("output_exists", "Output already exists; use --force to replace it")
        if args.command == "bibliography":
            result = normalize_records(read_json(args.input))
        elif args.command == "verify-evidence":
            result = verify_evidence(read_json(args.input), Path(args.input).resolve().parent)
        elif args.command == "search":
            result = search_crossref(args.query, args.limit)
        else:
            result = lookup_doi(args.doi)
        write_json(args.output, result, args.force)
        return 0 if result["status"] in ("success", "empty", "pass") else 2
    except (ToolError, OSError, ValueError, TypeError, RecursionError) as exc:
        print(json.dumps({"status": "failed", "error": {"code": getattr(exc, "code", "io_or_input_error"),
                                                       "message": str(exc)}}, sort_keys=True), file=sys.stderr)
        return 2


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
```
