## Included resource: scripts/literature_tools.py

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
"""Bounded public literature retrieval. Retrieval is never semantic verification."""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
import sys
import urllib.error
import urllib.parse
import urllib.request
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from datetime import datetime, timezone

MAX_BYTES = 10 * 1024 * 1024
BASES = {'crossref': 'https://api.crossref.org/v1/works',
         'europepmc': 'https://www.ebi.ac.uk/europepmc/webservices/rest/'}


def now():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


def doi(value):
    if not isinstance(value, str):
        raise ValueError('DOI must be a string')
    value = re.sub(r'(?i)^doi:\s*', '', value.strip())
    if re.match(r'(?i)^https?://(?:dx\.)?doi\.org/', value):
        value = urllib.parse.unquote(urllib.parse.urlsplit(value).path.lstrip('/'))
    if not re.fullmatch(r'10\.\d{4,9}/[^\s<>"{}]+', value):
        raise ValueError('Invalid DOI')
    return value.lower()


def pmcid(value):
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not re.fullmatch(r'PMC[1-9]\d*', value.upper()):
        raise ValueError('PMCID must be PMC followed by a positive integer')
    return value.upper()


def allowed_url(url):
    p = urllib.parse.urlsplit(url)
    return (p.scheme == 'https' and not p.username and not p.password and
            p.port in (None, 443) and
            ((p.hostname == 'api.crossref.org' and (p.path == '/v1/works' or p.path.startswith('/v1/works/'))) or
             (p.hostname == 'www.ebi.ac.uk' and p.path.startswith('/europepmc/webservices/rest/'))))


class RestrictedRedirect(urllib.request.HTTPRedirectHandler):
    def redirect_request(self, req, fp, code, msg, headers, newurl):
        if not allowed_url(newurl) or urllib.parse.urlsplit(req.full_url).hostname != urllib.parse.urlsplit(newurl).hostname:
            raise urllib.error.URLError('Redirect outside fixed provider endpoint blocked')
        return super().redirect_request(req, fp, code, msg, headers, newurl)


def request(url):
    if not allowed_url(url):
        raise ValueError('Only fixed public provider endpoints are permitted')
    receipt = {'url': url, 'retrieved_at': now(), 'timeout_seconds': 10, 'max_bytes': MAX_BYTES}
    req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={'User-Agent': 'research-writer-literature-tools/0.6.2',
                                               'Accept': 'application/json, application/xml;q=0.9'})
    try:
        opener = urllib.request.build_opener(RestrictedRedirect())
        with opener.open(req, timeout=10) as response:
            raw = response.read(MAX_BYTES + 1)
            receipt['http_status'] = response.status
        if len(raw) > MAX_BYTES:
            return {'status': 'failed', 'error': 'response_too_large', 'receipt': receipt}
        receipt['sha256'] = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
        return {'status': 'success', 'body': raw, 'receipt': receipt}
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
        receipt.update(http_status=exc.code, retry_after=exc.headers.get('Retry-After') if exc.headers else None)
        return {'status': 'unavailable' if exc.code in (404, 429) or exc.code >= 500 else 'failed',
                'error': 'http_error', 'receipt': receipt}
    except (urllib.error.URLError, TimeoutError, OSError) as exc:
        return {'status': 'unavailable', 'error': type(exc).__name__, 'receipt': receipt}


def _record(provider, raw):
    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
        raise ValueError('Provider record must be an object')
    if provider == 'crossref':
        identifier = doi(raw.get('DOI'))
        return {'identity': 'doi:' + identifier, 'doi': identifier,
                'title': raw.get('title'), 'version': raw.get('version'),
                'access': 'metadata', 'full_text_retrieved': False,
                'update_to': raw.get('update-to', []), 'relation': raw.get('relation', {}),
                'raw': raw}
    identity, source = raw.get('id'), raw.get('source')
    if not isinstance(identity, str) or not re.fullmatch(r'[A-Za-z0-9._-]+', identity) or not isinstance(source, str) or not re.fullmatch(r'[A-Z][A-Z0-9]*', source):
        raise ValueError('Invalid Europe PMC identity')
    if source == 'MED' and not re.fullmatch(r'[1-9]\d*', identity):
        raise ValueError('Invalid PMID')
    if source == 'PMC':
        pmcid(identity)
    normalized_doi = doi(raw['doi']) if raw.get('doi') else None
    if raw.get('pmcid'):
        pmcid(raw['pmcid'])
    return {'identity': 'doi:' + normalized_doi if normalized_doi else source + ':' + identity,
            'doi': normalized_doi, 'source': source, 'id': identity,
            'pmcid': raw.get('pmcid'), 'title': raw.get('title'),
            'version': raw.get('versionNumber'), 'access': 'abstract' if raw.get('abstractText') else 'metadata',
            'full_text_retrieved': False, 'license': raw.get('license'),
            'full_text_links': raw.get('fullTextUrlList'), 'raw': raw}


def search(provider, query, pages=1, page_size=10, filters=None):
    if provider not in BASES or not isinstance(query, str) or not query.strip() or len(query) > 1000:
        raise ValueError('Select crossref/europepmc and a query of 1–1000 characters')
    if isinstance(pages, bool) or not isinstance(pages, int) or not 1 <= pages <= 5 or isinstance(page_size, bool) or not isinstance(page_size, int) or not 1 <= page_size <= 20:
        raise ValueError('Pages must be 1–5 and page-size 1–20')
    if filters is not None and (not isinstance(filters, str) or not filters.strip() or len(filters) > 1000):
        raise ValueError('Filter must be 1–1000 characters')
    if provider == 'europepmc' and filters is not None:
        raise ValueError('Europe PMC filters belong in its explicit query syntax')
    result = {'status': 'empty', 'provider': provider, 'query': query, 'filter': filters,
              'started_at': now(), 'page_limit': pages, 'page_size': page_size,
              'pages': [], 'records': [], 'errors': [], 'coverage': 'bounded search; not field-exhaustive',
              'exhausted': False, 'full_text_retrieved': False, 'semantic_verification': 'not_performed',
              'evidence_families': 'not_assessed; identity deduplication is not study independence'}
    cursor, seen = '*', set()
    groups = {}
    for page in range(1, pages + 1):
        params = {'query': query, 'rows': page_size, 'cursor': cursor} if provider == 'crossref' else {
            'query': query, 'format': 'json', 'resultType': 'core', 'pageSize': page_size, 'cursorMark': cursor}
        if filters:
            params['filter'] = filters
        url = (BASES[provider] if provider == 'crossref' else BASES[provider] + 'search') + '?' + urllib.parse.urlencode(params)
        fetched = request(url)
        receipt = {**fetched['receipt'], 'page': page, 'cursor': cursor, 'status': fetched['status']}
        result['pages'].append(receipt)
        if fetched['status'] != 'success':
            result['errors'].append({'page': page, 'error': fetched.get('error'), 'status': fetched['status']})
            result['status'] = 'partial' if groups else fetched['status']
            break
        try:
            payload = json.loads(fetched['body'])
            if provider == 'crossref':
                message = payload['message']
                items, following = message['items'], message.get('next-cursor')
                result['provider_hit_count'] = message.get('total-results')
            else:
                items, following = payload['resultList']['result'], payload.get('nextCursorMark')
                result['provider_hit_count'] = payload.get('hitCount')
            if not isinstance(items, list) or len(items) > page_size or following is not None and not isinstance(following, str):
                raise ValueError('Malformed provider page/cursor')
            for raw in items:
                record = _record(provider, raw)
                key = record['identity']
                if key not in groups:
                    groups[key] = {**record, 'variants': [], 'provenance': []}
                group = groups[key]
                if raw not in group['variants']:
                    group['variants'].append(raw)
                group['provenance'].append({'page': page, 'provider': provider, 'retrieved_at': receipt['retrieved_at']})
                group['conflicting_variants'] = len(group['variants']) > 1
            if len(items) < page_size or not following:
                result['exhausted'] = True
                result['status'] = 'success' if groups else 'empty'
                break
            if following == cursor or following in seen:
                result['errors'].append({'page': page, 'error': 'repeated_cursor'})
                result['status'] = 'partial' if groups else 'failed'
                break
            seen.add(cursor)
            cursor = following
            result['status'] = 'success' if groups else 'empty'
        except (ValueError, TypeError, KeyError) as exc:
            result['errors'].append({'page': page, 'error': 'invalid_provider_response', 'detail': str(exc)})
            result['status'] = 'partial' if groups else 'failed'
            receipt['status'] = 'failed'
            break
    result['records'] = list(groups.values())
    result['next_cursor'] = None if result['exhausted'] else cursor
    result['finished_at'] = now()
    return result


def updates(identifier):
    identifier = doi(identifier)
    fetched = request(BASES['crossref'] + '/' + urllib.parse.quote(identifier, safe=''))
    result = {'status': fetched['status'], 'doi': identifier, 'retrieved_at': now(),
              'lookup_receipt': fetched['receipt'], 'identity_status': 'unresolved',
              'coverage': 'Crossref deposited notices only; absence is not certified',
              'semantic_verification': 'not_performed', 'full_text_retrieved': False}
    if fetched['status'] != 'success':
        result['error'] = fetched.get('error')
        return result
    try:
        record = _record('crossref', json.loads(fetched['body'])['message'])
        if record['doi'] != identifier:
            raise ValueError('DOI identity mismatch')
        result.update(record=record, identity_status='identity_verified')
    except (ValueError, TypeError, KeyError) as exc:
        return {**result, 'status': 'failed', 'error': str(exc)}
    # Reverse lookup matters: a notice's update-to points at the affected article.
    notices = _updates_search(identifier)
    result['notices'] = notices
    result['status'] = 'success' if notices['status'] in ('success', 'empty') else 'partial'
    return result


def _updates_search(identifier):
    # A DOI query is retained alongside the updates filter, making scope explicit.
    return search('crossref', identifier, 1, 20, 'updates:' + identifier)


def fulltext(identifier):
    identifier = pmcid(identifier)
    fetched = request(BASES['europepmc'] + identifier + '/fullTextXML')
    result = {'status': fetched['status'], 'pmcid': identifier, 'receipt': fetched['receipt'],
              'access': 'unavailable', 'full_text_retrieved': False, 'read_or_reviewed': False,
              'semantic_verification': 'not_performed', 'identity_status': 'unresolved'}
    if fetched['status'] != 'success':
        return {**result, 'error': fetched.get('error')}
    try:
        raw = fetched['body']
        decoded = raw.decode('utf-8-sig')
        if re.search(r'<!ENTITY\b', decoded, re.I):
            raise ValueError('Internal DTD/entity definitions are not accepted')
        declaration = re.search(r'<!DOCTYPE\b', decoded, re.I)
        if declaration:
            quote = None
            for character in decoded[declaration.end():]:
                if quote:
                    if character == quote:
                        quote = None
                elif character in ('"', "'"):
                    quote = character
                elif character == '[':
                    raise ValueError('Internal DTD/entity definitions are not accepted')
                elif character == '>':
                    break
        # ElementTree does not retrieve an external DTD. JATS commonly includes
        # an external declaration; accepting it never fetches that resource.
        tree = ET.fromstring(decoded)
        for node in tree.iter():
            node.tag = node.tag.split('}')[-1]
        if tree.tag != 'article':
            raise ValueError('Expected an article XML document')
        meta = tree.find('./front/article-meta')
        if meta is None:
            raise ValueError('Missing article metadata')
        identities = [n.text.strip() for n in meta.findall('article-id')
                      if n.attrib.get('pub-id-type') in ('pmcid', 'pmc') and n.text]
        identities = [x if x.upper().startswith('PMC') else 'PMC' + x for x in identities]
        if identifier not in {pmcid(x) for x in identities}:
            raise ValueError('Returned PMCID does not match requested article')
        body = tree.find('body')
        if body is None or not ''.join(body.itertext()).strip():
            raise ValueError('Article body unavailable; metadata is not full text')
        text = '\n'.join(x.strip() for x in tree.itertext() if x.strip()) + '\n'
        sections = [{'locator': 'body/sec[' + str(i) + ']', 'title': ''.join(n.find('title').itertext()) if n.find('title') is not None else None,
                     'text': '\n'.join(x.strip() for x in n.itertext() if x.strip())} for i, n in enumerate(body.findall('.//sec'), 1)]
        licenses = [{'text': ''.join(n.itertext()).strip(), 'attributes': n.attrib} for n in meta.findall('.//license')]
        versions = [''.join(n.itertext()).strip() for n in meta.findall('article-version')]
        return {**result, 'status': 'success', 'access': 'full_text', 'full_text_retrieved': True,
                'identity_status': 'identity_verified', 'publisher_version': versions or None,
                'identifiers': [{'type': n.attrib.get('pub-id-type'), 'value': ''.join(n.itertext()).strip()}
                                for n in meta.findall('article-id')],
                'title': ''.join(meta.find('title-group/article-title').itertext())
                         if meta.find('title-group/article-title') is not None else None,
                'version_identifier': 'xml-sha256:' + hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(),
                'license': licenses or None, 'reuse_rights': 'article-specific; unknown when no license supplied',
                'copyright': [''.join(n.itertext()).strip() for n in meta.findall('.//copyright-statement')],
                'text': text, 'text_sha256': hashlib.sha256(text.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest(), 'sections': sections}
    except (ValueError, TypeError, ET.ParseError, UnicodeError) as exc:
        return {**result, 'status': 'failed', 'error': str(exc)}


def write_json(path, value, force=False):
    with Path(path).open('w' if force else 'x', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n') as stream:
        json.dump(value, stream, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False)
        stream.write('\n')


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    subs = parser.add_subparsers(dest='command', required=True)
    for name in ('search', 'updates', 'fulltext'):
        command = subs.add_parser(name)
        command.add_argument('--output', required=True)
        command.add_argument('--force', action='store_true')
        if name == 'search':
            command.add_argument('--provider', required=True, choices=tuple(BASES))
            command.add_argument('--query', required=True)
            command.add_argument('--pages', type=int, default=1)
            command.add_argument('--page-size', type=int, default=10)
            command.add_argument('--filter')
        elif name == 'updates':
            command.add_argument('--doi', required=True)
        else:
            command.add_argument('--pmcid', required=True)
            command.add_argument('--text-output', required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    try:
        outputs = [Path(args.output)] + ([Path(args.text_output)] if args.command == 'fulltext' else [])
        if len({p.resolve() for p in outputs}) != len(outputs):
            raise ValueError('Text and JSON outputs must differ')
        if not args.force and any(p.exists() for p in outputs):
            raise FileExistsError('Existing output preserved; use new paths or explicit --force')
        if args.command == 'search':
            result = search(args.provider, args.query, args.pages, args.page_size, args.filter)
        elif args.command == 'updates':
            result = updates(args.doi)
        else:
            result = fulltext(args.pmcid)
            if result['status'] == 'success':
                with Path(args.text_output).open('w' if args.force else 'x', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n') as stream:
                    stream.write(result.pop('text'))
                result['text_path'] = str(Path(args.text_output).resolve())
        write_json(args.output, result, args.force)
        print(json.dumps({'status': result['status'], 'output': args.output}))
        return 0 if result['status'] in ('success', 'empty') else 2
    except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
        print(json.dumps({'status': 'failed', 'error': str(exc)}), file=sys.stderr)
        return 2


if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
"""Bounded ESM-2 representations. Only prepare-model --allow-download uses HTTP."""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path
import platform
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time
import urllib.parse
import urllib.request

MODEL = 'facebook/esm2_t6_8M_UR50D'
REVISION = 'c731040fcd8d73dceaa04b0a8e6329b345b0f5df'
FILES = {
 'model.safetensors': (31384292, '24c5fa474c48f3b754b86efe752d5f189d2bcd88190fa2270fc92b2ef3034189'),
 'config.json': (775, 'facb9355e8252149f6f99dff5fd1e32e890c3c655362b261d0c81cd6b5839c85'),
 'tokenizer_config.json': (95, '7e9161ecdb548ec45a41cbc6b24aa4476fdd418461f491c4207baa99419a29ad'),
 'special_tokens_map.json': (125, '3aedcd4211c0d43aec4e607ff60a63255f3174ead795e997350f09a5f8cd9ee1'),
 'vocab.txt': (93, '0b82cc0a7c7cf9e567b1e5892d793285b9fbae822c964ca48696f7db44598e03'),
 'README.md': (1705, '462a2f24724e19c6be0efab926315c294a863c9a9770e2c8b3d859b2d81a07de')}
MAX_BYTES = 1024 * 1024
AA = set('ACDEFGHIKLMNPQRSTVWY')


def sha(raw):
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()


def read_small(path):
    with Path(path).open('rb') as f:
        data = f.read(MAX_BYTES + 1)
    if len(data) > MAX_BYTES:
        raise ValueError('Input/report exceeds 1 MiB')
    return data


def write_new(path, value):
    payload = (json.dumps(value, indent=2, allow_nan=False) + '\n').encode()
    if len(payload) > MAX_BYTES:
        raise ValueError('Output exceeds 1 MiB')
    with Path(path).open('xb') as f:
        f.write(payload)


def fasta(raw):
    text = raw.decode('utf-8')
    records, name, seq, seen = [], None, [], set()
    def finish():
        if name is None:
            return
        sequence = ''.join(seq)
        if not 1 <= len(sequence) <= 512 or set(sequence) - AA:
            raise ValueError('Require 1–512 uppercase canonical amino acids; no truncation or residue mapping')
        records.append({'id': name, 'sequence': sequence, 'sequence_sha256': sha(sequence.encode())})
        if len(records) > 16:
            raise ValueError('At most 16 sequences per run')
    for line in text.splitlines():
        if not line.strip():
            continue
        if line.startswith('>'):
            finish()
            name = line[1:].strip()
            if not name or name in seen or len(name) > 256:
                raise ValueError('FASTA IDs must be unique, nonempty and <=256 characters')
            seen.add(name); seq = []
        else:
            if name is None:
                raise ValueError('Sequence before FASTA header')
            seq.append(line.strip())
    finish()
    if not records:
        raise ValueError('No sequences')
    return records


def verify_model(directory):
    root = Path(directory).resolve()
    if not root.is_dir() or {p.name for p in root.iterdir()} != set(FILES):
        raise ValueError('Model directory must contain exactly the six pinned assets; no unverified sidecars')
    for name, (size, expected) in FILES.items():
        p = root / name
        if p.is_symlink() or not p.is_file() or not p.resolve().is_relative_to(root) or p.stat().st_size != size:
            raise ValueError('Missing, escaping or wrong-sized pinned model asset: ' + name)
        if sha(p.read_bytes()) != expected:
            raise ValueError('Model asset hash mismatch: ' + name)
    config = json.loads((root / 'config.json').read_text())
    if config.get('hidden_size') != 320 or config.get('num_hidden_layers') != 6 or config.get('model_type') != 'esm':
        raise ValueError('Unsupported model architecture')
    return root


def allowed_download(url):
    p = urllib.parse.urlsplit(url)
    host = p.hostname or ''
    return p.scheme == 'https' and not p.username and not p.password and p.port in (None, 443) and (
        host == 'huggingface.co' or host.endswith('.huggingface.co') or host.endswith('.hf.co'))


class DownloadRedirect(urllib.request.HTTPRedirectHandler):
    def redirect_request(self, req, fp, code, msg, headers, newurl):
        if not allowed_download(newurl):
            raise ValueError('Model redirect outside approved HTTPS model hosts')
        return super().redirect_request(req, fp, code, msg, headers, newurl)


def prepare_model(directory, allow_download):
    if not allow_download:
        raise ValueError('Explicit --allow-download is required; about 31.4 MB from the pinned public model')
    target = Path(directory)
    if target.exists():
        raise FileExistsError('Model directory exists; preserve it or choose another path')
    if not target.parent.is_dir():
        raise ValueError('Model parent directory must exist')
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='.esm-download-', dir=target.parent) as temp:
        staged = Path(temp) / 'model'; staged.mkdir()
        opener = urllib.request.build_opener(DownloadRedirect())
        for name, (size, expected) in FILES.items():
            url = 'https://huggingface.co/' + MODEL + '/resolve/' + REVISION + '/' + name
            request = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={'User-Agent': 'research-writer-protein-tools/0.7.0'})
            with opener.open(request, timeout=30) as response:
                raw = response.read(size + 1)
            if len(raw) != size or sha(raw) != expected:
                raise ValueError('Downloaded asset identity mismatch: ' + name)
            (staged / name).write_bytes(raw)
        verify_model(staged)
        # Reservation is exclusive; a competing target is never replaced.
        target.mkdir()
        try:
            for name in FILES:
                shutil.copyfile(staged / name, target / name)
        except BaseException:
            shutil.rmtree(target)
            raise
    return {'status': 'prepared', 'model': MODEL, 'revision': REVISION,
            'directory': str(target.resolve()), 'downloaded_bytes': sum(x[0] for x in FILES.values()),
            'license': 'MIT model card; package/dependency terms separate', 'inference_executed': False}


def select_device(torch, requested):
    if requested in ('auto', 'cpu'):
        return 'cpu'
    if requested == 'cuda' and torch.cuda.is_available() and not getattr(torch.version, 'hip', None):
        return 'cuda'
    if requested == 'mps' and hasattr(torch.backends, 'mps') and torch.backends.mps.is_available():
        return 'mps'
    raise ValueError('Requested accelerator is unavailable or unsupported; no silent fallback: ' + requested)


def compute(request):
    records = request.get('records')
    if (not isinstance(records, list) or not 1 <= len(records) <= 16 or
            type(request.get('threads')) is not int or not 1 <= request['threads'] <= 8 or
            request.get('device') not in ('auto', 'cpu', 'cuda', 'mps')):
        raise ValueError('Invalid bounded worker request')
    ids = set()
    for record in records:
        if not isinstance(record, dict):
            raise ValueError('Invalid worker sequence record')
        name, sequence = record.get('id'), record.get('sequence')
        if (not isinstance(name, str) or not name.strip() or len(name) > 256 or name in ids or
                not isinstance(sequence, str) or not 1 <= len(sequence) <= 512 or set(sequence) - AA or
                record.get('sequence_sha256') != sha(sequence.encode())):
            raise ValueError('Invalid worker sequence identity/length/chemistry')
        ids.add(name)
    # No remote resolution, custom model code, pickle weights or implicit token.
    os.environ.update(HF_HUB_OFFLINE='1', TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE='1', HF_HUB_DISABLE_TELEMETRY='1')
    root = verify_model(request['model_dir'])
    import torch
    import transformers
    from transformers import EsmModel, EsmTokenizer
    torch.set_num_threads(request['threads'])
    device = select_device(torch, request['device'])
    tokenizer = EsmTokenizer.from_pretrained(str(root), local_files_only=True, token=False)
    model = EsmModel.from_pretrained(str(root), local_files_only=True, token=False,
                                    use_safetensors=True, trust_remote_code=False, add_pooling_layer=False)
    model.eval(); model.to(device)
    records = []
    with torch.inference_mode():
        for source in request['records']:
            sequence = source['sequence']
            encoded = tokenizer(sequence, return_tensors='pt', truncation=False)
            if encoded['input_ids'].shape[1] != len(sequence) + 2:
                raise ValueError('Tokenizer did not preserve one token per canonical residue plus BOS/EOS')
            encoded = {k: v.to(device) for k, v in encoded.items()}
            states = model(**encoded).last_hidden_state
            pooled = states[0, 1:len(sequence)+1].mean(dim=0).detach().cpu().tolist()
            if len(pooled) != 320 or not all(math.isfinite(x) for x in pooled):
                raise ValueError('Embedding must have 320 finite values')
            records.append({'id': source['id'], 'length': len(sequence),
                            'sequence_sha256': source['sequence_sha256'], 'embedding': pooled})
    return {'records': records, 'dimension': 320, 'device': device,
            'versions': {'python': platform.python_version(), 'torch': torch.__version__,
                         'transformers': transformers.__version__},
            'pooling': 'last hidden layer mean over residues, excluding BOS/EOS; no truncation'}


def validate_result(result, sources):
    records = result.get('records')
    if not isinstance(records, list) or len(records) != len(sources):
        raise ValueError('Worker output count mismatch')
    for actual, expected in zip(records, sources):
        values = actual.get('embedding')
        if any(actual.get(k) != expected[k] for k in ('id', 'sequence_sha256')) or actual.get('length') != len(expected['sequence']):
            raise ValueError('Worker output identity mismatch')
        if not isinstance(values, list) or len(values) != 320 or not all(type(v) in (int, float) and math.isfinite(v) for v in values):
            raise ValueError('Malformed or nonfinite embedding')
    if result.get('device') not in ('cpu', 'cuda', 'mps'):
        raise ValueError('Unknown observed device')


def run_worker(command, directory, timeout):
    log_path, result_path = directory / 'worker.log', directory / 'result.json'
    with log_path.open('xb') as log:
        proc = subprocess.Popen(command, stdout=log, stderr=log,
                                env={**os.environ, 'HF_HUB_OFFLINE': '1', 'TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE': '1',
                                     'HF_HUB_DISABLE_TELEMETRY': '1', 'PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE': '1'})
        deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
        try:
            while proc.poll() is None:
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                    raise subprocess.TimeoutExpired(command, timeout)
                if log_path.stat().st_size > MAX_BYTES or result_path.exists() and result_path.stat().st_size > MAX_BYTES:
                    raise ValueError('Inference worker exceeded output/log size limit')
                time.sleep(0.05)
            if log_path.stat().st_size > MAX_BYTES:
                raise ValueError('Inference worker exceeded log size limit')
            return proc.returncode
        finally:
            if proc.poll() is None:
                proc.kill()
            proc.wait()


def embed(args):
    if not 1 <= args.timeout <= 120 or not 1 <= args.threads <= 8:
        raise ValueError('Timeout must be 1–120 seconds and threads 1–8')
    outputs = [Path(args.output), Path(args.embedding_output)]
    if outputs[0].resolve() == outputs[1].resolve() or any(p.exists() for p in outputs):
        raise FileExistsError('Choose distinct new report and embedding paths; existing output is preserved')
    raw = read_small(args.input); sources = fasta(raw)
    root = verify_model(args.model_dir)
    request = {'records': sources, 'model_dir': str(root), 'device': args.device, 'threads': args.threads}
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='esm-embedding-') as temp:
        directory = Path(temp); inp = directory/'request.json'; out = directory/'result.json'
        write_new(inp, request)
        returncode = run_worker([sys.executable, str(Path(__file__).resolve()), '_worker',
                                 '--request', str(inp), '--result', str(out)], directory, args.timeout)
        if returncode:
            detail = (directory/'worker.log').read_bytes()[-3000:].decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
            raise RuntimeError('Inference worker failed: ' + detail)
        result = json.loads(read_small(out)); validate_result(result, sources)
    write_new(args.embedding_output, result)
    artifact = Path(args.embedding_output)
    return {'status': 'computed', 'model': MODEL, 'revision': REVISION,
            'model_weights_sha256': FILES['model.safetensors'][1], 'input_sha256': sha(raw),
            'artifact': {'path': str(artifact.resolve()), 'sha256': sha(artifact.read_bytes())},
            'device_requested': args.device, 'device': result['device'], 'versions': result['versions'],
            'platform': platform.platform(), 'sequences': len(sources), 'dimension': 320,
            'pooling': result['pooling'], 'timeout_seconds': args.timeout, 'threads': args.threads,
            'scientific_validation': 'not_performed',
            'interpretation': 'Protein sequence representation only; not folding, affinity, function or noncanonical chemistry validation.'}


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest='command', required=True)
    prepare = sub.add_parser('prepare-model')
    prepare.add_argument('--directory', required=True)
    prepare.add_argument('--allow-download', action='store_true')
    run = sub.add_parser('embed')
    for name in ('input', 'model-dir', 'output', 'embedding-output'):
        run.add_argument('--'+name, required=True)
    run.add_argument('--device', choices=('auto','cpu','cuda','mps'), default='cpu')
    run.add_argument('--threads', type=int, default=1)
    run.add_argument('--timeout', type=int, default=60)
    worker = sub.add_parser('_worker', help=argparse.SUPPRESS)
    worker.add_argument('--request', required=True); worker.add_argument('--result', required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    try:
        if args.command == '_worker':
            write_new(args.result, compute(json.loads(read_small(args.request))))
            return 0
        result = prepare_model(args.directory, args.allow_download) if args.command == 'prepare-model' else embed(args)
        if args.command == 'embed':
            write_new(args.output, result)
        print(json.dumps(result, allow_nan=False))
        return 0
    except (Exception,) as exc:
        failure = {'status': 'failed', 'executed_successfully': False, 'error': str(exc), 'error_type': type(exc).__name__}
        if args.command == 'embed' and not Path(args.output).exists():
            try: write_new(args.output, failure)
            except OSError: pass
        print(json.dumps(failure), file=sys.stderr)
        return 2


if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
"""Explicit bounded OpenSSH job lifecycle. Profile data is not authorization."""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import base64
from contextlib import contextmanager
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath, PureWindowsPath
import re
import shlex
import shutil
import signal
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
MAX_FILE = 8 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_INPUT = 16 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_RPC = 32 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_ERROR = 1024 * 1024
TERMINAL = {'succeeded', 'failed', 'timed_out', 'cancelled'}
STATES = TERMINAL | {'prepared', 'launching', 'running', 'uncertain', 'not_found'}
PROFILE_FIELDS = {'schema_version','ssh_alias','ssh_config','remote_python','remote_root',
                  'expected_hostname','expected_user','connect_timeout_seconds'}
BOOTSTRAP = ("import io,json,sys; e=json.load(sys.stdin); "
             "sys.stdin=io.TextIOWrapper(io.BytesIO(json.dumps(e['request']).encode('utf-8')),encoding='utf-8'); "
             "exec(compile(e['source'],'<research-writer-worker>','exec'),{'__name__':'__main__'})")


def digest(raw):
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()


def canonical(value):
    return json.dumps(value, sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':'), ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False).encode('utf-8')


def pairs(values):
    out = {}
    for key, value in values:
        if key in out:
            raise ValueError('Duplicate JSON key: ' + key)
        out[key] = value
    return out


def decode_json(raw):
    return json.loads(raw, object_pairs_hook=pairs,
                      parse_constant=lambda x: (_ for _ in ()).throw(ValueError('Nonfinite JSON: ' + x)))


def read_bytes(path, maximum=MAX_RPC):
    with Path(path).open('rb') as stream:
        raw = stream.read(maximum + 1)
    if len(raw) > maximum:
        raise ValueError('Input exceeds byte limit')
    return raw


def read_json(path, maximum=MAX_ERROR):
    return decode_json(read_bytes(path, maximum).decode('utf-8'))


def save(path, data, exclusive=False):
    path = Path(path)
    raw = json.dumps(data, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False) + '\n'
    if exclusive:
        with path.open('x', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n') as stream:
            stream.write(raw)
        return
    fd, temp = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix='.rw-state-', dir=path.parent)
    try:
        with os.fdopen(fd, 'w', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n') as stream:
            stream.write(raw)
        os.replace(temp, path)
    finally:
        if os.path.exists(temp):
            os.unlink(temp)


def text(value, name):
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip() or '\x00' in value or '\n' in value or '\r' in value:
        raise ValueError(name + ' must be a nonempty single-line string')
    return value


def relative(value):
    text(value, 'relative path')
    p, w = PurePosixPath(value), PureWindowsPath(value)
    if p.is_absolute() or w.drive or '\\' in value or str(p) != value or any(x in ('..', '.') for x in value.split('/')) or value.endswith('/'):
        raise ValueError('Confined POSIX relative path required')
    if any(x in ('.ssh','.aws','.git','.codex') or x == '.env' or x.startswith('.env.') for x in p.parts):
        raise ValueError('Credential/configuration path not permitted for transfer')
    return value


def job_id(value):
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not re.fullmatch('[0-9a-f]{32}', value):
        raise ValueError('Job ID must be 32 lower-case hexadecimal characters')
    return value


def profile(value):
    if not isinstance(value, dict) or set(value) - PROFILE_FIELDS or type(value.get('schema_version')) is not int or value['schema_version'] != 1:
        raise ValueError('Invalid profile schema or unknown fields; store no credentials')
    p = dict(value)
    alias = p.get('ssh_alias')
    if not isinstance(alias, str) or not re.fullmatch(r'[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,252}', alias):
        raise ValueError('Expected an existing plain SSH alias, not options or a URL')
    python = text(p.get('remote_python'), 'remote_python')
    if len(python) > 512 or not (re.fullmatch(r'python3(?:\.\d+)?', python) or
                               PurePosixPath(python).is_absolute() and '..' not in PurePosixPath(python).parts):
        raise ValueError('Remote Python must be a program token or absolute POSIX path')
    root = text(p.get('remote_root'), 'remote_root')
    if not PurePosixPath(root).is_absolute() or len(PurePosixPath(root).parts) < 3 or '..' in PurePosixPath(root).parts:
        raise ValueError('Select an absolute dedicated POSIX remote job root')
    p['remote_root'] = str(PurePosixPath(root))
    for name in ('expected_hostname','expected_user'):
        p.setdefault(name, None)
        if p[name] is not None:
            text(p[name], name)
    p.setdefault('ssh_config', None)
    if p['ssh_config'] is not None:
        conf = Path(text(p['ssh_config'], 'ssh_config')).expanduser().absolute()
        if not conf.is_file():
            raise ValueError('Explicit SSH config does not exist')
        p['ssh_config'] = str(conf)
    p.setdefault('connect_timeout_seconds', 10)
    if type(p['connect_timeout_seconds']) is not int or not 1 <= p['connect_timeout_seconds'] <= 30:
        raise ValueError('Connection timeout must be 1..30 seconds')
    return p


def validate_manifest(m):
    fields = {'schema_version','job_id','argv','inputs','outputs','resources','required_device'}
    if not isinstance(m, dict) or set(m) != fields or type(m.get('schema_version')) is not int or m['schema_version'] != 1:
        raise ValueError('Manifest fields/schema do not match version 1')
    job_id(m['job_id'])
    argv = m['argv']
    if not isinstance(argv, list) or not 1 <= len(argv) <= 128 or any(not isinstance(a,str) or not a or '\x00' in a or len(a)>8192 for a in argv):
        raise ValueError('A bounded explicit argv array is required')
    if m['required_device'] != 'cpu':
        raise ValueError('Only task-bounded CPU execution is supported in this release')
    r = m['resources']
    limits = {'wall_seconds':(1,3600),'max_output_bytes':(1,MAX_INPUT),
              'max_log_bytes':(1,MAX_ERROR),'threads':(1,16)}
    if not isinstance(r,dict) or set(r) != set(limits):
        raise ValueError('Exact resource fields required')
    for name, (low, high) in limits.items():
        if type(r[name]) is not int or not low <= r[name] <= high:
            raise ValueError('Resource bound out of range: ' + name)
    if not isinstance(m['inputs'],list) or len(m['inputs'])>32 or not isinstance(m['outputs'],list) or not 1 <= len(m['outputs']) <= 32:
        raise ValueError('Bounded inputs and explicit output names required')
    names = []
    for item in m['inputs']:
        if not isinstance(item,dict) or set(item) != {'source','destination','sha256'}:
            raise ValueError('Invalid input record')
        relative(item['source']); names.append(relative(item['destination']))
        if not isinstance(item['sha256'],str) or not re.fullmatch('[0-9a-f]{64}',item['sha256']):
            raise ValueError('Input needs an exact lower-case SHA-256')
    outputs = [relative(n) for n in m['outputs']]
    if '.collection.json' in outputs:
        raise ValueError('Reserved collection metadata name')
    if len(set(names))!=len(names) or len(set(outputs))!=len(outputs) or set(names)&set(outputs):
        raise ValueError('Input/output paths must be unique and nonoverlapping')
    # Reject file/directory collisions as well as exact duplicate names.
    allnames = names + outputs
    if any(a != b and b.startswith(a + '/') for a in allnames for b in allnames):
        raise ValueError('Input/output file-directory paths overlap')
    return m


def input_files(manifest, base):
    root = Path(base).resolve()
    out, total = [], 0
    for item in manifest['inputs']:
        p = root / relative(item['source'])
        if not p.resolve().is_relative_to(root) or any(x.is_symlink() for x in [p, *p.parents] if x != root and x.is_relative_to(root)):
            raise ValueError('Symlink or escaping input path')
        if not p.is_file():
            raise ValueError('Input must be a regular file')
        raw = read_bytes(p, MAX_FILE); total += len(raw)
        if total > MAX_INPUT or digest(raw) != item['sha256']:
            raise ValueError('Input total size/hash mismatch')
        out.append({'path':item['destination'],'sha256':item['sha256'],
                    'data':base64.b64encode(raw).decode('ascii')})
    return out


def ssh_argv(p):
    options = ['BatchMode=yes','StrictHostKeyChecking=yes',
               'ConnectTimeout='+str(p['connect_timeout_seconds']),'ConnectionAttempts=1',
               'ServerAliveInterval=5','ServerAliveCountMax=2','ForwardAgent=no',
               'ForwardX11=no','ClearAllForwardings=yes','PermitLocalCommand=no',
               'ControlMaster=no','ControlPath=none','RemoteCommand=none']
    argv = ['ssh','-T']
    for opt in options:
        argv.extend(['-o',opt])
    if p['ssh_config']:
        argv.extend(['-F',p['ssh_config']])
    argv.extend([p['ssh_alias'], shlex.join([p['remote_python'],'-c',BOOTSTRAP])])
    return argv


class TransportError(RuntimeError):
    pass


def rpc(p, request):
    source = read_bytes(ROOT / 'remote_worker.py', MAX_ERROR).decode('utf-8')
    payload = canonical({'source':source,'request':request})
    if len(payload)>MAX_RPC:
        raise ValueError('RPC input too large')
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='rw-ssh-') as folder:
        root=Path(folder); incoming=root/'request.json'; incoming.write_bytes(payload)
        stdout, stderr=root/'stdout',root/'stderr'
        with incoming.open('rb') as inp, stdout.open('wb') as out, stderr.open('wb') as err:
            child=subprocess.Popen(ssh_argv(p),stdin=inp,stdout=out,stderr=err,
                                   shell=False,start_new_session=os.name=='posix')
            deadline=time.monotonic()+p['connect_timeout_seconds']+30
            reason=None
            while child.poll() is None:
                if time.monotonic()>deadline:
                    reason='SSH RPC timeout; remote outcome may be uncertain';break
                if stdout.stat().st_size>MAX_RPC or stderr.stat().st_size>MAX_ERROR:
                    reason='SSH RPC output limit; remote outcome may be uncertain';break
                time.sleep(.05)
            if reason:
                if os.name=='posix':
                    try:os.killpg(child.pid,signal.SIGKILL)
                    except ProcessLookupError:pass
                else:child.kill()
                child.wait(timeout=5)
                raise TransportError(reason)
            child.wait()
        if stdout.stat().st_size>MAX_RPC or stderr.stat().st_size>MAX_ERROR:
            raise TransportError('SSH RPC output limit; remote outcome may be uncertain')
        error=read_bytes(stderr,MAX_ERROR).decode('utf-8','replace')
        if child.returncode:
            raise TransportError('SSH exited '+str(child.returncode)+': '+error[-2000:])
        try:
            response=decode_json(read_bytes(stdout,MAX_RPC).decode('utf-8'))
        except (ValueError,UnicodeError) as exc:
            raise TransportError('Malformed remote JSON; outcome may be uncertain') from exc
    validate_response(response,p,request)
    return response


def validate_response(r,p,request):
    if not isinstance(r,dict) or type(r.get('protocol')) is not int or r['protocol']!=1 or type(r.get('ok')) is not bool:
        raise TransportError('Invalid protocol response')
    ident=r.get('identity')
    if not isinstance(ident,dict) or not isinstance(ident.get('hostname'),str) or not isinstance(ident.get('user'),str):
        raise TransportError('Missing remote identity')
    for key,field in [('expected_hostname','hostname'),('expected_user','user')]:
        if p[key] is not None and ident[field]!=p[key]:
            raise TransportError('Remote identity mismatch: '+field)
    expected=request['operation']
    if expected=='inspect':
        if r.get('status')!='inspected':raise TransportError('Invalid inspection status')
    else:
        if r.get('job_id')!=request['job_id'] or r.get('manifest_sha256')!=request['manifest_sha256'] or r.get('status') not in STATES:
            raise TransportError('Job identity/hash/status mismatch')
    return r


@contextmanager
def local_lock(state_dir, identity):
    root=Path(state_dir);root.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    lock=root/('.'+job_id(identity)+'.lock')
    # Keep the inode: unlinking an active lock would let a new caller lock a
    # different file. OS locks release on close and abrupt process death.
    stream=lock.open('a+b')
    acquired=False
    try:
        stream.seek(0,os.SEEK_END)
        if stream.tell()==0:
            stream.write(b'0');stream.flush()
        stream.seek(0)
        try:
            if os.name=='nt':
                import msvcrt
                msvcrt.locking(stream.fileno(),msvcrt.LK_NBLCK,1)
            else:
                import fcntl
                fcntl.flock(stream.fileno(),fcntl.LOCK_EX|fcntl.LOCK_NB)
            acquired=True
        except OSError as exc:
            raise ValueError('Job is owned by another live local operation; do not replace its lock file') from exc
        yield root/(identity+'.json')
    finally:
        if acquired:
            stream.seek(0)
            if os.name=='nt':
                import msvcrt
                msvcrt.locking(stream.fileno(),msvcrt.LK_UNLCK,1)
            else:
                import fcntl
                fcntl.flock(stream.fileno(),fcntl.LOCK_UN)
        stream.close()


def base_request(p,operation,identity=None,manifest_hash=None):
    return {'protocol':1,'operation':operation,'remote_root':p['remote_root'],
            'expected_hostname':p['expected_hostname'],'expected_user':p['expected_user'],
            'job_id':identity,'manifest_sha256':manifest_hash}


def pinned(p):
    if not p['expected_hostname'] or not p['expected_user']:
        raise ValueError('Pin expected_hostname and expected_user after inspection before job operations')


def invoke(p,request,state,state_path):
    try:
        result=rpc(p,request)
    except (TransportError,OSError,subprocess.SubprocessError) as exc:
        state.update(status='uncertain',last_error=str(exc),uncertain_operation=request['operation'])
        save(state_path,state)
        raise TransportError(str(exc)) from exc
    # Keep restart metadata bounded: artifact/log bytes belong in returned
    # collection data, never in the durable state receipt.
    receipt={k:v for k,v in result.items() if k not in ('files','logs')}
    if isinstance(result.get('files'),list):
        receipt['files']=[{'path':f.get('path'),'sha256':f.get('sha256')} for f in result['files'] if isinstance(f,dict)]
    if isinstance(result.get('logs'),dict):
        receipt['logs']={name:{'sha256':entry.get('sha256')} for name,entry in result['logs'].items() if isinstance(entry,dict)}
    state.update(status=result['status'],last_remote=receipt,last_error=None)
    state.pop('uncertain_operation',None)
    save(state_path,state)
    return result


def load_state(path,p,identity):
    state=read_json(path,MAX_RPC)
    if not isinstance(state,dict) or state.get('job_id')!=identity or state.get('profile_sha256')!=digest(canonical(p)):
        raise ValueError('Saved state/profile binding differs')
    validate_manifest(state.get('manifest'))
    if digest(canonical(state['manifest']))!=state.get('manifest_sha256'):
        raise ValueError('Saved manifest binding differs')
    if not isinstance(state.get('worker_sha256'),str) or not re.fullmatch('[0-9a-f]{64}',state['worker_sha256']):
        raise ValueError('Saved controller source binding is missing')
    return state


def prepare(p,manifest_path,state_dir,allow_transfer=False):
    pinned(p)
    if not allow_transfer:raise ValueError('Explicit --allow-transfer required')
    m=validate_manifest(read_json(manifest_path));files=input_files(m,Path(manifest_path).resolve().parent)
    identity=m['job_id'];h=digest(canonical(m))
    worker=read_bytes(ROOT/'remote_worker.py',MAX_ERROR).decode('utf-8')
    with local_lock(state_dir,identity) as state_path:
        if state_path.exists():raise FileExistsError('Job already has local state; reconcile status instead of preparing again')
        state={'schema_version':1,'job_id':identity,'manifest_sha256':h,'manifest':m,
               'profile_sha256':digest(canonical(p)),'worker_sha256':digest(worker.encode('utf-8')),
               'status':'prepare_attempted','submit_attempted':False}
        save(state_path,state,exclusive=True)
        request=base_request(p,'prepare',identity,h)
        request.update(manifest=m,files=files,worker_source=worker)
        return invoke(p,request,state,state_path)


def operate(p,operation,identity,state_dir,allow_execute=False,allow_cancel=False,destination=None):
    pinned(p);job_id(identity)
    with local_lock(state_dir,identity) as state_path:
        state=load_state(state_path,p,identity)
        if operation=='submit':
            if not allow_execute:raise ValueError('Explicit --allow-execute required')
            if state.get('submit_attempted') or state.get('status')!='prepared':
                raise ValueError('Submission already attempted or job is not prepared; never resubmit an uncertain job')
            if digest(read_bytes(ROOT/'remote_worker.py',MAX_ERROR))!=state['worker_sha256']:
                raise ValueError('Controller source changed since preparation; reconcile before execution')
            state.update(submit_attempted=True,status='launching')
            save(state_path,state)
        elif operation=='cancel':
            if not allow_cancel:raise ValueError('Explicit --allow-cancel required')
            state['cancel_attempted']=True;save(state_path,state)
        request=base_request(p,operation,identity,state['manifest_sha256'])
        result=invoke(p,request,state,state_path)
        if operation=='collect' and result['ok']:
            collected=collect_files(result,state['manifest'],destination)
            state['collection']=collected;save(state_path,state)
            result={**result,'collection':collected}
        return result


def collect_files(result,manifest,destination):
    if destination is None or result['status'] not in TERMINAL:
        raise ValueError('Collect requires a new destination and a terminal job')
    target=Path(destination).absolute()
    if target.exists() or target.is_symlink():raise FileExistsError('Collection destination already exists')
    if not target.parent.is_dir():raise ValueError('Collection parent must exist')
    files=result.get('files')
    if not isinstance(files,list):raise ValueError('Missing artifact list')
    expected=set(manifest['outputs']);seen=set();decoded=[];total=0
    for item in files:
        if not isinstance(item,dict) or set(item)!={'path','sha256','data'}:
            raise ValueError('Malformed collected artifact')
        name=relative(item['path'])
        if name not in expected or name in seen:raise ValueError('Unexpected/duplicate artifact')
        raw=base64.b64decode(item['data'],validate=True);total+=len(raw)
        if total>manifest['resources']['max_output_bytes'] or digest(raw)!=item['sha256']:
            raise ValueError('Collected artifact size/hash mismatch')
        decoded.append((name,raw));seen.add(name)
    missing=sorted(expected-seen)
    if result['status']=='succeeded' and (missing or result.get('partial') is not False):
        raise ValueError('Success cannot omit expected artifacts or be partial')
    partial=result['status']!='succeeded' or bool(missing)
    guard=local_lock(target.parent,digest(('collection:'+str(target)).encode('utf-8'))[:32])
    guard.__enter__()
    staged=None
    try:
        if target.exists() or target.is_symlink():raise FileExistsError('Collection destination already exists')
        staged=Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix='.rw-collection-',dir=target.parent))
        for name,raw in decoded:
            path=staged/name;path.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True);path.write_bytes(raw)
        summary={'status':result['status'],'partial':partial,'missing':missing,
                 'files':[{'path':name,'sha256':digest(raw),'bytes':len(raw)} for name,raw in decoded]}
        save(staged/'.collection.json',summary,exclusive=True)
        if target.exists() or target.is_symlink():raise FileExistsError('Collection destination appeared')
        staged.rename(target);staged=None
        return {**summary,'destination':str(target)}
    finally:
        if staged is not None:shutil.rmtree(staged)
        guard.__exit__(None,None,None)


def main(argv=None):
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    commands=parser.add_subparsers(dest='operation',required=True)
    for op in ('inspect','prepare','submit','status','recover','collect','cancel'):
        cmd=commands.add_parser(op);cmd.add_argument('--profile',required=True);cmd.add_argument('--output',required=True)
        if op=='prepare':cmd.add_argument('--manifest',required=True);cmd.add_argument('--allow-transfer',action='store_true')
        if op!='inspect':cmd.add_argument('--state-dir',required=True)
        if op not in ('inspect','prepare'):cmd.add_argument('--job-id',required=True)
        if op=='submit':cmd.add_argument('--allow-execute',action='store_true')
        if op=='cancel':cmd.add_argument('--allow-cancel',action='store_true')
        if op=='collect':cmd.add_argument('--destination',required=True)
    args=parser.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        if Path(args.output).exists() or Path(args.output).is_symlink():raise FileExistsError('Output report exists')
        p=profile(read_json(args.profile))
        if args.operation=='inspect':result=rpc(p,base_request(p,'inspect'))
        elif args.operation=='prepare':result=prepare(p,args.manifest,args.state_dir,args.allow_transfer)
        else:result=operate(p,args.operation,args.job_id,args.state_dir,
                           getattr(args,'allow_execute',False),getattr(args,'allow_cancel',False),getattr(args,'destination',None))
        save(args.output,result,exclusive=True)
        return 0 if result['ok'] else 2
    except (OSError,ValueError,TypeError,KeyError,TransportError,subprocess.SubprocessError) as exc:
        failure={'protocol':1,'ok':False,'status':'failed','error':str(exc)}
        if not Path(args.output).exists() and not Path(args.output).is_symlink():
            try:save(args.output,failure,exclusive=True)
            except OSError:pass
        print(json.dumps(failure),file=sys.stderr)
        return 2


if __name__=='__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())
```
