```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
"""POSIX, trusted-job controller. Cooperative bounds are not an OS sandbox."""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import base64
import getpass
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath
import platform
import re
import shutil
import signal
import socket
import stat
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time
from contextlib import contextmanager

PROTOCOL = 1
MAX_REQUEST = 32 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_FILE = 8 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_INPUT = 16 * 1024 * 1024
TERMINAL = {'succeeded', 'failed', 'timed_out', 'cancelled'}
# Retain handles only within a long-lived library caller; SSH RPC exits detach.
_LOCAL_CONTROLLERS = {}


def digest(data):
    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()


def canonical(value):
    return json.dumps(value, sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':'), ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False).encode('utf-8')


def pairs(values):
    result = {}
    for key, value in values:
        if key in result:
            raise ValueError('Duplicate JSON key')
        result[key] = value
    return result


def parse(raw):
    if len(raw) > MAX_REQUEST:
        raise ValueError('JSON input limit exceeded')
    def constant(_):
        raise ValueError('Nonfinite JSON number')
    return json.loads(raw.decode('utf-8'), object_pairs_hook=pairs, parse_constant=constant)


def read_json(path):
    with Path(path).open('rb') as stream:
        return parse(stream.read(MAX_REQUEST + 1))


def atomic(path, value):
    path = Path(path)
    descriptor, name = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix='.state-', dir=path.parent)
    try:
        with os.fdopen(descriptor, 'wb') as stream:
            stream.write(canonical(value) + b'\n')
            stream.flush()
            os.fsync(stream.fileno())
        os.replace(name, path)
    finally:
        if os.path.exists(name):
            os.unlink(name)


def identity():
    if os.name != 'posix':
        raise ValueError('Remote worker requires a POSIX Python host')
    import pwd
    return {'hostname': socket.gethostname(), 'user': pwd.getpwuid(os.getuid()).pw_name,
            'python': sys.executable, 'python_version': platform.python_version(),
            'os': platform.system(), 'machine': platform.machine(), 'uid': os.getuid()}


def relative(value):
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value or len(value) > 512 or '\\' in value or '\x00' in value:
        raise ValueError('Expected bounded POSIX relative path')
    p = PurePosixPath(value)
    if p.is_absolute() or '..' in p.parts or value in ('.', '') or any(x in ('.ssh', '.aws', '.git', '.codex') for x in p.parts):
        raise ValueError('Unsafe relative path')
    if p.as_posix() != value:
        raise ValueError('Relative paths must be normalized')
    return p.as_posix()


def hash_string(value):
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not re.fullmatch('[0-9a-f]{64}', value):
        raise ValueError('Expected lowercase SHA-256')
    return value


def validate_manifest(m):
    if not isinstance(m, dict) or type(m.get('schema_version')) is not int or m['schema_version'] != 1:
        raise ValueError('Unsupported manifest schema')
    if set(m) != {'schema_version', 'job_id', 'argv', 'inputs', 'outputs', 'resources', 'required_device'}:
        raise ValueError('Unexpected/missing manifest fields')
    if not isinstance(m['job_id'], str) or not re.fullmatch('[0-9a-f]{32}', m['job_id']):
        raise ValueError('Expected 32 lowercase hex job ID')
    argv = m['argv']
    if not isinstance(argv, list) or not 1 <= len(argv) <= 64 or not all(isinstance(x, str) and x and len(x) <= 4096 and '\x00' not in x for x in argv) or sum(map(len, argv)) > 32768:
        raise ValueError('Invalid bounded command argument array')
    if m['required_device'] != 'cpu':
        raise ValueError('Only explicitly selected CPU jobs are supported by this controller')
    resources = m['resources']
    bounds = {'wall_seconds': (1, 3600), 'max_output_bytes': (1, MAX_INPUT),
              'max_log_bytes': (1, 1024 * 1024), 'threads': (1, 16)}
    if not isinstance(resources, dict) or set(resources) != set(bounds):
        raise ValueError('Missing/unknown resource fields')
    for key, (low, high) in bounds.items():
        if type(resources[key]) is not int or not low <= resources[key] <= high:
            raise ValueError('Invalid resource bound: ' + key)
    if not isinstance(m['inputs'], list) or len(m['inputs']) > 32 or not isinstance(m['outputs'], list) or not 1 <= len(m['outputs']) <= 32:
        raise ValueError('Invalid bounded input/output lists')
    names = []
    for entry in m['inputs']:
        if not isinstance(entry, dict) or set(entry) != {'source', 'destination', 'sha256'}:
            raise ValueError('Invalid input entry')
        relative(entry['source']); names.append(relative(entry['destination'])); hash_string(entry['sha256'])
    outputs = [relative(x) for x in m['outputs']]
    if len(names) != len(set(names)) or len(outputs) != len(set(outputs)) or set(names) & set(outputs):
        raise ValueError('Duplicate or overlapping input/output paths')
    # Parent/child file conflicts cannot be staged safely.
    paths = [PurePosixPath(x) for x in names + outputs]
    if any(a != b and a in b.parents for a in paths for b in paths):
        raise ValueError('Input/output paths conflict as files and directories')
    return m


def root_path(value):
    if not isinstance(value, str) or '\x00' in value or not PurePosixPath(value).is_absolute() or '..' in PurePosixPath(value).parts:
        raise ValueError('Expected absolute dedicated remote root')
    root = Path(value)
    if root == Path('/') or root.is_symlink():
        raise ValueError('Invalid remote root')
    root = root.resolve()
    if root.exists() and (not root.is_dir() or root.stat().st_uid != os.getuid() or root.stat().st_mode & 0o022):
        raise ValueError('Remote root must be owned by the current account')
    return root


def inside(root, name, must_exist=True):
    name = relative(name)
    if root.is_symlink():
        raise ValueError('Work directory symlink is forbidden')
    candidate = root / name
    cursor = candidate
    while cursor != root:
        if cursor.is_symlink():
            raise ValueError('Symlink artifact is forbidden')
        cursor = cursor.parent
    if not candidate.resolve().is_relative_to(root.resolve()):
        raise ValueError('Artifact escapes job work directory')
    if must_exist and not candidate.is_file():
        raise ValueError('Artifact is missing or not a regular file')
    return candidate


def nearest_existing(path):
    while not path.exists():
        path = path.parent
    return path


@contextmanager
def mutation_lock(job):
    import fcntl
    lock = job / '.rpc-lock'
    descriptor = None
    try:
        # Keep one inode. Kernel locks release after an interrupted RPC; a stale
        # directory from an older controller is deliberately not stolen.
        descriptor = os.open(lock, os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_NONBLOCK, 0o600)
        actual, named = os.fstat(descriptor), lock.lstat()
        if (not stat.S_ISREG(actual.st_mode) or actual.st_uid != os.getuid() or
                actual.st_nlink != 1 or actual.st_mode & 0o022 or
                (actual.st_dev, actual.st_ino) != (named.st_dev, named.st_ino)):
            raise ValueError('Remote lock ownership/type/identity mismatch')
        try:
            fcntl.flock(descriptor, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
        except BlockingIOError as exc:
            raise ValueError('Concurrent remote mutation; reconcile without stealing the lock') from exc
        yield
    finally:
        if descriptor is not None:
            os.close(descriptor)


def snapshot(job):
    state = read_json(job / 'state.json')
    if state['status'] in ('running', 'launching') and time.time() - state.get('heartbeat', state.get('updated_at', 0)) > 5:
        return {**state, 'status': 'uncertain', 'reason': 'Controller heartbeat stale; no PID-based action or relaunch performed'}
    return state


def reply(ident, request, status, **extra):
    child = _LOCAL_CONTROLLERS.get(request.get('job_id'))
    if child is not None and status in TERMINAL:
        try:
            child.wait(timeout=1)
            _LOCAL_CONTROLLERS.pop(request.get('job_id'), None)
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            pass
    return {'protocol': PROTOCOL, 'ok': True, 'status': status, 'identity': ident,
            'job_id': request.get('job_id'), 'manifest_sha256': request.get('manifest_sha256'), **extra}


def prepare(root, req, ident):
    m = validate_manifest(req.get('manifest'))
    if m['job_id'] != req['job_id'] or digest(canonical(m)) != req['manifest_sha256']:
        raise ValueError('Manifest identity/hash mismatch')
    files = req.get('files')
    if not isinstance(files, list) or len(files) != len(m['inputs']):
        raise ValueError('Transferred file count mismatch')
    expected = {x['destination']: x['sha256'] for x in m['inputs']}
    decoded, total = {}, 0
    for entry in files:
        if not isinstance(entry, dict) or set(entry) != {'path', 'sha256', 'data'}:
            raise ValueError('Invalid transfer record')
        name = relative(entry['path'])
        if name in decoded or name not in expected or entry['sha256'] != expected[name] or not isinstance(entry['data'], str) or len(entry['data']) > 4 * (MAX_FILE // 3 + 2):
            raise ValueError('Transferred identity/size mismatch')
        raw = base64.b64decode(entry['data'], validate=True)
        total += len(raw)
        if len(raw) > MAX_FILE or total > MAX_INPUT or digest(raw) != expected[name]:
            raise ValueError('Transfer size/hash mismatch')
        decoded[name] = raw
    code = req.get('worker_source')
    if not isinstance(code, str) or not code or len(code.encode()) > 96 * 1024:
        raise ValueError('Missing/bounded worker source required')
    compile(code, '<staged-controller>', 'exec')
    job = root / req['job_id']
    if job.is_symlink() or job.exists() and (not job.is_dir() or job.stat().st_uid != os.getuid()):
        raise ValueError('Existing job directory ownership/type mismatch')
    if job.exists():
        saved = read_json(job / 'prepared.json')
        if saved['manifest_sha256'] != req['manifest_sha256'] or saved['worker_sha256'] != digest(code.encode()):
            raise ValueError('Existing job has different immutable inputs/controller')
        s = snapshot(job)
        return reply(ident, req, s['status'], state=s, already_prepared=True)
    free = shutil.disk_usage(nearest_existing(root)).free
    required = total + m['resources']['max_output_bytes'] + 2 * m['resources']['max_log_bytes'] + MAX_INPUT
    if free < required:
        raise ValueError('Insufficient remote disk space for bounded preparation')
    root.mkdir(mode=0o700, parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    if root.stat().st_uid != os.getuid():
        raise ValueError('Remote root ownership changed')
    stage = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix='.prepare-', dir=root))
    try:
        (stage / 'work').mkdir(mode=0o700)
        for name, raw in decoded.items():
            path = inside(stage / 'work', name, False)
            path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            with path.open('xb') as stream:
                stream.write(raw)
        (stage / 'controller.py').write_text(code, encoding='utf-8', newline='\n')
        atomic(stage / 'manifest.json', m)
        atomic(stage / 'prepared.json', {'manifest_sha256': req['manifest_sha256'],
                                        'worker_sha256': digest(code.encode()), 'input_bytes': total})
        atomic(stage / 'state.json', {'status': 'prepared', 'updated_at': time.time(), 'job_id': m['job_id']})
        # A prepared directory is never empty; concurrent rename cannot replace it.
        os.rename(stage, job)
    finally:
        if stage.exists():
            shutil.rmtree(stage)
    return reply(ident, req, 'prepared', state=read_json(job / 'state.json'))


def checked_job(root, req):
    job = root / req['job_id']
    if job.is_symlink() or job.exists() and (not job.is_dir() or job.stat().st_uid != os.getuid()):
        raise ValueError('Invalid job directory ownership/type')
    if not job.exists():
        return None
    for name in ('prepared.json', 'manifest.json', 'controller.py', 'state.json'):
        if (job / name).is_symlink() or not (job / name).is_file():
            raise ValueError('Job metadata must remain regular files')
    saved = read_json(job / 'prepared.json')
    if saved['manifest_sha256'] != req['manifest_sha256']:
        raise ValueError('Job manifest binding mismatch')
    m = validate_manifest(read_json(job / 'manifest.json'))
    if digest(canonical(m)) != req['manifest_sha256'] or m['job_id'] != req['job_id']:
        raise ValueError('Stored manifest changed')
    if digest((job / 'controller.py').read_bytes()) != saved['worker_sha256']:
        raise ValueError('Stored controller changed')
    return job


def handle(req):
    ident = {}
    try:
        ident = identity()
        if not isinstance(req, dict) or type(req.get('protocol')) is not int or req['protocol'] != PROTOCOL:
            raise ValueError('Unsupported protocol')
        operation = req.get('operation')
        if operation not in ('inspect', 'prepare', 'submit', 'status', 'recover', 'collect', 'cancel'):
            raise ValueError('Unsupported operation')
        root = root_path(req.get('remote_root'))
        if operation == 'inspect':
            for key, observed in (('expected_hostname', ident['hostname']), ('expected_user', ident['user'])):
                if req.get(key) is not None and req[key] != observed:
                    raise ValueError('Remote identity mismatch: ' + key)
            usage = shutil.disk_usage(nearest_existing(root))
            return reply(ident, req, 'inspected', free_bytes=usage.free,
                         supported_devices=['cpu'], supported_remote_os='posix', root_exists=root.exists())
        if req.get('expected_hostname') != ident['hostname'] or req.get('expected_user') != ident['user']:
            raise ValueError('Pinned remote host/user identity mismatch')
        if not isinstance(req.get('job_id'), str) or not re.fullmatch('[0-9a-f]{32}', req['job_id']):
            raise ValueError('Invalid job ID')
        hash_string(req.get('manifest_sha256'))
        if operation == 'prepare':
            return prepare(root, req, ident)
        job = checked_job(root, req)
        if job is None:
            return reply(ident, req, 'not_found')
        if operation in ('status', 'recover'):
            s = snapshot(job)
            if operation == 'recover':
                return reply(ident, req, s['status'], state=s, reconciliation={
                    'mode': 'read_only', 'execution_attempted': (job / 'submission.json').exists(),
                    'restarted': False, 'pid_signal_sent': False,
                    'liveness': 'not_proven_by_heartbeat_or_stored_pid',
                    'next_action': 'Collect terminal artifacts; otherwise reconcile the original job without resubmitting'})
            return reply(ident, req, s['status'], state=s)
        if operation == 'collect':
            s = snapshot(job)
            if s['status'] not in TERMINAL:
                raise ValueError('Only terminal jobs can be collected; uncertain jobs require reconciliation')
            manifest = read_json(job / 'manifest.json')
            if (job / 'artifacts.json').is_symlink():
                raise ValueError('Artifact index symlink is forbidden')
            saved = read_json(job / 'artifacts.json') if (job / 'artifacts.json').exists() else {}
            files, missing, total = [], [], 0
            for name in manifest['outputs']:
                path = inside(job / 'work', name, False)
                if not path.exists():
                    missing.append(name); continue
                path = inside(job / 'work', name)
                with path.open('rb') as stream:
                    raw = stream.read(manifest['resources']['max_output_bytes'] + 1)
                total += len(raw)
                if total > manifest['resources']['max_output_bytes'] or name not in saved or digest(raw) != saved[name]['sha256']:
                    raise ValueError('Output changed, oversized or not checkpointed')
                files.append({'path': name, 'sha256': digest(raw), 'data': base64.b64encode(raw).decode('ascii')})
            if s['status'] == 'succeeded' and missing:
                raise ValueError('Successful-job output is missing')
            logs = {}
            for name in ('stdout.log', 'stderr.log'):
                path = job / name
                if path.is_symlink():
                    raise ValueError('Log symlink is forbidden')
                if path.exists():
                    with path.open('rb') as stream:
                        raw = stream.read(manifest['resources']['max_log_bytes'])
                    logs[name] = {'data': base64.b64encode(raw).decode('ascii'), 'sha256': digest(raw)}
            return reply(ident, req, s['status'], state=s, files=files, missing=missing,
                         partial=s['status'] != 'succeeded' or bool(missing), logs=logs)
        with mutation_lock(job):
            s = snapshot(job)
            if operation == 'cancel':
                if s['status'] in TERMINAL:
                    return reply(ident, req, s['status'], state=s, cancel_requested=False)
                with (job / 'cancel.request').open('a'):
                    pass
                if s['status'] == 'prepared':
                    s = {**s, 'status': 'cancelled', 'reason': 'Cancelled before launch', 'updated_at': time.time()}
                    atomic(job / 'state.json', s)
                return reply(ident, req, s['status'], state=s, cancel_requested=True)
            if (job / 'submission.json').exists():
                return reply(ident, req, s['status'], state=s, already_submitted=True)
            if s['status'] != 'prepared' or (job / 'cancel.request').exists():
                raise ValueError('Job is not prepared for first submission')
            with (job / 'submission.json').open('x', encoding='utf-8') as stream:
                json.dump({'attempted_at': time.time(), 'manifest_sha256': req['manifest_sha256']}, stream)
                stream.flush(); os.fsync(stream.fileno())
            atomic(job / 'state.json', {'status': 'launching', 'updated_at': time.time(), 'heartbeat': time.time(), 'job_id': req['job_id']})
            try:
                with (job / 'controller.log').open('xb') as log:
                    p = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, str(job / 'controller.py'), '--run-job', str(job)],
                                         stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=log, stderr=log,
                                         start_new_session=True, close_fds=True)
                _LOCAL_CONTROLLERS[req['job_id']] = p
                atomic(job / 'controller-receipt.json', {'pid': p.pid, 'purpose': 'diagnostic only; never used for PID-based cancellation'})
            except Exception:
                atomic(job / 'state.json', {'status': 'uncertain', 'updated_at': time.time(), 'reason': 'Launch attempted; operator reconciliation required'})
                raise
            return reply(ident, req, 'launching', state={'status': 'launching', 'job_id': req['job_id']})
    except Exception as exc:
        return {'protocol': PROTOCOL, 'ok': False, 'status': 'failed', 'identity': ident,
                'job_id': req.get('job_id') if isinstance(req, dict) else None,
                'manifest_sha256': req.get('manifest_sha256') if isinstance(req, dict) else None,
                'error': {'code': type(exc).__name__, 'message': str(exc)[:2000]}}


def terminate(child):
    # The controller owns this live Popen handle/group; RPC never trusts a stored PID.
    if child.poll() is None:
        try:
            os.killpg(child.pid, signal.SIGTERM)
        except ProcessLookupError:
            pass
        try:
            child.wait(timeout=1)
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            try:
                os.killpg(child.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
            except ProcessLookupError:
                pass
            child.wait(timeout=5)


def work_bytes(work):
    total, count = 0, 0
    for directory, dirs, files in os.walk(work, followlinks=False):
        if len(dirs) + len(files) > 256:
            raise ValueError('Work directory entry limit exceeded')
        for name in dirs + files:
            path = Path(directory) / name
            if path.is_symlink():
                raise ValueError('Work directory contains a symlink')
        for name in files:
            count += 1
            if count > 256:
                raise ValueError('Work file count exceeded')
            total += (Path(directory) / name).stat().st_size
    return total


def bound_logs(job, limit):
    """Bound available regular diagnostics without following replaced paths."""
    errors = []
    for name in ('stdout.log', 'stderr.log'):
        path = job / name
        descriptor = None
        try:
            before = path.lstat()
            if not stat.S_ISREG(before.st_mode) or before.st_uid != os.getuid():
                raise ValueError('Log is not a current-account regular file')
            descriptor = os.open(path, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_NONBLOCK)
            actual = os.fstat(descriptor)
            if not stat.S_ISREG(actual.st_mode) or actual.st_uid != os.getuid() or (before.st_dev, before.st_ino) != (actual.st_dev, actual.st_ino):
                raise ValueError('Log identity changed during cleanup')
            if actual.st_size > limit:
                os.ftruncate(descriptor, limit)
        except FileNotFoundError:
            pass  # No task was launched or its diagnostics are unavailable.
        except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:
            errors.append(name + ': ' + str(exc)[:200])
        finally:
            if descriptor is not None:
                os.close(descriptor)
    return errors


def run_job(directory):
    job = Path(directory).resolve()
    child = None
    resources = None
    state = {'status': 'failed', 'job_id': job.name, 'updated_at': time.time()}
    artifacts = {}
    try:
        m = validate_manifest(read_json(job / 'manifest.json'))
        prepared = read_json(job / 'prepared.json')
        if digest(canonical(m)) != prepared['manifest_sha256'] or digest((job / 'controller.py').read_bytes()) != prepared['worker_sha256']:
            raise ValueError('Controller/manifest changed before execution')
        resources = m['resources']
        if (job / 'cancel.request').exists():
            state.update(status='cancelled', reason='Cancelled before execution')
        else:
            env = {**os.environ, 'CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES': '', 'OMP_NUM_THREADS': str(resources['threads']),
                   'OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS': str(resources['threads']), 'MKL_NUM_THREADS': str(resources['threads']),
                   'NUMEXPR_NUM_THREADS': str(resources['threads']), 'VECLIB_MAXIMUM_THREADS': str(resources['threads'])}
            started = time.monotonic()
            with (job / 'stdout.log').open('xb') as stdout, (job / 'stderr.log').open('xb') as stderr:
                child = subprocess.Popen(m['argv'], cwd=job / 'work', stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                                         stdout=stdout, stderr=stderr, env=env, shell=False,
                                         start_new_session=True, close_fds=True)
                state.update(status='running', heartbeat=time.time(), started_at=time.time())
                atomic(job / 'state.json', state)
                while True:
                    reason = None
                    if (job / 'cancel.request').exists():
                        state['status'], reason = 'cancelled', 'Cancellation request observed by owning controller'
                    elif time.monotonic() - started >= resources['wall_seconds']:
                        state['status'], reason = 'timed_out', 'Wall-time limit exceeded'
                    elif any((job / n).stat().st_size > resources['max_log_bytes'] for n in ('stdout.log', 'stderr.log')):
                        state['status'], reason = 'failed', 'Log-size limit exceeded'
                    elif work_bytes(job / 'work') > prepared['input_bytes'] + resources['max_output_bytes']:
                        state['status'], reason = 'failed', 'Work/output-size limit exceeded'
                    if reason:
                        state['reason'] = reason; terminate(child); break
                    if child.poll() is not None:
                        state['returncode'] = child.returncode
                        state['status'] = 'succeeded' if child.returncode == 0 else 'failed'
                        if child.returncode:
                            state['reason'] = 'Task exited nonzero'
                        break
                    state['heartbeat'] = time.time(); atomic(job / 'state.json', state); time.sleep(0.1)
                state['returncode'] = child.wait()
            total = 0
            for name in m['outputs']:
                path = inside(job / 'work', name, False)
                if not path.exists():
                    if state['status'] == 'succeeded':
                        state.update(status='failed', reason='Declared output missing')
                    continue
                path = inside(job / 'work', name)
                with path.open('rb') as stream:
                    raw = stream.read(resources['max_output_bytes'] + 1)
                total += len(raw)
                if total > resources['max_output_bytes']:
                    state.update(status='failed', reason='Declared output-size limit exceeded')
                    artifacts = {}; break
                artifacts[name] = {'sha256': digest(raw), 'bytes': len(raw)}
    except Exception as exc:
        if child is not None:
            terminate(child)
        state.update(status='failed', reason=f'{type(exc).__name__}: {str(exc)[:1000]}')
    finally:
        # Artifact validation can fail after a task has emitted diagnostics.
        # Stop the owned child first, then bound logs on every terminal path.
        if child is not None:
            try:
                terminate(child)
            except Exception as exc:
                state.update(status='failed', reason='Owned child cleanup failed: ' + str(exc)[:500])
        if resources is not None:
            cleanup_errors = bound_logs(job, resources['max_log_bytes'])
            if cleanup_errors:
                state['log_cleanup_errors'] = cleanup_errors
                if state['status'] == 'succeeded':
                    state.update(status='failed', reason='Available diagnostic cleanup failed')
        state.update(updated_at=time.time(), finished_at=time.time())
        atomic(job / 'artifacts.json', artifacts)
        atomic(job / 'state.json', state)
    return 0


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--run-job', help=argparse.SUPPRESS)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.run_job:
        return run_job(args.run_job)
    try:
        request = parse(sys.stdin.buffer.read(MAX_REQUEST + 1))
        result = handle(request)
    except Exception as exc:
        result = {'protocol': PROTOCOL, 'ok': False, 'status': 'failed', 'error': {'code': type(exc).__name__, 'message': str(exc)[:2000]}}
    raw = canonical(result) + b'\n'
    if len(raw) > MAX_REQUEST:
        raw = canonical({'protocol': PROTOCOL, 'ok': False, 'status': 'failed', 'error': {'code': 'response_limit', 'message': 'Response exceeds limit'}}) + b'\n'
    sys.stdout.buffer.write(raw)
    return 0


if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Original MIT research utilities. Optional engines are planned, never auto-launched."""
import argparse
import csv
import hashlib
import importlib.metadata
import importlib.util
import io
import json
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path
import platform
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
MAX_INPUT = 10 * 1024 * 1024
CONTEXT = ['run', 'condition', 'dataset', 'metric', 'unit', 'seed', 'statistic', 'value']


def read_bytes(path):
    p = Path(path)
    if not p.is_file() or p.stat().st_size > MAX_INPUT:
        raise ValueError('Input must be a regular file no larger than 10 MiB')
    return p.read_bytes()


def read_json(path):
    return json.loads(read_bytes(path).decode('utf-8'))


def digest(data):
    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()


def write_json(path, value, force=False):
    p = Path(path)
    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    text = json.dumps(value, indent=2, sort_keys=True, allow_nan=False) + '\n'
    with p.open('w' if force else 'x', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        f.write(text)


def profile(path):
    p = {'schema_version': 1, 'mode': 'local', 'python': None,
         'remote_host': None, 'accelerator': 'unknown',
         'executables': {}, 'model_dirs': {}, 'notes': ''}
    if path:
        given = read_json(path)
        if not isinstance(given, dict) or set(given) - set(p):
            raise ValueError('Profile contains unknown fields; store no credentials or readiness claims')
        p.update(given)
    if p['schema_version'] != 1 or p['mode'] not in ('local', 'remote'):
        raise ValueError('Unsupported profile schema or mode')
    if p['accelerator'] not in ('auto', 'unknown', 'cpu', 'cuda', 'mps', 'rocm'):
        raise ValueError('Unsupported accelerator declaration')
    for key in ('executables', 'model_dirs'):
        if not isinstance(p[key], dict) or not all(isinstance(k, str) and isinstance(v, str) and v for k, v in p[key].items()):
            raise ValueError(key + ' must map engine IDs to nonempty paths')
    for key in ('python', 'remote_host'):
        if p[key] is not None and (not isinstance(p[key], str) or not p[key]):
            raise ValueError(key + ' must be a path/host string or null')
    return p


def catalog():
    c = read_json(ROOT / 'references' / 'tool-catalog.json')
    return c['engines']


def version(distribution):
    try:
        return importlib.metadata.version(distribution) if distribution else None
    except importlib.metadata.PackageNotFoundError:
        return None


def executable(path):
    if not path:
        return None
    found = shutil.which(path)
    if found:
        return found
    p = Path(path).expanduser()
    return str(p.resolve()) if p.is_file() else None


def doctor(args):
    p = profile(args.profile)
    remote = p['mode'] == 'remote'
    alternate = bool(p['python'] and Path(p['python']).expanduser().resolve() != Path(sys.executable).resolve())
    engines = []
    for e in catalog():
        v = version(e.get('distribution'))
        cli = executable(p['executables'].get(e['id']) or e.get('cli_name'))
        model = p['model_dirs'].get(e['id'])
        engines.append({'id': e['id'], 'status': 'remote_unprobed' if remote else 'dependency_present_unvalidated' if v or cli else 'not_detected',
                        'local_distribution_version': v, 'local_executable': cli,
                        'declared_model_directory': model,
                        'local_model_directory_exists': bool(model and Path(model).expanduser().is_dir()) if not remote else None,
                        'weights_verified': False, 'license_acceptance_verified': False,
                        'execution_tested': False})
    gpu = {'status': 'not_probed', 'source': None, 'devices': None}
    if args.probe_gpu and not remote:
        binary = shutil.which('nvidia-smi')
        if binary:
            try:
                result = subprocess.run([binary, '--query-gpu=name,memory.total,driver_version', '--format=csv,noheader'],
                                        capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=3, check=False)
                gpu = {'status': 'observed' if result.returncode == 0 else 'probe_failed',
                       'source': 'local nvidia-smi', 'devices': result.stdout.strip() if result.returncode == 0 else None,
                       'returncode': result.returncode}
            except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as err:
                gpu = {'status': 'probe_failed', 'source': 'local nvidia-smi', 'devices': None, 'error': type(err).__name__}
        else:
            gpu = {'status': 'not_detected', 'source': 'local PATH nvidia-smi lookup', 'devices': None}
    return {'status': 'inventory_only', 'observed_at': datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
            'probe_scope': 'current local Python interpreter and PATH; remote never contacted',
            'python': sys.executable, 'python_version': platform.python_version(),
            'os': platform.system(), 'machine': platform.machine(), 'profile': p,
            'profile_python_differs': alternate, 'gpu': gpu, 'engines': engines,
            'limits': ['Configured is not executed or scientifically validated.',
                       'MPS/ROCm/model VRAM adequacy are not established by this inventory.',
                       'Use the profile interpreter on its device to inspect that environment.',
                       'fair-esm and Biohub esm share a namespace: use separate environments.']}


def plan(args):
    p = profile(args.profile)
    e = next((x for x in catalog() if x['id'] == args.tool), None)
    if e is None:
        raise ValueError('Unknown engine ID; use doctor/catalog')
    data = read_bytes(args.input)
    replacements = {'input': str(Path(args.input).resolve()),
                    'output': '<SELECTED_RESULT_DIRECTORY>',
                    'python': p.get('python') or '<DEVICE_PYTHON>',
                    'executable': p['executables'].get(args.tool) or '<CONFIGURED_EXECUTABLE_OR_SCRIPT>',
                    'model_dir': p['model_dirs'].get(args.tool) or '<PROVISIONED_MODEL_DIRECTORY>',
                    'db_dir': '<PROVISIONED_DATABASE_DIRECTORY>'}
    argv = e.get('example_argv')
    if argv:
        for key, value in replacements.items():
            argv = [part.replace('{' + key + '}', value) for part in argv]
    return {'schema_version': 1, 'status': 'planned_not_executed', 'tool': e['id'],
            'repository': e['repository'], 'audited_revision': e['revision'], 'device_profile': p,
            'input': {'path': str(Path(args.input).resolve()), 'sha256': digest(data)},
            'upstream_example_argv': argv, 'argv_is_execution_ready': False,
            'input_schema_validated_by_engine': False, 'remote_contacted': False,
            'code_license': e['code_license'], 'weights_terms': e['weights_terms'],
            'data_service_terms': e['data_service_terms'],
            'required_checks': e['prerequisites'] + [
                'Inspect selected release/runtime and exact input schema on the target device.',
                'Verify model/data terms, checksums, storage/RAM/VRAM and run-time/spending limits.',
                'Provision inputs/models locally; inspect startup download and external-MSA behavior.',
                'Replace placeholders, choose working/result directories, then run a small authorized acceptance test.',
                'Record real command, versions, inputs, all failures, stdout/stderr and outputs before reporting execution.'],
            'scientific_limits': e['limitations']}


def provenance(tool, versions, inputs, settings):
    return {'tool': tool, 'versions': versions, 'python': platform.python_version(),
            'platform': platform.system() + '/' + platform.machine(), 'inputs': inputs,
            'settings': settings, 'executed': True, 'experimental_validation': False}


def rdkit_base(args):
    from rdkit import Chem, rdBase
    if not args.smiles or len(args.smiles) > 4096:
        raise ValueError('SMILES must contain 1..4096 characters')
    mol = Chem.MolFromSmiles(args.smiles)
    if mol is None or mol.GetNumAtoms() == 0 or mol.GetNumAtoms() > 256:
        raise ValueError('Invalid, empty, or oversized SMILES (maximum 256 heavy atoms)')
    canonical = Chem.MolToSmiles(mol, isomericSmiles=True)
    return Chem, rdBase, mol, canonical


def descriptors(args):
    from rdkit.Chem import Descriptors, rdMolDescriptors
    Chem, rdBase, mol, canonical = rdkit_base(args)
    values = {'MolWt': Descriptors.MolWt(mol), 'ExactMolWt': Descriptors.ExactMolWt(mol),
              'MolLogP': Descriptors.MolLogP(mol), 'HeavyAtomCount': mol.GetNumHeavyAtoms(),
              'NumHDonors': Descriptors.NumHDonors(mol), 'NumHAcceptors': Descriptors.NumHAcceptors(mol),
              'FormalCharge': Chem.GetFormalCharge(mol)}
    if not all(math.isfinite(x) for x in values.values()):
        raise ValueError('A descriptor was nonfinite')
    return {'status': 'computed', 'input_smiles': args.smiles, 'canonical_smiles': canonical,
            'formula': rdMolDescriptors.CalcMolFormula(mol), 'fragments': len(Chem.GetMolFrags(mol)),
            'descriptors': values, 'units': {'MolWt': 'g/mol', 'ExactMolWt': 'Da', 'MolLogP': 'dimensionless', 'FormalCharge': 'elementary_charge'},
            'interpretation': 'Calculated molecular properties; not measured potency, safety, solubility or activity.',
            'provenance': provenance('RDKit', {'rdkit': rdBase.rdkitVersion},
                                     {'smiles_sha256': digest(args.smiles.encode())}, {'descriptor_set': list(values)})}


def conformer(args):
    from rdkit.Chem import AllChem
    Chem, rdBase, mol, canonical = rdkit_base(args)
    if mol.GetNumAtoms() > 128 or len(Chem.GetMolFrags(mol)) != 1:
        raise ValueError('Conformer route requires one connected molecule with <=128 heavy atoms')
    if not 0 <= args.seed < 2147483647 or not 1 <= args.max_iters <= 2000:
        raise ValueError('Seed or optimization iteration limit out of range')
    out = Path(args.sdf)
    if out.exists() and not args.force:
        raise FileExistsError('SDF exists; choose another output or explicitly use --force')
    mol = Chem.AddHs(mol)
    params = AllChem.ETKDGv3()
    params.randomSeed = args.seed
    cid = AllChem.EmbedMolecule(mol, params)
    if cid < 0:
        raise ValueError('RDKit embedding failed; no conformer result')
    if not AllChem.MMFFHasAllMoleculeParams(mol):
        raise ValueError('MMFF parameters unavailable; no silent force-field substitution')
    optimizer = AllChem.MMFFOptimizeMolecule(mol, maxIters=args.max_iters)
    if optimizer < 0:
        raise ValueError('MMFF setup failed')
    mol.SetProp('_Name', 'RDKit generated geometry; not an experimental structure')
    block = (Chem.MolToMolBlock(mol, confId=cid) + '\n$$$$\n').encode()
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with out.open('wb' if args.force else 'xb') as f:
        f.write(block)
    return {'status': 'computed' if optimizer == 0 else 'not_converged', 'canonical_smiles': canonical,
            'atom_count_with_hydrogens': mol.GetNumAtoms(), 'embedding_id': cid,
            'optimizer_status': optimizer, 'converged': optimizer == 0,
            'artifact': {'path': str(out.resolve()), 'sha256': digest(block)},
            'interpretation': 'One seeded, locally optimized model geometry; not a measured bound pose or unique physical conformation.',
            'provenance': provenance('RDKit', {'rdkit': rdBase.rdkitVersion},
                                     {'smiles_sha256': digest(args.smiles.encode())},
                                     {'seed': args.seed, 'embedding': 'ETKDGv3', 'force_field': 'MMFF', 'max_iters': args.max_iters})}


def openmm_smoke(args):
    import openmm
    from openmm import unit
    system = openmm.System()
    system.addParticle(12 * unit.dalton)
    system.addParticle(12 * unit.dalton)
    force = openmm.HarmonicBondForce()
    force.addBond(0, 1, 0.1 * unit.nanometer, 100 * unit.kilojoule_per_mole / unit.nanometer**2)
    system.addForce(force)
    integrator = openmm.VerletIntegrator(0.001 * unit.picosecond)
    context = openmm.Context(system, integrator, openmm.Platform.getPlatformByName('Reference'))
    context.setPositions([openmm.Vec3(0, 0, 0), openmm.Vec3(0.2, 0, 0)] * unit.nanometer)
    value = context.getState(getEnergy=True).getPotentialEnergy().value_in_unit(unit.kilojoule_per_mole)
    observed = context.getPlatform().getName()
    if not math.isfinite(value) or not math.isclose(value, 0.5, rel_tol=0, abs_tol=1e-8) or observed != 'Reference':
        raise ValueError('Analytic energy or Reference platform acceptance failed')
    return {'status': 'computed', 'kind': 'artificial_two_particle_energy_check', 'energy': value,
            'unit': 'kJ/mol', 'expected': 0.5, 'error': value - 0.5, 'platform': observed,
            'interpretation': 'Installation/unit smoke test only; no biological simulation or validation performed.',
            'provenance': provenance('OpenMM', {'openmm': openmm.__version__}, {},
                                     {'r0_nm': 0.1, 'r_nm': 0.2, 'k_kJ_mol_nm2': 100, 'particles': 2})}


def fasta(args):
    raw = read_bytes(args.input)
    records, current, seq, seen = [], None, [], set()
    def append():
        if current is None:
            return
        sequence = ''.join(seq).upper()
        if not sequence or len(sequence) > 1000000 or any(not (c.isascii() and (c.isalpha() or c in '*-.')) for c in sequence):
            raise ValueError('Invalid or oversized FASTA sequence')
        nonstandard = [{'position_1based': i + 1, 'symbol': c} for i, c in enumerate(sequence) if c not in 'ACDEFGHIKLMNPQRSTVWY']
        records.append({'id': current, 'length': len(sequence), 'sequence_sha256': digest(sequence.encode()),
                        'nonstandard_count': len(nonstandard), 'first_nonstandard_positions': nonstandard[:100],
                        'canonical_sequence_only': not nonstandard})
    for line in raw.decode('utf-8').splitlines():
        line = line.strip()
        if not line:
            continue
        if line.startswith('>'):
            append()
            current = line[1:].strip()
            if not current or current in seen:
                raise ValueError('Missing or duplicate FASTA identifier')
            seen.add(current)
            seq = []
        else:
            if current is None:
                raise ValueError('FASTA sequence before header')
            seq.append(''.join(line.split()))
    append()
    if not records:
        raise ValueError('No FASTA records')
    return {'status': 'inspected', 'input_sha256': digest(raw), 'records': records,
            'interpretation': 'Syntax/identity check only. Residue symbols, including U/O/X, do not establish model support for chemical modifications or cofactors.'}


def measurements(args, result):
    if args.command == 'rdkit-descriptors':
        metrics = result['descriptors']
        units = result['units']
        condition, seed = result['canonical_smiles'], 'not_applicable'
    elif args.command == 'openmm-smoke':
        metrics, units = {'potential_energy': result['energy']}, {'potential_energy': result['unit']}
        condition, seed = 'two_particle_harmonic_reference', 'not_applicable'
    else:
        return
    output = Path(args.output).with_suffix('.measurements.csv')
    if output.resolve() == Path(args.output).resolve():
        raise ValueError('JSON output must not end in .measurements.csv')
    stream = io.StringIO(newline='')
    writer = csv.DictWriter(stream, fieldnames=CONTEXT, lineterminator='\n')
    writer.writeheader()
    for name, value in metrics.items():
        writer.writerow({'run': args.run_id or args.command, 'condition': condition, 'dataset': 'explicit_local_input',
                         'metric': name, 'unit': units.get(name, 'count' if isinstance(value, int) else 'dimensionless'),
                         'seed': seed, 'statistic': 'single_deterministic_calculation', 'value': value})
    data = stream.getvalue().encode()
    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with output.open('wb' if args.force else 'xb') as f:
        f.write(data)
    result['measurement_artifact'] = {'path': str(output.resolve()), 'sha256': digest(data), 'columns': CONTEXT}


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    subs = parser.add_subparsers(dest='command', required=True)
    for name in ('doctor', 'profile', 'plan', 'rdkit-descriptors', 'rdkit-conformer', 'openmm-smoke', 'fasta'):
        sub = subs.add_parser(name)
        sub.add_argument('--output', required=True, help='New JSON report; existing files preserved unless --force')
        sub.add_argument('--force', action='store_true')
        if name in ('doctor', 'plan'):
            sub.add_argument('--profile')
        if name == 'doctor':
            sub.add_argument('--probe-gpu', action='store_true')
        if name in ('plan', 'fasta'):
            sub.add_argument('--input', required=True)
        if name == 'plan':
            sub.add_argument('--tool', required=True)
        if name.startswith('rdkit-'):
            sub.add_argument('--smiles', required=True)
        if name == 'rdkit-conformer':
            sub.add_argument('--sdf', required=True)
            sub.add_argument('--seed', type=int, required=True)
            sub.add_argument('--max-iters', type=int, default=200)
        if name in ('rdkit-descriptors', 'openmm-smoke'):
            sub.add_argument('--run-id')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    try:
        outputs = [Path(args.output)]
        if args.command == 'rdkit-conformer':
            outputs.append(Path(args.sdf))
        if args.command in ('rdkit-descriptors', 'openmm-smoke'):
            outputs.append(Path(args.output).with_suffix('.measurements.csv'))
        if len({p.resolve() for p in outputs}) != len(outputs):
            raise ValueError('Report, SDF and measurement output paths must be distinct')
        if any(p.exists() for p in outputs) and not args.force:
            raise FileExistsError('An output exists; choose new paths or explicitly use --force')
        if args.command == 'profile':
            result = profile(None)
        else:
            function = {'doctor': doctor, 'plan': plan, 'rdkit-descriptors': descriptors,
                        'rdkit-conformer': conformer, 'openmm-smoke': openmm_smoke, 'fasta': fasta}[args.command]
            result = function(args)
            measurements(args, result)
        write_json(args.output, result, args.force)
        print(json.dumps({'status': result.get('status', 'profile_created'), 'output': args.output}, allow_nan=False))
        return 2 if result.get('status') == 'not_converged' else 0
    except (ValueError, TypeError, KeyError, OSError, ImportError, RuntimeError) as err:
        failure = {'status': 'unavailable' if isinstance(err, ImportError) else 'failed',
                   'error': str(err), 'error_type': type(err).__name__, 'executed_successfully': False}
        if not Path(args.output).exists():
            try:
                write_json(args.output, failure)
            except OSError:
                pass
        print(json.dumps(failure), file=sys.stderr)
        return 2


if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
"""Offline installed-skill smoke; use --molecular to require RDKit and OpenMM.

Writes only to a disposable temporary directory. No models, downloads, remote
connections or GPU probes. A pass is an installation check, not research validation.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import hashlib
import json
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
CONTEXT = ('run', 'condition', 'dataset', 'metric', 'unit', 'seed', 'statistic', 'value')
REQUIRED = ('SKILL.md', 'LICENSE', 'README.md', 'scripts/evidence_tools.py',
            'scripts/research_tools.py', 'scripts/self_check.py',
            'scripts/literature_tools.py', 'scripts/support_tools.py',
            'scripts/conversion_tools.py', 'scripts/protein_tools.py',
            'scripts/remote_tools.py', 'scripts/remote_worker.py',
            'scripts/discovery_tools.py', 'scripts/claim_audit.py',
            'scripts/linux_tools.py', 'scripts/linux_supervisor.py',
            'scripts/checkpoint_tools.py', 'scripts/backup_tools.py',
            'templates/research-record.md', 'references/tool-catalog.json',
            *('references/' + name + '.md' for name in (
                'argument-and-synthesis', 'computational-research', 'delivery',
                'device-profiles', 'modes', 'prewriting', 'publication-workflow',
                'question-development', 'research-and-sources', 'standards',
                'tool-usage', 'testing', 'literature-retrieval', 'source-support',
                'molecular-conversion', 'protein-embeddings', 'remote-execution',
                'deeper-retrieval', 'claim-audit', 'linux-isolation', 'durable-checkpoints', 'off-device-backups')))


def require(condition, message):
    if not condition:
        raise ValueError(message)


def package_check():
    for name in REQUIRED:
        p = ROOT / name
        require(p.is_file() and p.resolve().is_relative_to(ROOT), 'Missing/escaping resource: ' + name)
    for folder in ('references', 'templates', 'scripts'):
        for p in (ROOT / folder).rglob('*'):
            if p.suffix == '.py':
                compile(p.read_text(encoding='utf-8'), str(p), 'exec')
    # README includes repository-facing installation links; bundled resources
    # and entry point must resolve inside the independently installed skill.
    for p in [ROOT / 'SKILL.md', *(ROOT / 'references').glob('*.md'), *(ROOT / 'templates').glob('*.md')]:
        for target in re.findall(r'\[[^\]]*\]\(([^)]+)\)', p.read_text(encoding='utf-8')):
            if target.startswith('#') or re.match(r'[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9+.-]*:', target):
                continue
            linked = (p.parent / target.split('#')[0]).resolve()
            require(linked.is_relative_to(ROOT) and linked.is_file(), 'Broken/escaping link: ' + target)
    text = (ROOT / 'SKILL.md').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    require(re.search(r'^name: research-writer$', text, re.M), 'Skill discovery name differs')
    entries = json.loads((ROOT / 'references/tool-catalog.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    engines = entries.get('engines') if isinstance(entries, dict) else None
    require(isinstance(engines, list) and len(engines) == 13, 'Incomplete engine catalog')


def run_cli(work, script, arguments, expected=0):
    result = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ROOT / 'scripts' / script), *map(str, arguments)],
                            cwd=work, text=True, capture_output=True, timeout=60,
                            env={**os.environ, 'PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE': '1'})
    require(result.returncode == expected,
            f'{script} {arguments[0]} exited {result.returncode}: {result.stderr[-1000:]}')
    return result


def load(path):
    return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))


def write(path, data):
    path.write_text(json.dumps(data), encoding='utf-8')


def core_check(work):
    run_cli(work, 'evidence_tools.py', ['init-project', '--directory', work])
    project = work / 'research-project.json'
    require(load(project)['target'] is None and load(project)['destination'] is None, 'Invented intake default')
    project.write_text('{"customized": true}', encoding='utf-8')
    run_cli(work, 'evidence_tools.py', ['init-project', '--directory', work])
    require(load(project) == {'customized': True}, 'Project customization overwritten')
    bibliography = work / 'input.json'
    write(bibliography, [{'doi': 'doi:10.1234/SAMPLE'}, {'doi': 'https://doi.org/10.1234/sample'}])
    bib_out = work / 'bibliography.json'
    run_cli(work, 'evidence_tools.py', ['bibliography', '--input', bibliography, '--output', bib_out])
    records = load(bib_out)['records']
    require(len(records) == 1 and len(records[0]['provenance']) == 2, 'Bibliography identity/provenance failed')
    before = bib_out.read_bytes()
    run_cli(work, 'evidence_tools.py', ['bibliography', '--input', bibliography, '--output', bib_out], expected=2)
    require(before == bib_out.read_bytes(), 'Existing output overwritten')
    profile = work / 'device.json'
    run_cli(work, 'research_tools.py', ['profile', '--output', profile])
    inventory = work / 'inventory.json'
    run_cli(work, 'research_tools.py', ['doctor', '--profile', profile, '--output', inventory])
    observed = load(inventory)
    require(observed['gpu']['status'] == 'not_probed' and
            all(not x['execution_tested'] for x in observed['engines']), 'Inventory invented execution')
    fasta = work / 'input.fa'
    fasta.write_text('>control\nACDX\n', encoding='utf-8')
    output = work / 'fasta.json'
    run_cli(work, 'research_tools.py', ['fasta', '--input', fasta, '--output', output])
    record = load(output)['records'][0]
    require(record['length'] == 4 and record['nonstandard_count'] == 1, 'FASTA identity failed')
    row = dict(zip(CONTEXT, ('control', 'baseline', 'toy', 'signal', 'mM', '0', 'single', '0')))
    table = work / 'control.csv'
    with table.open('w', newline='', encoding='utf-8') as stream:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(stream, fieldnames=CONTEXT)
        writer.writeheader(); writer.writerow(row)
    manifest = {'artifacts': [{'id': 'a', 'path': table.name, 'sha256': hashlib.sha256(table.read_bytes()).hexdigest()}],
                'measurements': [{'id': 'm', 'artifact_id': 'a', 'row': 1, **row}],
                'claims': [{'id': 'c', 'evidence_id': 'm', **row}]}
    input_file, report = work / 'evidence.json', work / 'verified.json'
    write(input_file, manifest)
    run_cli(work, 'evidence_tools.py', ['verify-evidence', '--input', input_file, '--output', report])
    require(load(report)['passed'], 'Valid zero evidence rejected')
    manifest['claims'][0]['unit'] = 'M'
    write(input_file, manifest)
    failed = work / 'rejected.json'
    run_cli(work, 'evidence_tools.py', ['verify-evidence', '--input', input_file, '--output', failed], expected=2)
    require(not load(failed)['passed'], 'Wrong-unit claim accepted')
    # These help calls import both optional tools without any HTTP request.
    run_cli(work, 'literature_tools.py', ['--help'])
    run_cli(work, 'support_tools.py', ['--help'])
    run_cli(work, 'conversion_tools.py', ['--help'])
    run_cli(work, 'protein_tools.py', ['--help'])
    run_cli(work, 'remote_tools.py', ['--help'])
    run_cli(work, 'linux_tools.py', ['--help'])
    run_cli(work, 'linux_supervisor.py', ['--help'])
    source = work / 'source.txt'
    text = 'Fictional control: the observed signal was zero.'
    source.write_text(text, encoding='utf-8')
    sha = hashlib.sha256(source.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    packet = {'schema_version': 1,
              'sources': [{'id': 's', 'version': 'fixture-v1', 'access': 'full_text',
                           'identity_status': 'identity_verified', 'path': source.name,
                           'sha256': sha, 'locator': 'fictional control'}],
              'reviews': [{'id': 'r', 'claim': 'The fictional signal was zero.',
                           'source_id': 's', 'source_version': 'fixture-v1', 'source_sha256': sha,
                           'start': 0, 'end': len(text), 'passage': text, 'locator': 'control sentence',
                           'judgment': 'supports', 'rationale': 'Explicit zero in this fictional control.',
                           'reviewer': 'installation-control', 'reviewed_at': '2026-01-01T00:00:00Z'}]}
    packet_path = work / 'support.json'
    write(packet_path, packet)
    support_report = work / 'support-pass.json'
    run_cli(work, 'support_tools.py', ['review-support', '--input', packet_path, '--output', support_report])
    require(load(support_report)['passed'] and
            load(support_report)['semantic_entailment'] == 'not_automatically_verified',
            'Support record integrity/semantic boundary failed')
    packet['reviews'][0]['passage'] = 'Invented source text'
    write(packet_path, packet)
    support_fail = work / 'support-fail.json'
    run_cli(work, 'support_tools.py', ['review-support', '--input', packet_path, '--output', support_fail], expected=2)
    require(not load(support_fail)['passed'], 'Invented passage accepted')


def deeper_check(work):
    # Loading the optional discovery CLI must make no network request.
    run_cli(work, 'discovery_tools.py', ['--help'])
    run_cli(work, 'claim_audit.py', ['--help'])
    source = work / 'audit-source.txt'
    text = 'Synthetic installation control: the signal was zero.'
    source.write_text(text, encoding='utf-8')
    digest = hashlib.sha256(source.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    claim = 'The synthetic control signal was zero.'
    packet = {'schema_version': 1,
              'sources': [{'id': 's', 'version': 'control-v1', 'access': 'full_text',
                           'identity_status': 'identity_verified', 'path': source.name,
                           'sha256': digest, 'locator': 'synthetic control',
                           'study_family': 'control-family', 'publication_status': 'current'}],
              'claims': [{'id': 'c1', 'text': claim, 'required_source_ids': ['s'],
                          'required_access': 'full_text'}],
              'reviews': [{'id': 'r', 'claim_id': 'c1', 'claim': claim,
                           'source_id': 's', 'source_version': 'control-v1',
                           'source_sha256': digest, 'start': 0, 'end': len(text),
                           'passage': text, 'locator': 'synthetic control',
                           'judgment': 'supports', 'rationale': 'Explicit statement in fictional control.',
                           'reviewer': 'installation-control', 'reviewed_at': '2026-01-01T00:00:00Z'}]}
    manifest, report = work / 'claims.json', work / 'claim-audit.json'
    write(manifest, packet)
    run_cli(work, 'claim_audit.py', ['audit-claims', '--input', manifest, '--output', report])
    result = load(report)
    require(result['status'] == 'records_valid' and
            result['semantic_entailment'] == 'not_automatically_verified' and
            result['drafting_clearance'] == 'not_granted',
            'Claim audit confused record integrity with drafting clearance')
    packet['claims'].append({'id': 'c2', 'text': 'An additional unreviewed claim.',
                             'required_source_ids': ['s'], 'required_access': 'full_text'})
    write(manifest, packet)
    incomplete = work / 'claim-audit-incomplete.json'
    run_cli(work, 'claim_audit.py', ['audit-claims', '--input', manifest,
                                  '--output', incomplete], expected=2)
    require(load(incomplete)['status'] == 'incomplete', 'Missing declared review was accepted')


def checkpoint_check(work):
    """Require actual SQLite save/backup/restore without an engine or network."""
    run_cli(work, 'checkpoint_tools.py', ['--help'])
    store, backup = work / 'checkpoint-store', work / 'checkpoint-backup'
    run_cli(work, 'checkpoint_tools.py', ['init', '--store', store,
            '--max-store-bytes', '4194304', '--max-generations', '2',
            '--report', work / 'checkpoint-init.json'])
    source = work / 'checkpoint-empty.bin'
    source.write_bytes(b'')
    identity = '1' * 32
    manifest = {'schema_version': 1, 'checkpoint_id': identity,
                'application': {'format': 'installation-control', 'version': '1', 'resume_notes': ''},
                'files': [{'source': source.name, 'path': 'state/empty.bin', 'size': 0,
                           'sha256': hashlib.sha256(b'').hexdigest()}]}
    packet = work / 'checkpoint-manifest.json'
    write(packet, manifest)
    for report in ('checkpoint-save.json', 'checkpoint-save-retry.json'):
        run_cli(work, 'checkpoint_tools.py', ['save', '--store', store,
                '--manifest', packet, '--allow-save', '--report', work / report])
    run_cli(work, 'checkpoint_tools.py', ['backup', '--store', store,
            '--destination', backup, '--allow-copy', '--report', work / 'checkpoint-backup.json'])
    run_cli(work, 'checkpoint_tools.py', ['verify', '--store', backup,
            '--checkpoint-id', identity, '--report', work / 'checkpoint-verified.json'])
    restored = work / 'checkpoint-restored'
    run_cli(work, 'checkpoint_tools.py', ['restore', '--store', backup,
            '--checkpoint-id', identity, '--destination', restored, '--allow-restore',
            '--report', work / 'checkpoint-restored.json'])
    require((restored / 'state/empty.bin').read_bytes() == b'' and
            (restored / '.checkpoint.json').is_file(), 'Durable checkpoint restore differs')
    # Selected checkpoint checks also verify the offline backup restore-drill API.
    import importlib.util
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('installed_backup_control', ROOT / 'scripts/backup_tools.py')
    helper = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); spec.loader.exec_module(helper)
    drill = helper.drill_store(backup)
    require(drill.get('full_restore_drill') is True and drill.get('generations') == 1,
            'Offline backup restore drill failed')
    run_cli(work, 'backup_tools.py', ['--help'])
    source.write_bytes(b'changed')
    run_cli(work, 'checkpoint_tools.py', ['save', '--store', store,
            '--manifest', packet, '--allow-save', '--report', work / 'checkpoint-rejected.json'], expected=2)


def molecular_check(work):
    output = work / 'ethanol.json'
    run_cli(work, 'research_tools.py', ['rdkit-descriptors', '--smiles', 'CCO', '--output', output])
    descriptor = load(output)
    require(descriptor['formula'] == 'C2H6O' and abs(descriptor['descriptors']['MolWt'] - 46.069) < 0.001,
            'Ethanol descriptor control failed')
    sdf, conf = work / 'ethanol.sdf', work / 'conformer.json'
    run_cli(work, 'research_tools.py', ['rdkit-conformer', '--smiles', 'CCO', '--seed', '481516',
                                     '--sdf', sdf, '--output', conf])
    conformer = load(conf)
    require(conformer['converged'] and conformer['optimizer_status'] == 0 and
            hashlib.sha256(sdf.read_bytes()).hexdigest() == conformer['artifact']['sha256'], 'Conformer integrity failed')
    from rdkit import Chem
    mol = Chem.SDMolSupplier(str(sdf), removeHs=False)[0]
    require(mol is not None and mol.GetNumAtoms() == 9 and mol.GetConformer().Is3D() and
            Chem.MolToSmiles(Chem.RemoveHs(mol)) == 'CCO', 'Conformer is not 3D ethanol')
    energy = work / 'energy.json'
    run_cli(work, 'research_tools.py', ['openmm-smoke', '--output', energy])
    observed = load(energy)
    require(observed['platform'] == 'Reference' and observed['unit'] == 'kJ/mol' and
            abs(observed['energy'] - 0.5) < 1e-8, 'Analytic OpenMM control failed')
    for obj in (descriptor, observed):
        artifact = obj['measurement_artifact']
        require(hashlib.sha256(Path(artifact['path']).read_bytes()).hexdigest() == artifact['sha256'],
                'Molecular measurement hash mismatch')


def conversion_check(work, executable):
    source, output, report = work / 'conversion.smi', work / 'conversion.sdf', work / 'conversion.json'
    source.write_text('C[C@H](O)C(=O)O chiral_control\nF/C=C/F trans_control\n', encoding='utf-8')
    run_cli(work, 'conversion_tools.py', ['convert', '--executable', executable,
            '--input', source, '--input-format', 'smi', '--output', output,
            '--output-format', 'sdf', '--report', report])
    require(load(report)['passed'], 'Conversion identity control failed')
    from rdkit import Chem
    molecules = list(Chem.SDMolSupplier(str(output)))
    expected = [Chem.MolFromSmiles(s) for s in ('C[C@H](O)C(=O)O', 'F/C=C/F')]
    require(len(molecules) == 2 and all(m is not None for m in molecules) and
            [Chem.MolToSmiles(m, isomericSmiles=True) for m in molecules] ==
            [Chem.MolToSmiles(m, isomericSmiles=True) for m in expected],
            'Independent stereo conversion parse failed')


def protein_check(work, python, model_dir):
    source, report, artifact = work / 'protein.fa', work / 'protein.json', work / 'embeddings.json'
    source.write_text('>canonical_control\nACDEFGHIKLMNPQRSTVWY\n', encoding='utf-8')
    result = subprocess.run([python, str(ROOT / 'scripts' / 'protein_tools.py'), 'embed',
            '--input', str(source), '--model-dir', model_dir, '--device', 'cpu',
            '--output', str(report), '--embedding-output', str(artifact), '--timeout', '120'],
            cwd=work, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=180,
            env={**os.environ, 'PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE': '1', 'HF_HUB_OFFLINE': '1'})
    require(result.returncode == 0, 'Protein control failed: ' + result.stderr[-1000:])
    metadata, data = load(report), load(artifact)
    values = data['records'][0]['embedding']
    require(metadata['dimension'] == 320 and data['device'] == 'cpu' and len(values) == 320 and
            all(isinstance(v, (int, float)) and not isinstance(v, bool) and math.isfinite(v) for v in values),
            'Protein vector dimension/finite/device control failed')
    require(hashlib.sha256(artifact.read_bytes()).hexdigest() == metadata['artifact']['sha256'],
            'Protein output hash mismatch')


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--molecular', action='store_true', help='Require, execute and validate optional CPU controls')
    parser.add_argument('--checkpoints', action='store_true', help='Require actual local SQLite checkpoint controls')
    parser.add_argument('--obabel', help='Explicit Open Babel executable; require actual independently checked conversion')
    parser.add_argument('--protein-python', help='Separate protein runtime interpreter')
    parser.add_argument('--protein-model-dir', help='Verified local small ESM-2 assets; paired with --protein-python')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if bool(args.protein_python) != bool(args.protein_model_dir):
        parser.error('--protein-python and --protein-model-dir must be supplied together')
    checks = []
    try:
        package_check()
        checks.append({'name': 'package', 'status': 'passed'})
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='research-writer-check-') as directory:
            work = Path(directory).resolve()
            core_check(work)
            checks.append({'name': 'core', 'status': 'passed'})
            deeper_check(work)
            checks.append({'name': 'deeper_retrieval_and_claim_inventory', 'status': 'passed'})
            if args.checkpoints:
                checkpoint_check(work)
                checks.append({'name': 'durable_checkpoints', 'status': 'passed'})
            else:
                checks.append({'name': 'durable_checkpoints', 'status': 'skipped', 'reason': 'not requested'})
            if args.molecular:
                molecular_check(work)
                checks.append({'name': 'molecular', 'status': 'passed'})
            else:
                checks.append({'name': 'molecular', 'status': 'skipped', 'reason': 'not requested'})
            if args.obabel:
                conversion_check(work, args.obabel)
                checks.append({'name': 'conversion', 'status': 'passed'})
            else:
                checks.append({'name': 'conversion', 'status': 'skipped', 'reason': 'not requested'})
            if args.protein_python:
                protein_check(work, args.protein_python, args.protein_model_dir)
                checks.append({'name': 'protein', 'status': 'passed'})
            else:
                checks.append({'name': 'protein', 'status': 'skipped', 'reason': 'not requested'})
    except Exception as exc:
        print(json.dumps({'passed': False, 'checks': checks, 'error': f'{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}'}))
        return 1
    print(json.dumps({'passed': True, 'checks': checks, 'scope': 'offline installation/control checks; no scientific validation'}))
    return 0


if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

## Included resource: scripts/support_tools.py

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
"""Offline source-support review records. Integrity is not semantic entailment."""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from datetime import datetime
from decimal import Decimal
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path, PureWindowsPath
import re
import sys
import tempfile

MAX_BYTES = 10 * 1024 * 1024
ACCESS = ('metadata', 'abstract', 'full_text')
JUDGMENTS = ('supports', 'contradicts', 'uncertain')


def _pairs(pairs):
    result = {}
    for key, value in pairs:
        if key in result:
            raise ValueError('Duplicate JSON key: ' + key)
        result[key] = value
    return result


def _invalid_constant(value):
    raise ValueError('Nonfinite JSON number: ' + value)


def _bytes(path):
    with Path(path).open('rb') as stream:
        raw = stream.read(MAX_BYTES + 1)
    if len(raw) > MAX_BYTES:
        raise ValueError('Input exceeds 10 MiB')
    return raw


def read_json(path):
    return json.loads(_bytes(path).decode('utf-8'), object_pairs_hook=_pairs,
                      parse_float=Decimal, parse_constant=_invalid_constant)


def _write(path, value, force=False):
    target = Path(path)
    payload = json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, allow_nan=False) + '\n'
    if not force:
        with target.open('x', encoding='utf-8') as stream:
            stream.write(payload)
        return
    descriptor, temporary = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix='.support-record-', dir=target.parent)
    try:
        with os.fdopen(descriptor, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as stream:
            stream.write(payload)
        os.replace(temporary, target)
    finally:
        if os.path.exists(temporary):
            os.unlink(temporary)


def verify_support(manifest, base_directory='.'):
    """Check recorded source identity, exact passage and reviewer fields only.

    A valid judgment remains its reviewer's assertion. This function cannot
    determine whether the passage actually supports, contradicts or answers a claim.
    """
    errors, recorded = [], []
    result = {'status': 'fail', 'passed': False, 'record_integrity': 'checked',
              'semantic_entailment': 'not_automatically_verified',
              'scientific_validity': 'not_checked', 'errors': errors,
              'recorded_judgments': recorded,
              'scope': 'local source hash/version, exact text offsets and review-record completeness'}
    def error(code, record, detail):
        errors.append({'code': code, 'record': record, 'detail': detail})
    def string(record, field, label):
        value = record.get(field)
        if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
            error('missing_string', label, field + ' must be a nonempty string')
            return False
        return True
    def records(name):
        values = manifest.get(name, [])
        if not isinstance(values, list):
            error('invalid_records', name, 'Expected an array')
            return []
        indexed = {}
        for n, value in enumerate(values):
            label = name + '[' + str(n) + ']'
            if not isinstance(value, dict):
                error('invalid_record', label, 'Expected an object')
                continue
            if not string(value, 'id', label):
                continue
            if value['id'] in indexed:
                error('duplicate_id', label, value['id'])
                continue
            indexed[value['id']] = value
        return list(indexed.values())
    if not isinstance(manifest, dict):
        error('invalid_manifest', 'manifest', 'Expected an object')
        return result
    if type(manifest.get('schema_version')) is not int or manifest['schema_version'] != 1:
        error('invalid_schema', 'manifest', 'schema_version must be integer 1')
    sources = records('sources')
    reviews = records('reviews')
    root = Path(base_directory).resolve()
    verified = {}
    for source in sources:
        label = 'source:' + source['id']
        before = len(errors)
        for field in ('version', 'access', 'identity_status', 'path', 'sha256', 'locator'):
            string(source, field, label)
        if source.get('access') not in ACCESS:
            error('invalid_access', label, 'Expected metadata, abstract or full_text')
        if source.get('identity_status') not in ('verified', 'identity_verified'):
            error('unverified_identity', label, 'Source identity must be verified separately')
        expected = source.get('sha256')
        if not isinstance(expected, str) or not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-fA-F]{64}', expected):
            error('invalid_hash', label, 'Expected SHA-256 hex')
        name = source.get('path')
        try:
            if not isinstance(name, str) or not name or Path(name).is_absolute() or PureWindowsPath(name).drive or '..' in Path(name).parts or '..' in PureWindowsPath(name).parts:
                raise ValueError('Relative artifact path without traversal required')
            path = (root / name).resolve()
            if not path.is_relative_to(root):
                raise ValueError('Artifact resolves outside manifest directory')
            raw = _bytes(path)
            if not isinstance(expected, str) or hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest() != expected.lower():
                error('hash_mismatch', label, 'Source bytes differ from recorded SHA-256')
            text = raw.decode('utf-8')
            if not text.strip():
                error('empty_source', label, 'Source text is empty')
        except (OSError, UnicodeError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as exc:
            error('source_unreadable_or_unsafe', label, str(exc))
            continue
        if before == len(errors):
            verified[source['id']] = (source, text)
    for review in reviews:
        label = 'review:' + review['id']
        before = len(errors)
        for field in ('claim', 'source_id', 'source_version', 'source_sha256', 'passage',
                      'locator', 'judgment', 'rationale', 'reviewer', 'reviewed_at'):
            string(review, field, label)
        if review.get('judgment') not in JUDGMENTS:
            error('invalid_judgment', label, 'Expected supports, contradicts or uncertain')
        try:
            stamp = review.get('reviewed_at')
            if not isinstance(stamp, str):
                raise ValueError('Timestamp must be a string')
            observed = datetime.fromisoformat(stamp[:-1] + '+00:00' if stamp.endswith('Z') else stamp)
            if observed.tzinfo is None or observed.utcoffset() is None:
                raise ValueError('Timestamp requires timezone')
        except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
            error('invalid_review_time', label, str(exc))
        sid = review.get('source_id')
        source_data = verified.get(sid) if isinstance(sid, str) else None
        if source_data is None:
            error('unverified_source', label, 'Source ID is missing or its integrity failed')
            continue
        source, text = source_data
        if source['access'] == 'metadata':
            error('insufficient_access', label, 'Metadata is not an inspected abstract or full-text passage')
        if review.get('source_version') != source['version']:
            error('version_mismatch', label, 'Review version differs from recorded source')
        if not isinstance(review.get('source_sha256'), str) or review['source_sha256'].lower() != source['sha256'].lower():
            error('review_hash_mismatch', label, 'Review is not bound to the recorded source bytes')
        start, end = review.get('start'), review.get('end')
        if type(start) is not int or type(end) is not int or not 0 <= start < end <= len(text):
            error('invalid_offsets', label, 'Expected 0 <= start < end <= text length; booleans forbidden')
        elif review.get('passage') != text[start:end]:
            error('passage_mismatch', label, 'Passage does not equal source text at recorded offsets')
        if len(errors) == before:
            recorded.append({'id': review['id'], 'claim': review['claim'], 'source_id': sid,
                             'source_version': source['version'], 'source_sha256': source['sha256'],
                             'access': source['access'], 'judgment': review['judgment'],
                             'reviewer': review['reviewer'], 'reviewed_at': review['reviewed_at'],
                             'rationale': review['rationale'], 'locator': review['locator'],
                             'judgment_status': 'recorded_not_automatically_verified'})
    result['counts'] = {'sources': len(sources), 'reviews': len(reviews), 'valid_review_records': len(recorded)}
    if not reviews and not errors:
        result['status'] = 'not_checked'
        result['reason'] = 'no_reviews_to_check'
    elif not errors:
        result['status'], result['passed'] = 'pass', True
        result['review_status'] = 'review_records_valid'
    return result


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    commands = parser.add_subparsers(dest='command', required=True)
    review = commands.add_parser('review-support')
    review.add_argument('--input', required=True)
    review.add_argument('--output', required=True)
    review.add_argument('--force', action='store_true')
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        if Path(args.output).exists() and not args.force:
            raise FileExistsError('Output exists; use a new path or explicit --force')
        manifest = read_json(args.input)
        result = verify_support(manifest, Path(args.input).resolve().parent)
        _write(args.output, result, args.force)
        return 0 if result['passed'] else 2
    except (OSError, UnicodeError, ValueError, TypeError, RecursionError) as exc:
        print(json.dumps({'status': 'failed', 'passed': False, 'error': str(exc)}), file=sys.stderr)
        return 2


if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())
```
